<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离自由空间量子通信（特别是卫星链路）在白天因背景噪声而极具挑战，QKD在此场景下难以实用。QKPC具有更简单的系统要求、更强的抗噪声能力且无需密钥分发，因此探索QKPC在白天自由空间链路的可行性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用实验简化的装置，在90米屋顶链路上实现完整的QKPC协议，包括编码、同步、消息解码所有阶段。系统完全离线后处理实现，不依赖辅助经典同步信道。

Result: 成功在白天高噪声自由空间链路上演示了QKPC的完整实现，证明了其在无辅助同步信道条件下的可行性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: QKPC可作为QKD的互补方案，适用于QKD不实用的高噪声场景（如白天链路），对未来地面和空间量子通信系统具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) is the most established approach in quantum communication. However, long-distance free-space implementations, particularly satellite links, remain challenging, especially during the day due to daylight background noise. Quantum keyless private communication (QKPC) is a quantum communication protocol that enables information-theoretic security with simpler system requirements, improved robustness against noise, and without the need for secret key distribution. QKPC and QKD are complementary, with QKPC enabling free-space links where QKD is impractical, while QKD provides channel monitoring for applications that require eavesdropping detection. Here, we report a complete implementation of QKPC in a daylight free-space experiment over a 90 m rooftop link, using an experimentally simple setup. Our demonstration includes all stages of the protocol, from encoding and synchronization to message decoding, operates entirely without auxiliary classical synchronization channels, and is implemented offline through post-processing. This work demonstrates the feasibility of practical and scalable quantum communication over high-noise daylight links and highlights the potential of QKPC as a complementary solution to QKD for future ground-based and space-based communication systems.

</details>


### [99] [The arrival position problem in quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03538)
*Ali Ayatollah Rafsanjani,Will Cavendish*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子理论中"始终开启"探测器实验中的到达位置问题，比较了多种屏幕问题解决方案的定量预测，发现即使在远场极限下这些模型仍存在可区分的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子理论无法处理"始终开启"探测器实验中的到达位置预测问题，这是一个普遍存在的理论盲点，类似于更广为人知的到达时间问题。

Method: 比较多种屏幕问题解决方案（如Bohmian mechanics、quantum flux等）在到达位置分布上的定量预测，通过相对简单的实验设置进行对比分析。

Result: 不同模型在到达位置预测上存在可区分的差异，且许多差异在远场极限下仍然存在，而标准半经典近似通常被认为在该极限下有效。

Conclusion: 到达位置问题是一个可实验检验的量子理论基本问题，现有技术可以区分不同模型的预测，对量子理论的基础理解具有重要意义。

Abstract: The problem of making unambiguous probabilistic predictions about experiments involving waiting "always on" detectors remains a challenge for quantum theory. While most research on this problem studies arrival time, i.e., predicting the distribution of when detection events occur, this paper studies the arrival position problem, which is the complementary challenge of predicting the distribution of where detection events occur. Despite the widespread recognition of the arrival time problem, the inability of standard quantum theory to address the arrival position problem remains a pervasive theoretical blind spot. In this paper, we compare quantitative arrival position predictions derived from prominent proposed solutions to the screen problem. As we show, these models yield distinguishable predictions even in relatively simple experiments achievable with current technology. Notably, many of these discrepancies persist even in the far-field limit, where standard semiclassical approximations are typically assumed to be valid.

</details>


### [100] [A Pulsed Live-Cell Quantum Microscope for Entangled Solid State and Biological Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03552)
*Javier Noé Ramos-Silva,Sangjun Noh,Minghao Jiang,Parisa Aghaei,Ayla Hazrathosseini,Jocelyn Leon,Philip Hemmer,Peter J. Burke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种脉冲量子显微镜，能够在活细胞中同时操控生物量子比特（遗传编码、光学可寻址的量子系统）和固态自旋量子比特（如纳米金刚石中的NV色心），实现多路复用量子传感。该平台集成了纳秒门控光激发、三维衍射极限寻址、快速切换磁场、单光子定时和宽频微波控制（DC至5 GHz），并演示了在同一个活细胞中同时成像两种量子比特的能力，为最终实现固态与生物量子比特之间的纠缠铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物量子比特（如基因编码的荧光蛋白自由基对）和固态自旋量子比特（如NV色心）分别在活细胞量子传感领域取得了突破，但两者各自有局限性：单自旋探针灵敏度有限，而生物量子比特缺乏固态探针的相干操控能力。将两种技术结合到同一活细胞中，有望实现互补优势——固态量子比特提供高灵敏度和空间分辨率，生物量子比特提供基因靶向和生物相容性——但目前缺乏能同时操控两种量子比特的平台。

Method: 设计并构建了一台脉冲量子显微镜，关键技术包括：(1) 450 nm和520 nm纳秒门控光激发，分别激发固态量子比特和生物量子比特；(2) 振镜扫描和压电物镜聚焦实现三维衍射极限寻址；(3) 快速切换静磁场和射频磁场；(4) 皮秒精度的单光子定时；(5) 宽频微波控制（DC至5 GHz），覆盖固态自旋的2.87 GHz共振、生物量子比特中自由基对的500-800 MHz共振以及未来量子比特的GHz频段。

Result: 成功实现了在同一个活细胞中同时进行固态纳米颗粒量子比特和生物量子比特的成像，验证了该平台的多路复用量子传感能力。

Conclusion: 该脉冲量子显微镜为在活细胞中进行多路复用量子传感开辟了新途径，最终目标是实现固态量子比特与蛋白质宿主自旋量子比特之间的纠缠，这将代表量子生物学和量子传感领域的重大突破。

Abstract: Two revolutions in quantum sensing are converging on the same microscope stage. Biological qubits have emerged as genetically encoded, optically addressable quantum systems inside live cells. Solid state spin based qubits have been entangled and used as nanoscale correlator magnetometers, delivering sensitivity and spatial-resolution gains that single-spin probes cannot reach. Here we report a pulsed quantum microscope that enables simultaneous quantum state manipulation of both qubit technologies in live cells. The platform combines nanosecond-gated optical excitation at 450 nm and 520 nm, three-dimensional diffraction-limited addressing by galvo beam scanning and piezo objective focus, rapidly switched static and radio-frequency magnetic fields, single-photon timing with picosecond resolution, and microwave control for frequencies from DC to 5 GHz, including the 2.87 GHz resonance of solid state spins, the 500 MHz to 800 MHz resonance of radical pairs in biological qubits, and any future qubit resonance in the GHz range. Live cell imaging with simultaneous biological and solid state nanoparticle qubits in the same cell demonstrates the power of this technique for multiplexed quantum sensing. We anticipate this approach will open new opportunities for researchers to explore quantum sensing in live cells, and, ultimately, entanglement between a solid-state qubit and a protein-hosted spin qubit.

</details>


### [101] [Device-independent Quantum Key Distribution in the commuting operator framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03579)
*Gereon Koßmann,René Schwonnek,Po-Chieh Liu,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在交换子算子框架下对设备无关量子密钥分发（DIQKD）进行了严格分析，证明了测量可无损假设为投影测量，并将密钥率计算问题转化为非交换多项式优化（NPO）问题，推广了相对熵的积分表示到一般von Neumann代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DIQKD安全性证明技术依赖于张量积结构和隐藏有限维假设，这些假设不够普遍，需要在最一般的交换子算子框架下进行分析。

Method: 在交换子算子框架下开展严格证明；将相对熵的积分表示从量子推广到一般von Neumann代数；应用NPA层级松弛技术处理NPO问题。

Result: 严格证明了DIQKD中测量可无损假设为投影测量；证明了密钥率计算可归约为NPO问题，且NPA层级提供正确且收敛的松弛。

Conclusion: 该工作为DIQKD在交换子算子框架下的完整分析提供了所需工具，避免了张量积和有限维假设带来的微妙问题，是最为一般的量子实验安全性分析视角。

Abstract: Device-independent quantum key distribution (DIQKD) is arguably the gold standard for secure quantum communication, as it aims to rely only on observed input-output statistics of an uncharacterized device which is only assumption to obey the laws of quantum physics. A corresponding security analysis hence demands a description of a quantum experiment from a most general perspective. Under close inspection, existing proof techniques do not always meet this goal as they tend to rely on subtile assumptions on a tensor product structure of the underlying Hilbert space and a 'hidden but finite' dimensionality. In this work, we collect the tools needed for a full analysis of DIQKD in the commuting operator framework, which avoids these subtilities and provides the arguably most general view on a quantum experiment. We rigorously proof the common assumption that in DIQKD measurements can be w.l.o.g. assumed to be projective. Furthermore, we show that task of computing key rates can be casted as a non-commutative polynomial optimization (NPO) problem to which the Navascués-Pironio-Acín (NPA) hierarchy gives a correct and converging relaxation.
  As a tool, we generalize the integral representation for the relative entropy by Frenkel [Quantum 7, 1102 (2023)] to general von Neumann algebras and apply techniques from Kossmann and Schwonnek [arXiv: 2411.04858] for the approximation in an NPO program.

</details>


### [102] [Macroscopic Quantum Interference of the Center-of-Mass Motion of Levitated Superconducting Microparticles enabled by Magnetic Higher-Order Traps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03622)
*Jaume Cunill-Subiranas,Fabian Resare,Suocheng Zhao,Sofia Qvarfort,A. Metelmann,Carles Navau,Witlef Wieczorek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用静磁高阶多极陷阱产生超导微粒悬浮运动的宏观量子干涉，通过组合多极磁场构建非简谐陷阱势（如Duffing或双阱型），实现纳米尺度非线性效应，从而生成质心运动的非高斯量子态，并区分量子与经典行为。该研究为质量大于皮克量级的宏观物体进入量子力学区域开辟了道路，可用于量子-经典过渡、量子引力及暗物质探测等基础物理实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是实现宏观量子干涉，探索大质量物体（>皮克量级）的质心运动进入量子力学区域，以研究量子-经典过渡、量子引力以及暗物质探测等基础物理问题。

Method: 利用静磁高阶多极陷阱（磁光阱），通过组合多极磁场构建各种陷阱势（包括Duffing势和双阱势等非简谐势），实现纳米尺度（约零点点运动百倍量级）的非线性效应。通过静态线圈排列（电流可调）产生所需势场，并设计协议生成非高斯运动量子态，在相空间中求解质心运动动力学并分析参数依赖关系。

Result: 非简谐陷阱势实现约百倍零点点运动的非线性长度尺度（亚纳米量级）；可生成非高斯运动量子态；提出了通过位置测量在统计意义上区分量子与经典行为的方案。

Conclusion: 该方案为质量大于皮克量级（10^13原子质量单位）的宏观物体的质心运动进入量子力学区域提供了可行路径，可用于量子-经典过渡、量子引力交叉以及特定暗物质类型的探测等基础物理实验。

Abstract: We show how magnetostatic higher-order multipole traps can be used to generate macroscopic quantum interference of the motion of levitated superconducting microparticles. An appropriate combination of multipolar magnetic fields offers great versatility in constructing various trap potentials, including anharmonic trap potentials such as Duffing or double-well types. Crucially, the anharmonic trap potentials realize a nonlinearity on the order of hundred times the zero-point motion, i.e., on a length scale below nanometers. These anharmonic potentials allow for the generation of quantum features of the center-of-mass motion of a magnetically levitated superconducting microparticle. Importantly, they can be easily generated with a static arrangement of coils, requiring only that the current running through them is tunable. We propose protocols exploiting the versatility of the magnetic trap landscape to generate non-Gaussian motional states. We solve the dynamics of the center-of-mass motion of the particle in phase space and analyze its parameter dependence. Furthermore, we give a recipe to distinguish classical from quantum behavior in a statistically meaningful way through measurement of the position of the particle. Our results open a path to accessing the quantum regime of the center-of-mass motion of objects with masses larger than picogram, i.e., $10^{13}$ atomic mass units. This will enable fundamental physics experiments for studying the transition between quantum and classical behavior, exploring the intersection between quantum physics and gravity as well as probing of certain types of dark matter.

</details>


### [103] [From the Hong-Ou-Mandel Effect to Quantum Sensing: Interference of Nonclassical Light with Partial Distinguishability and Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03636)
*Matheus Eiji Ohno Bezerra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非经典光（Fock态和高斯态）在含噪线性干涉仪中的干涉现象，开发了基于生成函数的统一分析工具，发现了Fock态的新抑制定律，将高斯玻色采样扩展到部分可区分情形，并探讨了干涉效应与量子多参数估计的联系，发现测量不相容性即使在光子数很大时仍是基本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非经典光在噪声环境下的干涉行为，并建立其在量子信息和量子传感中的应用基础，特别是解决已有理论（如对称置换原理）未能涵盖的干涉结构和部分可区分性问题。

Method: 采用相空间形式体系，基于生成函数开发统一的分析工具；对于Fock态推导新的零概率事件（抑制定律）；对于高斯态，将基于Hafnian的高斯玻色采样描述扩展至包含部分可区分性（通过光子的内态重叠矩阵）；最后结合量子多参数估计框架分析相位与损耗的同时估计。

Result: 发现了超越对称置换原理的Fock态新抑制定律，揭示了光子可区分性退化下丰富的干涉结构；将高斯玻色采样理论推广到部分可区分情形；在量子多参数估计中，证明了非高斯优化态和某些双模高斯态可消除探针不相容性，但测量不相容性在高光子数极限下仍是根本性约束。

Conclusion: 含噪线性干涉仪中非经典光的干涉效应具有丰富结构，可通过生成函数统一描述；部分可区分性的引入对干涉效应有显著影响；在量子传感中，探针不相容性可被优化克服，但测量不相容性构成更基本的限制。

Abstract: This thesis explores the interference of nonclassical states of light, particularly Fock and Gaussian states, in noisy linear interferometers, with applications to quantum information and quantum sensing. Using the phase-space formalism, analytical tools based on generating functions are developed to describe quantum optical interference in a unified way. For multiphoton Fock states, new zero probability events (suppression laws) are identified beyond the previously derived symmetry permutation principle, revealing rich interference structures that is degraded with photon distinguishability. For Gaussian states, the Hafnian-based description of Gaussian Boson Sampling is extended to include partial distinguishability via the overlap matrix of the internal state of the photons. Finally, the link between these interference effects and quantum multiparameter estimation is examined for the simultaneous estimation of phase and loss. This study shows that while probe incompatibility can vanish for optimized non-Gaussian states and some two-mode Gaussian states, at high photon number, measurement incompatibility remains a fundamental constraint even in this limit.

</details>


### [104] [The influence of the transverse electric field on accelerating vortex state in the axisymmetric electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03747)
*Ziyang Ding,Ziqiang Huang,Qi Meng,Alexander J. Silenko,Pengming Zhang,Liping Zou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非均匀轴对称电场中传播的相对论性涡旋粒子束，通过相对论Foldy-Wouthuysen变换和近轴近似，推导了束宽、波前曲率和Gouy相位的耦合演化方程，并针对三种静电势构型进行了数值求解，揭示了横向场对束流演化的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立相对论性量子框架，用于利用静电场控制加速的涡旋粒子束，填补该领域相对论效应研究的空白。

Method: 从相对论FW变换后的稳态方程出发，采用近轴近似，推导束宽、波前曲率和Gouy相位的耦合演化方程，并对二次静电势、浸没透镜、单透镜三种情形进行数值求解。

Result: 数值求解得到了三种静电势构型下涡旋束的演化特性，证实了横向电场对束流演化具有本质性影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用静电场控制加速涡旋粒子束提供了相对论性量子理论框架。

Abstract: The relativistic vortex states of massive charged particles propagating in non-uniform axisymmetric electric field are studied. Starting from the stationary-state equation after the relativistic Foldy-Wouthuysen (FW) transformation and employing the paraxial approximation, the coupled evolution equations for the beam width, wavefront curvature, and Gouy phase are derived. The equations are solved numerically for a quadratic electrostatic potential, an immersion lens, and an einzel lens. The essential influence of the transverse field on beam evolution is demonstrated. The results provide a relativistic quantum framework for controlling accelerated vortex particle beams using electrostatic fields.

</details>


### [105] [Entanglement-Assisted Timing Optimization for Discriminating Amplitude-Damping Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03757)
*Massimiliano F. Sacchi,Milajiguli Rexiti,Stefano Mancini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了单次使用场景下两类相位协变振幅阻尼Lindblad过程的量子态最小误差区分问题，分别研究了无辅助探针和纠缠辅助探针的最优区分策略，并给出了闭式解与充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两种量子动力学过程（通道）的最小误差区分，特别是考虑相位协变振幅阻尼Lindbladian的情形，探索纠缠辅助能否带来优势。

Method: 通过优化输入探针（可能纠缠辅助粒子）和探测时间，推导无辅助探针的最优迹范数区分度的闭式表达式（含内部支与边界支）；利用相位协变性将纠缠辅助情形下的金刚范数优化约化至单参数Schmidt族。

Result: 无辅助情形下得到固定时间最优迹范数区分的闭式解；纠缠辅助情形给出固定时间纠缠优势的透明充分条件，并区分局部优势与全局时间优化后的优势。存在例子中无辅助策略需渐近达到最优，而纠缠探针在有限时间即可实现严格更小的错误概率。

Conclusion: 纠缠辅助探针在区分相位协变振幅阻尼动力学过程中能够提供有限时间的严格优势，且该优势优于无辅助策略的渐近极限。

Abstract: We analyze minimum-error discrimination of two qubit dynamical processes generated by phase-covariant amplitude-damping Lindbladians in a single-use scenario. The optimization involves both the input probe, possibly entangled with an isolated ancilla, and the interrogation time. For unassisted probes we obtain a closed expression for the optimal trace-norm distinguishability at fixed time, with distinct interior and boundary branches. For entanglement-assisted probes, the common phase covariance of the two channels reduces the diamond-norm optimization to a one-parameter Schmidt family. The resulting formula gives transparent sufficient conditions for fixed-time entanglement advantage and separates this local advantage from advantage after global optimization over time. We exhibit examples in which the best unassisted strategy is approached only asymptotically, whereas an entangled probe achieves a strictly smaller error probability at finite time.

</details>


### [106] [Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad equation in different bases: application to driven-dissipative two- and multilevel systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03779)
*O. A. Ilinskaya,O. V. Ivakhnenko,A. I. Ryzhov,O. Yu. Kitsenko,S. N. Shevchenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了驱动-耗散开放量子系统中基矢选择的恰当性问题，研究了在不同基矢下GKSL主方程的形式及其对量子比特和量子比特系统动力学的影响，指出恰当的基矢选择对理论描述和实验实现至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视驱动-耗散开放量子系统中基矢的恰当选择问题，因为基矢选择是开放量子系统理论描述中的基本问题，并且为在实际中实现期望的演化提供了可能。

Method: 通过在不同基矢下考虑GKSL方程，计算了量子比特和量子比特系统的动力学行为，分析了基矢选择对主方程形式及系统演化的影响。

Result: 发现恰当的基矢选择是开放量子系统理论描述中的基本问题，并提供了在实际中实现期望演化的机会。

Conclusion: 基矢的恰当选择对于驱动-耗散开放量子系统的理论描述和实践实现都具有重要意义。

Abstract: An open quantum system can be described by a master equation, of which one of the most popular is the Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) equation. We revisit description of driven-dissipative quantum systems focusing on the appropriate choice of the system's basis and the respective transformations. We consider the GKSL equation in different bases and calculate the dynamics for a qubit and for a qudit. An appropriate choice of the basis is a fundamental problem for theoretical consideration of open quantum systems and provides an opportunity to obtain the desired evolution in practice.

</details>


### [107] [Perturbed quantum billiards on the hyperbolic plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03786)
*Matic Orel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲平面上算术台球从算术体系向通用量子混沌（GOE）的交叉过程。通过引入小面积保持几何扰动，计算了长序列特征值和特征态。谱统计显示从Poisson-like算术行为到GOE的交叉，交叉尺度随扰动减小向高波数移动。使用Brody和广义伽马拟合描述最近邻间距分布，并用有效块GOE模型诊断弱扰动下的残差块结构。通过Poincaré-Husimi表示和熵基局域化度量补充了本征态分析，发现局域化波动的宽度在GOE区域后遵循幂律衰减σ(β) ~ k^{-η}，η ≈ 0.37–0.38。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双曲平面上算术台球从非混沌（算术）行为到通用量子混沌（GOE）的交叉机制，理解几何扰动如何影响量子混沌的涌现过程。

Method: 从算术三角形出发，引入小面积保持几何扰动；计算长序列特征值和特征态；使用Brody分布和广义伽马分布拟合最近邻间距分布；采用有效块GOE模型诊断弱扰动下的残差块结构；利用Poincaré-Husimi表示可视化本征态，并用熵基度量量化相空间局域化程度，结合Beta分布拟合和幂律分析。

Result: 谱统计呈现扰动依赖的Poisson-to-GOE交叉，交叉尺度随扰动减小向高波数移动；广义伽马拟合作为双参数诊断工具有效区分了小间距结构和尾部行为；弱扰动下有效块GOE模型揭示了残差的块状结构；局域化波动宽度在GOE区域后服从σ(β) ~ k^{-η}的幂律衰减，η ≈ 0.37–0.38。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了双曲量子台球中算术-to-GOE交叉的完整谱统计和本征态图像，揭示了交叉过程中谱统计与相空间局域化的协同演化规律，为理解量子混沌从算术体系到通用体系的转变提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: We study the emergence of generic quantum chaos from an arithmetic billiard in the hyperbolic plane. Starting from an arithmetic triangle, we introduce small area-preserving geometric perturbations and compute long consecutive sequences of eigenvalues and eigenstates. The spectral statistics show a perturbation-dependent crossover from Poisson-like arithmetic behaviour to the Gaussian orthogonal ensemble (GOE), with the crossover scale moving to higher wavenumbers as the perturbation decreases. To characterize the nearest-neighbour spacing distribution, we compare the data with Brody and generalized-gamma fits. The generalized-gamma form is used only as a two-parameter diagnostic, allowing the small- and intermediate-spacing structure to vary independently from the large-spacing tail, rather than as a universal crossover law. For the weakest perturbation, we also compare the data with an effective block-GOE model, which diagnoses residual block-like structure deep into the computed spectral range. We complement the spectral analysis with Poincaré-Husimi representations of the eigenstates and quantify phase-space localization using an entropy-based measure. Its distribution is well described by Beta distributions whose width decays as a power law in the wavenumber. Before the spectral crossover, the localization statistics retain memory of the near-arithmetic regime. After the GOE regime is reached, the localization-fluctuation widths collapse onto a common decay law, sigma(Beta) ~ k^{-eta}, with eta approximately 0.37--0.38. These results provide a combined spectral and eigenfunction-level picture of the arithmetic-to-GOE crossover in hyperbolic quantum billiards.

</details>


### [108] [A regularization method for quantum neural networks using data symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03805)
*Hiroshi Ohno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输入数据对称性的量子神经网络正则化方法，通过添加惩罚项鼓励模型对齐数据对称性，无需对称群先验知识，在分类和量子生成对抗网络任务中实现更快收敛和更低测试误差，并提供了基于Rademacher复杂度的泛化界理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用数据对称性来改进量子神经网络的泛化能力和训练效率，但现有方法通常需要对称群的先验知识，限制了其实用性。

Method: 提出一种基于输入数据对称性的正则化方法，通过在损失函数中引入惩罚项来鼓励模型与数据对称性对齐，该方法实现简单且不需要对称群的先验知识。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在分类任务和量子生成对抗网络上均实现了更快的收敛速度和更低的测试误差；同时利用Rademacher复杂度给出了理论泛化界。

Conclusion: 对称感知正则化在提升量子机器学习模型性能方面具有潜力，并推测满足对称性的模型通常具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Leveraging data symmetries has recently become a key strategy in quantum neural networks (QNNs) to improve generalization and training efficiency. In this study, we propose a novel regularization method for QNNs based on input data symmetry. By introducing a penalty term that encourages the model to align with data symmetry, our method enables improved training speed and generalization. This symmetry-based regularization is simple to implement and does not require prior knowledge of the symmetry group. We validate its effectiveness through numerical experiments on both classification tasks and quantum generative adversarial networks. Empirical results demonstrate faster convergence and lower test errors. Furthermore, we provide a theoretical generalization bound using Rademacher complexity and conjecture a condition under which models with symmetry exhibit better generalization. Our findings highlight the potential of symmetry-aware regularization in enhancing the performance of QML models.

</details>


### [109] [Matter-wave Induced Transparenc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03820)
*Tongkang wang,Yuqi Liu,Wenlan Chen,Zhendong Zhang,Jiazhong Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了物质波诱导透明现象，通过原子碰撞相互作用在损耗性分子势中产生透射窗口，利用铯玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚和调制诱导的Feshbach共振实现了灵活的三能级原子-分子耦合系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将电磁诱导透明（EIT）的概念从光学系统拓展到物质波领域，利用原子碰撞相互作用替代光与物质的耦合，探索新的干涉机制来控制原子散射过程。

Method: 使用铯玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，结合调制诱导的Feshbach共振构建三能级原子-分子耦合系统。通过暗态条件实现窄可调谐透明窗口，利用调制诱导耦合控制线宽，采用多频Floquet调制选择散射路径。

Result: 在宽损耗性碰撞共振中观测到窄且可调谐的透明窗口；透明窗口线宽可通过调制诱导耦合控制；散射路径可通过多频Floquet调制进行选择。

Conclusion: 物质波诱导透明为探索可编程非平衡和非厄米物理提供了基于干涉的新途径，可推动量子化学和精密测量领域的发展。

Abstract: Electromagnetically induced transparency suppresses optical absorption through destructive interference, playing a central role in light-matter interaction and quantum information science. We report matter-wave induced transparency, where atomic collisional interactions induce transmission through a lossy molecular potential for the incident atomic scattering waves. Using cesium Bose-Einstein condensates and modulation-induced Feshbach resonances, we realize a three-level atom-molecule coupled system with unprecedented flexibility. Under the dark state condition, a narrow and tunable transparency window appears within a broad dissipative collisional resonance. The transparency window linewidth is controlled by modulation-induced coupling. And scattering pathways are selectable via multifrequency Floquet modulation. These results establish an interference-based route for exploring programmable nonequilibrium and non-Hermitian physics, steering quantum chemistry and precision measurements.

</details>


### [110] [Algebra of quantum mechanics via classical phonons. I: The Schrodinger equation as the Newtonian equation of motion and quantum observables as classical averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03897)
*Emmanuel Giner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过经典Frenkel-Kontorova声子模型（一维耦合谐振子链）的连续极限，在非相对论极限下将Klein-Gordon方程形式化为薛定谔方程，并通过复变量变换将经典场观测量（能量、动量）映射为量子力学对应量。此外，引入摩擦力的经典模型可导出非厄米哈密顿量的薛定谔方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从经典物理（声子模型）出发，在不涉及量子化公设的前提下，形式化地重现量子力学的代数结构与复值波函数，探索经典与量子之间的深层联系。

Method: 从一维Frenkel-Kontorova模型（耦合谐振子链）的牛顿运动方程出发，取连续极限得到实值Klein-Gordon方程；引入混合位移场和速度场的复变量变换，并分离快慢时间尺度，在非相对论极限下将Klein-Gordon方程转化为薛定谔方程；同时将经典总能量、动量等观测量映射为量子力学期望值形式；最后引入摩擦力项，导出非厄米哈密顿量对应的薛定谔方程。

Result: 成功在经典声子模型框架下形式化地导出了薛定谔方程，实现了经典观测量到量子力学算符期望值的映射，并拓展至非厄米情形。

Conclusion: 经典动力学可以在非相对论极限下精确复现量子力学的代数结构和复值波函数形式，但该工作不涉及测量问题、量子化公设及相对论效应，后者将在系列论文的第二部分讨论。

Abstract: The Schrodinger equation for a single spinless particle is formally obtained via a classical phonon model, namely the Frenkel-Kontorova model. Starting from a one-dimensional lattice of coupled harmonic oscillators, we show that the continuous limit of the corresponding Newtonian equation of motion yields the Klein-Gordon equation for a real-valued field. By introducing a complex-valued change of variables mixing the real-valued displacement and velocity fields, and by separating fast and slow time scales, the Klein-Gordon equation is written as the Schrodinger equation within the non-relativistic limit. This complex change of variable also allows to rewrite classical global observables of the phonon field, such as the total energy or momentum, as the corresponding quantum observables. Additionally, we show that when a friction force is incorporated into the classical model, the corresponding Klein-Gordon equation can be rewritten as a Schrodinger equation with a non-Hermitian Hamiltonian. While the global approach is limited here to the non-relativistic regime and does not address the measurement problem, quantization or relativistic effects, it nonetheless illustrates how quantum algebra and complex-valued wave functions can be exactly reproduced using classical dynamics. The relativistic regime for a spinless particle and the link between commutators and Poisson brackets is addressed in the second part of this series.

</details>


### [111] [On estimating operator norm distance, with optimal trace distance estimation when one state is pure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03905)
*Yupan Liu,Qisheng Wang,Zhan Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态算子范数距离 $T_{\infty}(\rho_0,\rho_1)$ 的计算复杂度。当其中一个态为纯态时，给出查询复杂度为 $\Theta(1/\varepsilon)$ 的最优量子估计器；对一般量子态，给出 $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^{3/2})$ 的量子估计器，证明对应承诺问题是 BQP-完全的，并将下界改进为 $\Omega(1/\varepsilon)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究算子范数距离的计算复杂度，该距离定义为 $\|A\|_{\infty} = \sigma_{\max}(A)$，给定 poly(n) 大小的态制备电路。现有方法（如 van Apeldoorn 等人及 Wang 和 Zhang 的工作）的查询复杂度至少与态的秩线性相关，而秩可能高达 $\exp(n)$，因此需要秩无关的高效量子估计器。

Method: 1) 当 $\rho_0$ 为纯态时，利用纯态 $|\psi\rangle$ 与 $|\psi\rangle\!\langle\psi|-\rho$ 最大本征向量至少有 $1/2$ 重叠这一结构特性，设计最优量子估计器。2) 对一般量子态，给出 $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^{3/2})$ 查询复杂度的量子估计器，并证明对应的承诺问题是 BQP-完全的。

Result: 1) 纯态情形：最优量子估计器，查询复杂度 $\Theta(1/\varepsilon)$，常数误差时运行时间为 poly(n)。2) 一般态情形：量子估计器查询复杂度 $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^{3/2})$，将 Liu 和 Wang 的 QMA 上界改进为 BQP，下界为 $\Omega(1/\varepsilon)$，仅剩平方根差距。

Conclusion: 当其中一个态为纯态时，算子范数距离与迹距离（$T(|\psi\rangle\!\langle\psi|,\rho)$ 的一半）均可实现秩无关的高效量子估计；一般情形下承诺问题为 BQP-完全的，复杂度接近最优。

Abstract: We investigate the computational complexity of estimating the operator norm distance ${\rm T}_{\infty}(ρ_0,ρ_1)$, defined via the operator norm $\|A\|_{\infty} = σ_{\max}(A)$, given ${\rm poly}(n)$-size state-preparation circuits of $n$-qubit quantum states $ρ_0$ and $ρ_1$. We provide efficient quantum estimators for the operator norm distance whose complexity is independent of the rank (and thus the dimension) of the states:
  1. When one state is pure, we establish an optimal quantum estimator using $Θ(1/ε)$ queries to the state-preparation circuits. Consequently, for constant additive error, say $ε=1/5$, our estimator runs in ${\rm poly}(n)$ time. Since the operator norm distance ${\rm T}_{\infty}(|ψ\rangle\!\langleψ|,ρ)$ is exactly half of the trace distance ${\rm T}(|ψ\rangle\!\langleψ|,ρ)$, our result also gives rank-independent query complexity for estimating both quantities, whereas the approaches due to van Apeldoorn, Cornelissen, Gily{é}n, and Nannicini (SODA 2023) and Wang and Zhang (TIT 2024) have query complexity scaling at least linearly with ${\rm rank}(ρ)$, which can be $\exp(n)$ in general.
  2. For general quantum states, we also provide a quantum estimator using $\widetilde{O}(1/ε^{3/2})$ queries to the state-preparation circuits, which shows that the corresponding promise problem is ${\sf BQP}$-complete and improves the ${\sf QMA}$ upper bound sketched by Liu and Wang (ESA 2025). Together with an $Ω(1/ε)$ quantum query complexity lower bound, this leaves only square-root room for improvement.
  The key intuition behind our estimators is that, when one state is pure, the pure state $|ψ\rangle$ has overlap at least $1/2$ with the top unit eigenvector of $|ψ\rangle\!\langleψ|-ρ$, reflecting a structural feature specific to the operator norm distance.

</details>


### [112] [Algebra of quantum mechanics \textit{via} classical phonons. II: Klein-Gordon dynamics, the Heisenberg formalism, the Dirac canonical commutation rule and the Poincaré algebra through the continuous Poisson bracket formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03909)
*Emmanuel Giner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过经典声子模型的哈密顿形式，导出了单粒子无自旋相对论量子力学的特征，包括实值Klein-Gordon场的Ehrenfest关系、运动常数、Poisson括号与对易子的等价性，以及1+1维Poincaré群变换及其代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在第一部分工作中，作者已展示了如何从经典声子模型通过牛顿运动方程得到单粒子的薛定谔方程和非相对论量子可观测量。本文旨在进一步展示，对同一声子系统应用经典哈密顿形式，能够恢复单粒子无自旋相对论量子力学的特征。

Method: 利用声子模型的经典性质，定义经典可观测量之间的连续Poisson括号，恢复实值Klein-Gordon场的Ehrenfest关系；通过引入非局域复值变量变换，将实值Klein-Gordon方程改写为类似薛定谔方程的形式，并将经典可观测量转化为量子期望值；进而将经典Poisson括号重写为厄米算符的对易子，建立Heisenberg形式与经典Poisson括号形式的严格等价性；最后展示Poisson括号如何恢复1+1维Poincaré群变换及其代数。

Result: 成功从经典声子模型的哈密顿形式导出了相对论量子力学的关键特征：实值Klein-Gordon场的Ehrenfest关系、运动常数的一般形式（推广了Klein-Gordon场上的内积和动量概念）、Poisson括号与厄米算符对易子的等价关系，以及1+1维Poincaré群变换和代数，并建立了Lorentz不变内积与粒子质量Casimir不变量之间的联系。

Conclusion: 经典声子模型的哈密顿形式能够系统导出单粒子无自旋相对论量子力学的基本结构，揭示了经典Poisson括号形式与量子Heisenberg形式之间的严格等价性，为量子力学的经典起源提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In the first part of this series we have shown how the Schrodinger equation for a single particle and the corresponding non relativistic quantum observables can be obtained from a purely classical phonon model through the Newtonian equations of motion. In this work we focus instead on how the classical Hamiltonian formalism applied to the same phonon system allows to recover the feature of relativistic quantum mechanics for a single spinless particle. Using the classical nature of the phonon model, we naturally define continuous Poisson brackets between classical observables, which allows to recover the dynamics of such observables, i.e. the Ehrenfest relations associated to real-valued Klein-Gordon fields. The Poisson brackets also permits to obtain the generic form of constants of motions, thus generalizing the concept of inner products and momentum on Klein-Gordon fields. We then connect the formalism of real-valued classical functionals with that of hermitian operators and complex-valued wave functions. This is done through the introduction of a non-local complex-valued change of variables which allows to rewrite the real-valued Klein-Gordon equation in a form akin to the Schrodinger equation, and the classical observables as quantum expectation values. Then, we show how this change of variables allows to rewrite the classical Poisson brackets as commutators of hermitian operators. This points out the strict equivalence between the Heisenberg formalism and the formalism of classical Poisson bracket. Eventually, we illustrate how the Poisson brackets allows to recover the transformations of Poincaré group in 1+1 dimension together with its algebra. The latter makes the link between the Lorentz invariant inner product of Mostafazadeh and the Casimir invariant associated to the mass of particle.

</details>


### [113] [A Semantic Framework for Reproducible Variational Quantum Algorithm Execution Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03982)
*Silvie Illésová,Martin Beseda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于本体的框架，将变分量子算法（VQA）执行记录表示为结构化、机器可读的软件工程制品，通过OWL本体建模实验实体，结合SHACL约束验证完整性和一致性，以及SPARQL查询支持可重现性信息检索，并在VQE实验上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VQA执行信息散落在代码、配置、日志、后端元数据和论文描述中，导致实验难以重现、比较、调试和复用，亟需一种结构化的表示方法。

Method: 构建OWL本体以建模VQA实验中的算法、电路、ansatz、哈密顿量、优化器、后端、噪声模型、缓解技术、执行步骤、软件环境、测量结果等实体；结合SHACL约束进行完整性和一致性验证；使用SPARQL查询实现可重现性信息的检索。

Result: 在VQE执行记录上验证，框架能够表示完整的执行上下文、检测缺失或格式错误的元数据，并支持基于查询的可重现信息检查。

Conclusion: 该 ontology-supported 框架能有效提升VQA实验的可重现性、可比较性和可复用性，为量子软件工程提供结构化支持。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms are hybrid quantum-classical workflows whose results depend on many interacting choices, including the ansatz, Hamiltonian, optimizer, backend, shot count, noise model, mitigation method, random seed, stopping criteria, and software versions. In current practice, this information is often scattered across code, configuration files, logs, backend metadata, and paper descriptions, making executions difficult to reproduce, compare, debug, and reuse. This paper proposes an ontology-supported framework for representing Variational Quantum Algorithm (VQA) execution records as structured and machine-readable software engineering artifacts. The framework defines a Web Ontology Language (OWL) ontology for modeling the main entities involved in VQA experimentation, including algorithms, circuits, ansatzes, Hamiltonians, optimizers, backends, noise models, mitigation techniques, execution steps, software environments, measurement outcomes, and results. It further combines the ontology with Shapes Constraint Language (SHACL) constraints for validating completeness and consistency, and SPARQL Protocol and RDF Query Language (SPARQL) competency queries for retrieving reproducibility-relevant information. We demonstrate the approach using Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) execution records, including a valid record and intentionally incomplete or inconsistent examples. The results show that the framework can represent complete VQA execution contexts, detect missing or malformed metadata, and support query-based inspection of information needed for reproducible quantum software experimentation.

</details>


### [114] [Verification of Quantum Computations: Hardware-Efficient Security Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03983)
*Harold Ollivier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了统计安全下的量子验证协议，提出了一个由远程态制备、陷阱偏差检测和纠错嵌入三原语组成的模块化框架，并系统论述了如何降低硬件需求、扩展至多方设置及容错计算委托。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限量子资源用户如何以信息论安全方式验证不可信全量子服务器输出结果的核心问题，推动量子验证从理论走向硬件高效实现。

Method: 提出模块化组合框架，将验证分解为三个独立原语（远程态制备、陷阱偏差检测、纠错嵌入）；系统梳理了消除空间开销、去除计算基伪态、用可信局部旋转或弱相干脉冲替代单光子源等硬件简化方案。

Result: 展示了早期协议的高硬件需求如何被系统性放松；分析了技术在不对称多方设置和门级噪声下全容错计算委托中的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文旨在作为实用量子验证架构原理的指南，揭示了各协议间的结构联系及迈向硬件高效实现的路径。

Abstract: How can a user with limited quantum resources verify the output of an untrusted, fully quantum server? This manuscript provides a conceptual synthesis of some recent developments toward answering this question under statistical (information-theoretic) security. Rather than duplicating the dense technical proofs of the underlying publications, our focus here is on the physical motivations, the structural connections between different protocols, and the path toward hardware-efficient implementation.
  We begin by introducing a modular, composable framework that partitions verification into three distinct, independent primitives: remote state preparation, trap-based deviation detection, and error-correcting embedding. Using this framework, we show how the demanding hardware requirements of early protocols can be systematically relaxed. We review schemes that eliminate the spatial overhead, remove the need to prepare computational-basis dummy states, and replace single-photon sources with trusted local rotations or weak coherent pulses. Finally, we examine how these techniques scale, both to asymmetric multi-party settings and to the delegation of fully fault-tolerant computations in the presence of gate-level noise. This document is intended as a guide to the architectural principles of practical quantum verification.

</details>


### [115] [Stellar Braid Monodromy of Finite-Rank Non-Gaussian Photonic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04007)
*Arnaud Coatanhay,Angelique Dremeau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对有限秩非高斯玻色子态引入了一种拓扑精炼——辫子单值（braid monodromy），作为对恒星秩的补充。该方法通过追踪正则参数化族中恒星除子零点的运动轨迹，在有限Fock图下将其表示为复平面上无序配置空间的辫子群不变量，从而为实验相关态族（如三次子空间、三项式切片）提供了后层析成像的非高斯性诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非高斯性诊断工具（如恒星秩、Wigner负性等）是标量型不变量，无法捕获参数化态族内部的拓扑结构。本文旨在引入一种拓扑不变量——辫子单值，用于刻画正则参数化族中恒星除子零点在变形下的运动模式，从而提供更精细的非高斯性刻画。

Method: (1) 将正则有限秩恒星态映射为有限Fock图下的首一多项式，其简单零点对应恒星除子；(2) 正则分层与复平面无序配置空间双全纯同构，其基本群为Artin辫子群；(3) 将辫子单值定义为正则有限秩恒星态空间中环路的本质不变量；(4) 扩展至可允许有限恒星除子形式 E_{τ,μ}(z)P(z)，其中高斯参数构成可缩纤维；(5) 在三次子空间（前四个Fock态张成）和三项式切片中具体计算判别式和局部半扭结。

Result: 建立了辫子单值作为有限秩非高斯玻色子态正则族的拓扑不变量；证明了高斯参数空间是可缩的，因此拓扑信息完全由配置空间底空间决定；在三次子空间和三项式切片等实验相关族中给出了显式判别式和局部半扭结结构。

Conclusion: 辫子单值是后层析成像（post-tomographic）不变量，适用于制备环路和参数化族，与Wigner负性、恒星秩、近似恒星秩等标量诊断工具互补，为量子光学的非高斯态提供了新的拓扑分类视角。

Abstract: Finite-rank non-Gaussian bosonic states admit a holomorphic description in the Bargmann representation: after a zero-free Gaussian factor is separated off, their non-Gaussian structure is encoded by a finite stellar divisor. This article introduces a topological refinement of stellar rank for regular parameterized families of such states. Rather than only counting the zeros of the stellar divisor, we follow their motion under deformations of the state and record the associated braid monodromy. In the finite-Fock chart, a regular degree-r stellar state is represented by a monic polynomial with r simple zeros. The regular stratum is biholomorphic to the unordered configuration space of r points in the complex plane, and its fundamental group is the Artin braid group on r strands. Thus braid monodromy is an intrinsic invariant of loops in the regular finite-rank stellar state space. We then extend the construction to admissible finite stellar divisors of the form E_tau,mu(z) P(z); the zero-free Gaussian parameters form a contractible fiber over the same configuration-space base. Experimentally motivated finite-Fock families, especially the cubic subspace spanned by the first four Fock states, provide concrete laboratories, while trinomial slices yield explicit discriminants and local half-twists. The resulting invariant is post-tomographic and applies to preparation loops and parameterized families; it complements Wigner negativity, stellar rank, approximate stellar rank, and other scalar diagnostics of non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [116] [The Delayed Stabilizer ZX-Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04015)
*Cole Comfort,Giovanni de Felice*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出延迟稳定子ZX演算，一种用于描述无限平移不变稳定子量子纠错码的有限图语言，通过引入延迟生成元，并赋予两种语义（序列等价语义和生成函数语义），建立了完整的公理化体系，证明了其完备性和规范性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稳定子ZX图只能描述有限截断的量子纠错码，无法刻画晶格码、平移不变图态和量子卷积码等系统中重复的无限平移不变结构，需要一种有限的图语言来捕捉这些无限系统的本质。

Method: 在奇素数维稳定子ZX演算中引入单个新生成元——延迟（delay），实现数据在时间步之间的传递；赋予两种语义：序列等价语义（将行为视为量子信道序列的等价类）和生成函数语义（将延迟视为形式变量，用生成函数编码平移不变的Pauli算子族）；通过推广的Euler分解和颜色变换规则，以及推广的局部互补和旋转操作，将每个图归约为唯一规范形。

Result: 证明了延迟ZX图的唯一确定无限稳定子群；建立了生成函数表（tableau）表示，实现了从表到无限稳定子群的恢复；给出了完整的公理化，建立了规范形，证明了生成函数表语义下的可靠性、普适性和完备性。

Conclusion: 延迟稳定子ZX演算为无限平移不变量子纠错系统提供了一个紧凑、完整且可操作的图语言框架，为研究量子卷积码和晶格码等结构提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: Many stabilizer quantum error-correcting codes are built from a finite pattern repeated across space or time, such as lattice codes, translation-invariant graph states, and quantum convolutional codes. Ordinary stabilizer ZX-diagrams capture only finite truncations of such systems, obscuring the repeated structure that defines them. We introduce the delayed stabilizer ZX-calculus, a finite graphical language for these infinite, translation-invariant processes. It extends the odd-prime-dimensional stabilizer ZX-calculus with a single new generator, the delay, which feeds data from one time step to the next. We equip the calculus with two semantics. In the first semantics, we interpret the behaviour of a delayed ZX-diagram as an equivalence class of sequences of quantum channels; where two sequences are identified if they have the same information content. We show that the behaviour of a delayed ZX-diagram uniquely determines an infinite stabilizer group. In the second semantics, we interpret the delay as a formal variable, encoding the translation-invariant families of Pauli operators as generating functions. This allows us to represent a delayed ZX-diagram in terms of a tableau of generating functions, from which the infinite stabilizer group can be recovered. Finally, we give a complete axiomatization of the delayed stabilizer ZX-calculus, featuring generalised Euler decomposition and colour change rules. Using generalised forms of local complementation and pivoting, we reduce every diagram to a unique normal form. This establishes soundness, universality, and completeness for the generating tableau semantics.

</details>


### [117] [Detection methods for optimal target reflectivity estimation with two-mode squeezed vacuum probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04021)
*Harel Radia,Tuvia Gefen,Nadav Katz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了双模压缩真空探针在微波平台测量限制下的目标反射率估计精度极限，发现最优测量结构随反射率阈值转变，并证明在强噪声下适当的非局域高斯测量可接近量子Cramér-Rao界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双模压缩真空探针在理论上相比经典方案具有目标反射率估计的优势，但在微波平台的实际测量约束下实现这一潜力仍是核心挑战，因此需要研究实际限制下的精度极限。

Method: 通过理论分析表征不同能量和损耗区间下的最优测量，识别反射率阈值导致的最优接收机结构转变（参量放大器 vs. 双模压缩生成器）；随后研究高斯测量的性能表现。

Result: 发现反射率高于某阈值时参量放大器接收机最优，低于阈值时双模压缩生成器最优；标准局域零差探测下TMSV探针高度非最优，但适当的非局域高斯测量在强噪声下可接近量子Cramér-Rao界。

Conclusion: 即使在高斯测量限制下，近最优的量子目标反射率估计在多种相关噪声区间内是可实现的。

Abstract: Target reflectivity estimation using a two-mode squeezed vacuum (TMSV) probe offers a theoretical advantage over classical schemes, but realizing this potential under the measurement constraints of microwave platforms remains a central challenge. In this work, we study the precision limits for target reflectivity estimation across different energy and loss regimes, while accounting for realistic measurement restrictions. We characterize the optimal measurements and identify a transition in their structure: above a specific reflectivity threshold, a parametric amplifier receiver is optimal, whereas below it, the optimal observables are two-mode squeezing generators. We then study the performance of Gaussian measurements. When restricted to standard local homodyne detection, the TMSV probe is highly non-optimal. However, we show that suitable non-local Gaussian measurements can closely approach the quantum Cramér-Rao bound at the large noise limit. These results demonstrate that near-optimal quantum target reflectivity estimation is achievable in various relevant noisy regimes, even under the restriction of Gaussian measurements.

</details>


### [118] [Robust self-test of the maximally entangled state of two-qubits without assuming unitary observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04035)
*Alexandre C. Orthey,Magdalena Stobińska-Moretto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需Naimark dilation的设备无关自测试方法，直接处理非投影测量算子，以singlet态和Pauli可观测量为例，给出了基于纯态假设的O(√ε)鲁棒性界，表明实际设备无关认证比标准投影模型更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准设备无关自测试依赖Naimark dilation假设投影测量，掩盖了实际非酉可观测量在操作上的局限性。本文旨在消除这种理想化假设，建立更贴近真实物理实现的自测试框架。

Method: 假设不可信源产生纯态，对物理上的非投影算子进行正则化处理，推导出解析的O(√ε)鲁棒性界，完全避免对测量装置的dilation操作。

Result: 获得了针对singlet态和Pauli可观测量鲁棒的纯自测试方案，鲁棒性界为O(√ε)，且不依赖Naimark dilation。

Conclusion: 实际设备无关认证比标准投影模型所暗示的要求更严格，论文揭示了理想化假设与现实实现之间的差距。

Abstract: Standard device-independent self-testing uses Naimark dilation to assume projective measurements, masking the operational limitations of realistic non-unitary observables. We establish a robust pure self-test for the singlet and Pauli observables that entirely circumvents dilation of the measurement apparatus. Assuming a pure state to model an untrusted source, we regularize the physical non-projective operators and derive an analytic $\mathcal{O}(\sqrtε)$ robustness bound. Our results suggest that device-independent certification of real implementations is significantly more demanding than standard projective models imply.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum simulation of gauge theories on dynamical spacetimes via Floquet-induced matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04040)
*Samuel Buckley-Bonanno,Noah Eckstein,Susanne F. Yelin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于Floquet框架的量子模拟方法，通过大型N矩阵模型模拟杨-米尔斯规范场论，避免了传统空间格点对固定背景几何的依赖，支持弯曲和动态时空的量子模拟，并在数值上验证了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统格点规范理论的量子模拟依赖于空间格点，这需要固定的背景几何，难以处理弯曲时空和动态时空。大型N矩阵模型提供了另一种途径，将时空几何和规范场编码在厄米矩阵的对易结构中，但缺乏直接适用于可编程量子平台的框架。

Method: 引入Floquet框架，证明在耦合主导阶下，杨-米尔斯矩阵模型的欧几里得路径积分权重可由Haar随机态在周期矩阵算符序列下的系综平均保真度重现。利用Loschmidt回波通过标准随机基准测试协议访问可观测量，所需量子比特数指数级少于正则量子化方法。

Result: 数值验证了保真度-权重对应关系，展示了并行化量子电路采样路径积分测度的能力，并识别了平坦时空和膨胀宇宙背景下SU(2)规范场的去禁闭相变。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了固定时空格点，保留了连续对称性并在动态几何上保持幺正性，为超越传统格点方法的场和时空动力学量子模拟开辟了道路。

Abstract: Quantum simulations of gauge theories are typically built on spatial lattices, an approach that has enabled major progress at the cost of requiring fixed background geometries and obscuring the treatment of curved and dynamical spacetimes. Large-$N$ matrix models offer an alternative, encoding spacetime geometry and gauge fields in the commutation structure of a set of Hermitian matrices, with the classical continuum emerging smoothly at large matrix dimensions. Here we introduce a Floquet framework that makes these models directly accessible to programmable quantum platforms. We show that Euclidean path integral weights of a Yang-Mills matrix models are reproduced, at leading order in the coupling, by the ensemble-averaged fidelities of Haar-random states evolved under periodic sequences of matrix operators. The observables for the simulated matrix model can then be accessed through established randomized benchmarking protocols in terms of the Loschmidt echo. The encoding requires exponentially fewer qubits than canonically quantized approaches. Numerically, we validate the fidelity-weight correspondence, demonstrate parallelized quantum circuits that sample the path-integral measure, and identify the deconfinement transition of an $SU(2)$ gauge field on both flat and expanding cosmological backgrounds. By avoiding a fixed spacetime lattice, the framework preserves continuous symmetries and unitarity on dynamical geometries, opening quantum simulation to field and spacetime dynamics beyond the reach of conventional lattice methods.

</details>


### [120] [A Lie-Jordan Geometric Formulation of Lindblad Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04052)
*Leonel Bixano,Victor Alberto Cruz-Barriguete,Guillermo López-Alvarez,V. G. Ibarra-Sierra,José Luis Cardoso,Alejandro Kunold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了有限维开放量子动力学的Lie-Jordan几何形式化，基于先前工作的代数框架。在Hilbert-Schmidt算符空间中引入正交厄米基，利用结构张量编码对易子和反对易子，识别出基无关的通用三线性耗散映射，该映射将通用代数结构与模型依赖的物理信息分离。进一步展示了(B+C)和(B-C)组合生成内部左右输运，并推导了迹保持、厄米性保持等性质，最后以量子比特的纯退相和振幅阻尼通道为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为开放量子动力学提供一种Lie-Jordan几何形式化框架，将通用代数结构与模型依赖的物理信息明确分离，从而获得超算符结构的张量化和仿射几何解释。

Method: 在Hilbert-Schmidt算符空间中构建正交厄米基，用结构张量C和B分别编码对易子和反对易子；识别基无关的通用三线性耗散映射D(X,Y)Z；利用(B+C)和(B-C)组合生成内部左右输运；将物理耗散子表示为通用张量与Lindblad算符展开系数及Kossakowski矩阵的缩并。

Result: 建立了通用三线性耗散映射D(X,Y)Z = XZY - {YX,Z}/2，其分量仅依赖于Lie-Jordan结构张量；证明了(B+C)和(B-C)生成内部左右输运；推导了迹保持、厄米性保持、以及Hilbert-Schmidt伴随之恒等式；在量子比特的纯退相和振幅阻尼通道中验证了该形式化。

Conclusion: 该Lie-Jordan几何形式化成功将开放量子动力学的超算符结构分解为通用代数部分和模型依赖部分，为理解耗散过程的几何结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: We develop a Lie-Jordan geometric formulation of finite-dimensional open quantum dynamics, building on the algebraic framework introduced in our previous work (arXiv:2606.26477). The Hilbert-Schmidt operator space is endowed with an orthonormal Hermitian basis, in which the commutator and anticommutator are encoded by the structure tensors \(C_{μν}{}^λ\) and \(B_{μν}{}^λ\). Within this formulation, the von Neumann and Gorini-Kossakowski-Lindblad-Sudarshan equations admit a direct component representation. Our central result is the identification of a basis-independent universal trilinear dissipative map, \( \mathcal D(X,Y)Z=XZY-\frac12\{YX,Z\}, \) whose components define a universal operator-space tensor depending only on the Lie-Jordan structure tensors. The physical dissipator is obtained by contracting this tensor with the expansion coefficients of the Lindblad operators and the Kossakowski matrix, thereby separating the universal algebraic structure from the model-dependent physical information. We further show that the combinations \((B+C)\) and \((B-C)\) generate internal left and right transports, allowing the elementary dissipative map to be expressed as a left-right bimodule action corrected by an ordered Jordan contribution. The universal map satisfies basis-independent trace and Hermitian-conjugation identities, from which trace preservation, Hermiticity preservation of the complete dissipator, and the reality of its component representation in a Hermitian Hilbert-Schmidt basis follow. We also derive its Hilbert-Schmidt adjoint and illustrate the formalism for a qubit with pure dephasing and amplitude-damping channels. This construction provides a tensorial and affine-geometric interpretation of the universal superoperator structure derived in arXiv:2606.26477 while keeping the algebraic and model-dependent sectors explicitly separated.

</details>


### [121] [Frozen-Tree Sampling Refutes Quantum Advantage of Random Circuit Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04054)
*Sangchul Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出经典冻树采样算法，证明Haar随机量子态比特串采样可高效经典实现，挑战了随机电路采样作为量子优势基准的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑随机电路采样作为量子优势基准的可靠性，证明其经典可模拟性。

Method: 利用Haar随机量子态的条件尺度不变性，提出冻树采样算法，每样本O(n)时间从Dirichlet分布中采样。

Result: 冻树采样器输出概率与随机量子电路输出概率在统计上不可区分，均服从同一Dirichlet分布。

Conclusion: 随机电路采样的量子优势不成立，其困难在于识别具体电路实现而非从Dirichlet分布中采样。

Abstract: Random circuit sampling of bitstrings from a Haar-random quantum state is widely believed to be classically intractable, and has therefore been implemented as a primary benchmark for demonstrating quantum advantage. Here, we challenge this premise by proposing an efficient classical frozen-tree sampling algorithm that exploits the conditional scale invariance of Haar-random quantum states [Oh, arXiv:2602.19448]. The frozen-tree sampler draws bitstrings of $n$ qubits in $O(n)$ time per sample. Moreover, its output probability $p_F(x)$ is statistically identical to the probability $p_C(x)$ of a random quantum circuit, since both are independent instances of the same Dirichlet distribution. Consequently, no statistical test acting on samples alone can distinguish the classical frozen-tree sampler from a quantum random circuit. The claimed quantum advantage of random circuit sampling therefore does not withstand scrutiny: its hardness lies not in sampling from the Dirichlet distribution, which is classically efficient, but in identifying a specific circuit realization.

</details>


### [122] [Coherent quantum control of dark excitons in hybrid metal organic chalchogenolates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04080)
*Christian L. McCoy,Tobias Saule,Mariya Aleksich,Maggie C. Willson,J. Nathan Hohman,Thomas Weinacht,George N. Gibson,Carlos A. Trallero-Herrero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在室温下利用超快脉冲整形器对金属有机硫属化物（MOChas）中暗激子态进行相干控制，通过双光子吸收实现光学访问，并用四波混频过程直接读取。基于非微扰双光子哈密顿量描述该系统，为基于三能级系统和双光子跃迁的量子处理平台奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗激子具有高温下最长寿命之一，是下一代量子处理的 promising 候选体系，但如何实现对其的相干控制是核心挑战。

Method: 使用超快脉冲整形器在室温下实现相干控制；通过双光子吸收光学访问暗激子态；利用四波混频过程直接读取；采用基于原子物理的非微扰双光子哈密顿量描述三能级系统（两个暗激子）。

Result: 通过简单光学脉冲形状展示了经验性和理论性的态特异性光学访问；开发了基于哈密顿量的描述；给出了门操作的简单条件。

Conclusion: 该哈密顿量描述是迈向基于三能级系统和双光子跃迁（如MOCha银苯硒酚盐mithrene中的暗激子）的量子处理平台的第一步。

Abstract: Artificial atom-like systems are a promising candidate for next generation quantum processing. Among them, dark excitons exhibit one of the longest lifetimes at high temperatures. Here, we demonstrate coherent control of dark excitonic states in metal-organic chalcogenolates (MOChas) by using an ultrafast pulse shaper at room temperature. These dark exciton states are optically accessed via two-photon absorption and directly read out with a four-wave mixing process. The system is described by a non-perturbative, two-photon Hamiltonian based on well-known atomic physics and applied to a three level system comprised of two dark excitons. Empirical and theoretical state specific optical access is shown via a simple optical pulse shape. The developed Hamiltonian-based description is a first step towards a quantum processing platform using three-level systems and two photon transitions, one example being dark excitons in the MOCha silver benzeneselenolate (mithrene). Simple conditions for gate operations are laid out and described.

</details>


### [123] [A Cross-Platform Analysis of High-Performance Quantum Error Correction Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04082)
*Bryan Pan,Yufeng Xin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的分析框架，用于估计先进量子纠错码在不同硬件平台（包括分布式量子计算系统）上的逻辑错误率。该框架通过分析代码结构和两量子比特门开销两个主要因素，能够快速估计逻辑错误率，识别不同硬件平台上的主导因素（如电路体积、路由开销、跨QPU操作或非对称噪声保护），并可用于寻找分布式量子纠错的最优设计区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错理论虽已建立数十年，但当前量子计算平台在噪声水平和可用物理量子比特数量方面存在严重限制，阻碍了可扩展量子计算系统的发展。需要一个简单但统一的框架来快速估计逻辑错误率并识别不同硬件平台上的主导因素。

Method: 提出了一个统一的分析框架，通过捕获两个主要贡献因素——代码结构和两量子比特门开销，来估计逻辑错误率。该框架考虑了电路体积、路由开销、跨QPU操作和非对称噪声保护等因素。

Result: 该框架能够复现和解释大规模模拟中观察到的若干定性趋势，并能用于寻找分布式量子纠错的最优设计区域。

Conclusion: 该分析框架为分布式量子计算系统的设计提供了关键指导，尤其适用于寻找分布式QEC的甜点设计区域，有助于推动可扩展量子计算系统的发展。

Abstract: The theory of quantum error correction was established decades ago. Yet the limitation of the quantum computing platforms in terms of noise level and available physical qubit count persists, which greatly hinders the development of scalable quantum computing systems. In this paper, we present analytical estimates of logical error rates of advanced QEC codes across leading hardware platforms and distributed quantum computing systems using a simple but unified framework. The analysis captures two dominant contributors to logical error: code structure and two-qubit gate overhead. The framework provides a fast estimate of logical error rates and identification of dominating factors in different hardware platforms, such as circuit volume, routing overhead, inter-QPU operations, or asymmetric noise protection. We show that several qualitative trends observed in larger-scale simulations can be reproduced and interpreted analytically within this framework. We further demonstrate that the framework can be used to find the sweet spot design region of distributed QEC, which is critical for the design of distributed quantum computing systems.

</details>


### [124] [Controlling Atom Array in an Ultra-high-cooperativity Optical Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04090)
*Jilai Ye,Zhihao Chi,Ye Tian,Shuyao Mei,Xiaoyu Li,Wenjun Zhang,Yajuan Zhao,Jiazhong Hu,Wenlan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过两步镜面制备方法（精密机械成型+二氧化碳激光抛光），成功将中性原子阵列集成到毫米级法布里-珀罗腔中，实现了单原子协同性125±13和光谱协同性112.3±3.3，同时耦合多达16个独立囚禁原子，为腔辅助量子态读出和长程纠缠工程开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列与腔量子电动力学各有优势（可扩展可重构的量子比特架构 vs. 强相干光-物质耦合），但将两者结合在单一平台面临挑战：需要同时具备高协同性、足够容纳原子阵列的模体积、以及充足的侧向光学通道用于原子囚禁、成像、冷却和重排。

Method: 采用两步镜面制备方法：精密机械成型结合二氧化碳激光抛光，制备出亚毫米曲率半径、残余粗糙度低于2 Å的凹面熔融石英镜片，构建毫米尺度法布里-珀罗腔，并将中性原子阵列集成其中。

Result: 光学表征的单原子协同性达125±13；囚禁原子的腔透射光谱获得有效光谱协同性112.3±3.3，验证了强耦合；实现多达16个独立囚禁原子同时耦合到腔模反节点。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了同时提供高协同性、大模体积和灵活单原子操控的腔集成原子阵列平台，为腔辅助量子态读出和原子阵列中的长程纠缠工程开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: Neutral-atom array and cavity quantum electrodynamics offer complementary strengths for quantum science: scalable, reconfigurable qubit architectures and strong coherent light-matter coupling. Combining them in a single platform requires an optical cavity with simultaneously high cooperativity, sufficient mode volume to accommodate atom array, and ample side optical access for atom trapping, imaging, cooling, and rearrangement, a combination that is challenging to achieve. Here we realize an atomic array integrated with a millimeter-scale Fabry--Pérot cavity whose optically-characterized single-atom cooperativity reaches $η_{\mathrm{cav}}=125\pm13$. Atom-cavity transmission spectra of trapped atoms yield an effective spectroscopic cooperativity $η_{\mathrm{spec}}=112.3\pm3.3$, providing an in-situ verification of strong coupling in the integrated platform, and we demonstrate simultaneous coupling of up to 16 individually trapped atoms to the antinode of the cavity mode. The key technical advance is a two-step mirror-fabrication method combining precision mechanical shaping and carbon-dioxide laser polishing, which produces concave fused-silica mirrors with sub-millimeter radii of curvature and residual roughness below 2 Å. Our results establish a regime of cavity-integrated atomic array that simultaneously provides high cooperativity, large mode volume, and flexible manipulation of individual atoms, opening opportunities for cavity-assisted quantum state readout and long-range entanglement-engineering in atom-array platforms.

</details>


### [125] [Circuit Design Informed Adaptive Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04110)
*Muhammad Umer,Dimitris G. Angelakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在变分量子算法中系统性地整合电路设计约束（如Hadamard测试电路架构、硬件感知的量子比特连接和问题自适应框架），可将自适应算法中候选门池重复评估的测量开销减少25%-55%，并通过非线性薛定谔方程的基态问题验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NISQ时代，退相干、门错误和受限的量子比特连接严重限制了量子算法的可靠执行，因此资源高效计算至关重要。现有自适应算法在候选门池重复评估中测量开销过大，需要更系统的电路设计优化方法。

Method: 聚焦于Hadamard测试电路架构、硬件感知的量子比特连接和问题特定的自适应框架，通过系统性地整合电路设计约束来减少自适应算法中候选门池重复评估所需的测量资源。

Result: 所需测量资源减少至少25%，最高可达50%-55%。

Conclusion: 算法框架应系统性地整合电路设计约束、硬件感知结构和问题特定结构，以增强NISQ时代量子器件的实际可行性。

Abstract: Resource-efficient computation is of central importance in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era, where decoherence, gate errors, and restricted qubit connectivity severely limit the reliable execution of quantum algorithms. In this work, we demonstrate that incorporating circuit design considerations is crucial for developing resource-efficient variational quantum algorithms. By focusing on the Hadamard test circuit architecture, hardware-aware qubit connectivity, and problem-specific adaptive framework, we analyze how circuit design constraints can systematically reduce the measurement overhead associated with repeated evaluations of the candidate gate pool in adaptive algorithms. Specifically, we demonstrate reductions in the required measurement resources ranging from at least 25% to as high as 50% - 55%. To assess the effectiveness of our approach, we investigate the ground state problem of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation. Overall, our work contributes to resource-friendly strategies for quantum computation and underscores that algorithmic frameworks should systematically integrate circuit design constraints with hardware-aware and problem-specific structures to enhance the practical feasibility of quantum devices in the NISQ era.

</details>


### [126] [Realizing Next-Nearest-Neighbor Coupling and Peierls Phase in Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04175)
*Xin-Sheng Zhang,Wen-Jie Xu,Hang-Yu Gu,Chuan-Xun Du,Yong-Long Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非厄米一维Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型设计了三种三聚化电路（初始、增强、再增强），通过引入近邻耦合、次近邻耦合和Peierls相位，研究电路拉普拉斯算子的动力学，发现拓扑态出现并受耦合通道和相位显著影响，数值模拟和实验验证了结果，表明三聚化电路是研究新拓扑物理态的可调简单平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了构建一个可调且简单的实验平台来探索非厄米拓扑物理中的新拓扑态，作者设计了基于三聚化非厄米SSH模型的电路系统。

Method: 设计了三种递进的三聚化非厄米SSH电路模型：初始模型仅含最近邻耦合，增强模型加入次近邻耦合，再增强模型进一步引入Peierls相位；通过电路拉普拉斯算子的动力学分析、数值模拟和实验测量来研究拓扑性质。

Result: 初始模型中出现拓扑态；次近邻耦合通道和Peierls相位会显著影响拓扑响应区间；数值模拟与实验测量结果一致，验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 三聚化电路为研究新拓扑物理态提供了一个可调、简单的实验平台。

Abstract: We really design the trimerized circuits for the non-Hermitian one-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger models. There are three models, the initial one just considers the nearest neighbor coupling, the enhanced one is extended to contain the next-nearest-neighbor coupling, and the final one is reenhanced by introducing the Peierls phase. We investigate the dynamics of the circuit Laplacians with respect to the models, find that the topological states appear in the initial model and the response intervals are substantially affected by the next-nearest-neighbor coupling channels and the Peierls phase. These results are practically demonstrated by numerical simulations and experimental measurements. As a conclusion, the trimerized circuits can provide an adjustable and simple platform to investigate new topological physical states.

</details>


### [127] [Analytic properties of cross-click operators in passive multi-basis photodetection: monotonicity, exact convergence rates, and dimension reduction for quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04186)
*Zhangdong Ye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对被动线性光学分析仪中的交叉点击算子（cross-click operators）进行了解析刻画，给出了其最小特征值 \(f^{(n)}\) 的单调性、指数界、精确公式及双方案因子分解，并应用于量子密钥分发（QKD）和纠缠验证中的光子数权重界限计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉点击算子在QKD和纠缠验证中被用于限制多光子贡献，但此前对其最小特征值 \(f^{(n)}\) 的增长特性仅能在有限Fock空间中进行数值验证，缺乏解析理解。

Method: 关键发现是每个静默算子（silence operator）可表示为单光子收缩算子 \(A\) 的二次量子化 \(\Gamma(A)\)，从而将 \(n\) 光子限制化为对称子空间上的 \(A^{\otimes n}\)。基于此建立了谱分析与算子单调性论证。

Result: (i) 单调性 \(f^{(n+1)}\ge f^{(n)}\)；(ii) 双指数界 \(\max_b \gamma_b |A_b|^n \le 1-f^{(n)} \le \sum_b \gamma_b |A_b|^n\)；(iii) 理想探测器下精确公式 \(f^{(n)}=1-\sum_b p_b^n\)；(iv) 双方案交叉点击算子的精确因子分解 \(1-f^{(n_A,n_B)}=(1-f_A^{(n_A)})(1-f_B^{(n_B)})\)。

Conclusion: 该解析刻画替代了之前有限截断Fock空间中的数值方法，适用于偏振、时间仓和空间模式分析仪，并可用于检测效率失配分析中闭合形式的光子数权重界限。

Abstract: Cross-click operators, the POVM elements for simultaneous clicks in detectors assigned to different measurement bases, are used in QKD and entanglement-verification analyses with realistic threshold detectors to bound multiphoton contributions. Earlier applications verified the needed growth of the minimum eigenvalue $f^{(n)}$ on the $n$-photon subspace only numerically over finite sectors. This work gives an analytic characterization for passive linear-optical analyzers with arbitrary efficiency mismatch and dark counts. The key observation is that every silence operator is the second quantization $Γ(A)$ of an explicit single-photon contraction $A$, whose $n$-photon restriction is $A^{\otimes n}$ on $\mathrm{Sym}^n$. This yields: (i) monotonicity $f^{(n+1)}\ge f^{(n)}$; (ii) two-sided exponential bounds $\max_b γ_b|A_b|^n \le 1-f^{(n)} \le \sum_b γ_b|A_b|^n$, which determine the exact asymptotic convergence rate from single-photon spectral data; (iii) for ideal detectors and $n\ge1$, the exact formula $f^{(n)}=1-\sum_b p_b^n$; and (iv) an exact factorization $1-f^{(n_A,n_B)}=(1-f_A^{(n_A)})(1-f_B^{(n_B)})$ for the two-party cross-click operator. The results apply to polarization, time-bin, and spatial-mode analyzers within the stated threshold-detector model. As an application, we obtain closed-form photon-number weight bounds used in detection-efficiency-mismatch analyses, replacing finite-sector Fock-space numerics by formulas valid for all photon numbers.

</details>


### [128] [Genuine Multipartite Entanglement between Logical Qubits via Cross-Code Lattice Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04227)
*Alex Steiner,Tomasz Andrzejewski,Phila Rembold,Hendrik Poulsen Nautrup,Christian D. Marciniak,Robert Freund,Ivan Pogorelov,Thomas Monz,Philipp Schindler,Marcel Meyer,Nicolai Friis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在离子阱量子处理器上，利用晶格手术（lattice surgery）跨两种不同量子纠错码（四量子比特曲面码和八量子比特3D颜色码）实现了通用逻辑门集，包括Hadamard门和双控Pauli-Z（CCZ）门。实验生成了三个逻辑量子比特的稳定子（GHZ态）和非稳定子（|CCZ⟩态）纠缠态，并通过跨码原语演示了任意单逻辑量子比特旋转，验证了逻辑真多体纠缠，展示了容错量子计算架构的核心构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用量子计算机需要容错地实现通用逻辑门集，但单一纠错码无法横向实现所有门，需通过晶格手术组合互补码来突破这一限制。实验验证这一方案在真实硬件上的可行性是推动容错量子计算发展的关键。

Method: 采用离子阱量子处理器，通过晶格手术连接四量子比特曲面码（执行Hadamard门）和八量子比特3D颜色码（执行CCZ门），生成三逻辑量子比特的GHZ态和|CCZ⟩非稳定子态，并使用跨码原语实现任意单逻辑量子比特旋转。通过真多体纠缠验证方法认证逻辑纠缠。

Result: 成功生成并认证了三逻辑量子比特的稳定子（GHZ）和非稳定子（|CCZ⟩）真多体纠缠态，演示了基于CCZ的资源小工具实现任意单逻辑量子比特旋转，验证了晶格手术跨码通用门集的可行性。

Conclusion: 实验展示了容错量子计算架构的核心构建模块：通过晶格手术跨码实现通用逻辑门集，并能生成复杂的逻辑量子态，为未来大规模容错量子计算铺平了道路。

Abstract: Universal quantum computers are expected to generate arbitrary complex quantum states of logical qubits encoded in many physical qubits. This capability hinges on a fault-tolerantly implemented universal gate set, which no single quantum error-correction code admits transversally but which becomes accessible by joining complementary codes via lattice surgery. Here we report on the experimental generation and certification of logical genuine multipartite entanglement in a trapped-ion quantum processor using a transversally implemented universal logical gate set. The gate set is accessed via lattice surgery across two different codes and comprises a Hadamard gate on a four-qubit surface code and a doubly controlled Pauli-$Z$ ($\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}$) gate on an eight-qubit 3D colour code. To showcase this lattice-surgery toolbox, we generate both stabiliser (Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger) and non-stabiliser ($|\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}\rangle$) states of three logical qubits and verify their genuine multipartite entanglement--a form of correlation beyond statistical mixtures of bipartite entanglement across any bipartition. We further use these cross-code primitives to demonstrate arbitrary rotations of single logical qubits via a $\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}$-based resource gadget accessing the full universal gate set through lattice surgery. Together, these demonstrations showcase the core building blocks of an architecture for fault-tolerant quantum computation and its ability to generate complex logical quantum states.

</details>


### [129] [Weak ergodicity breaking without nonthermal eigenstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04279)
*Boning Huang,Yongguan Ke,Li Zhang,Lin Ling,Chaohong Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在无非热本征态的系统中，通过空间调制Bose-Hubbard lattice中近线性能带的Wannier态构造，发现了一种新的弱遍历性破缺机制，该机制基于ETH本征态间的谱相位相干性产生长寿命集体回复，而非量子疤痕等非热本征态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找除多体局域化和量子疤痕之外，由ETH本征态间的谱相位相干性驱动的弱遍历性破缺新机制。

Method: 在空间调制的Bose-Hubbard晶格中，利用周期性调制将原有能带折叠分离为多个子带，从中提取近线性子带；构造多粒子Wannier态（MWSs）作为单个能带内多粒子Bloch态的叠加，实现能带分辨的Wannier扇区分裂；分析MWSs的能级间距与集体回复动力学行为。

Result: 尽管多粒子Bloch态满足ETH，但近线性子带中的MWSs由于具有近似等间距的能级结构，仍展现出长寿命的集体回复现象。

Conclusion: 提供了一种弱遍历性破缺的新途径：长寿命回复可以来源于满足ETH的本征态之间的谱相位相干性，而非传统的非热本征态（如疤痕态）。

Abstract: The typical mechanisms of ergodicity breaking in isolated interacting quantum systems, such as many-body localization and quantum many-body scars, originate from the nonthermal nature of the underlying eigenstates. Here, in the absence of nonthermal eigenstates, we identify a mechanism for collective revivals of multiparticle Wannier states (MWSs) associated with nearly linear bands in a spatially modulated Bose-Hubbard lattice. The MWSs, as superpositions of multiparticle Bloch states within individual energy bands, give rise to band-resolved Wannier-sector fragmentation. The key idea is that spatially periodic modulation folds and separates energy bands of a simple lattice into several sub-bands, among which nearly linear sub-bands inherit the linear segments of the original bands. Although multiparticle Bloch states satisfy the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), the MWSs in the nearly linear band still exhibit long-lived collective revivals, due to emergent equally spaced energy levels. Our work provides a route to weak ergodicity breaking in which long-lived revivals arise from spectral phase coherence among ETH-satisfying eigenstates rather than from scar-like nonthermal eigenstates.

</details>


### [130] [Chromatic Completeness and the Independence of Geometric Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04289)
*Karl Svozil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在正交超图中严格区分了两种现象：色完备性（为所有语境分配全局一致的非退化谱）与几何坐标化（存在由射线构成的正交表示）。通过两个具有相同强色数（均为4）的三维例子——Yu-Oh构型的25射线版本（存在忠实正交表示）与Greechie的G32超图（不存在C^3中的忠实正交表示）——证明了强色数大于希尔伯特空间维度仅阻碍前者，而非后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将正交超图的色数性质与几何坐标化能力混为一谈，认为强色数大于空间维度即意味着无法实现正交表示。本文旨在澄清这一混淆，建立两者的严格逻辑分离。

Method: 通过构造两个三维例子（25射线的Yu-Oh构型与Greechie G32超图）进行比较分析。前者给出显式的R^3忠实正交表示，后者则通过初等代数证明其不存在C^3中的忠实正交表示。

Result: 1) 25射线Yu-Oh构型：强色数=4，但存在R^3中的忠实正交表示；2) Greechie G32超图：强色数=4，具有分离且单位的双值态集，但不存在C^3中的忠实正交表示。G32的障碍在于射影几何性质——关联关系迫使两个不同的原子坍缩到同一射线上。

Conclusion: 强色数大于希尔伯特空间维度仅阻碍色完备性，不阻碍忠实正交表示的存在性。正交超图的坐标化障碍本质上是射影几何性的，而非色数性的。

Abstract: We establish a strict logical separation between two distinct phenomena in orthogonality hypergraphs: chromatic completeness, the possibility of assigning a single globally consistent nondegenerate spectrum to all contexts, and geometric coordinatizability, the existence of a faithful orthogonal representation by rays. A strong chromatic number larger than the Hilbert-space dimension obstructs only the former. It does not, by itself, obstruct the existence of a faithful orthogonal representation. We make this separation explicit by comparing two three-dimensional examples with the same strong chromatic number. A completed 25-ray version of the Yu-Oh configuration has strong chromatic number four and nevertheless possesses an explicit faithful orthogonal representation in R^3. Conversely, Greechie's G_{32} hypergraph also has strong chromatic number four, and has a separating and unital set of two-valued states, but we give an elementary algebraic proof that it admits no faithful orthogonal representation in C^3. The obstruction in G_{32} is therefore not chromatic but projective-geometric: the incidence relations force two distinct atoms to collapse onto the same ray.

</details>


### [131] [Optimal estimation of quantum boundary effect in cosmic string space-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04295)
*Yao Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙弦对真空涨落的修正如何使两能级极化原子的演化产生位置依赖性，这种量子边界效应可通过单探测原子的N次测量序列来估计。研究发现在固定总探测时间下，通过优化初始态和测量方案可达到精度极限，且激发态初始化的精度是等权重叠加态的四倍。原子-边界或原子-弦分离距离与估计精度呈阻尼振荡关系，而最优极化方向在宇宙弦时空中依赖于原子-弦间距和缺陷角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙弦引起的真空涨落修正对原子动力学产生的量子边界效应，以及如何通过量子测量技术对这些效应进行高精度估计。

Method: 采用两能级极化原子作为探针，通过执行N次最优序列测量，在固定总探测时间下优化初始态和测量方案，分析不同极化方向和原子-弦间距对估计精度的影响。

Result: 激发态初始化的精度极限是等权重叠加态的四倍；估计精度随原子-边界或原子-弦间距呈阻尼振荡；在宇宙弦时空中最优极化方向依赖于间距和缺陷角，小缺陷角且大间距时沿弦方向极化不再最优。

Conclusion: 宇宙弦的量子边界效应可通过单原子探针的序列测量实现高精度估计，为探测宇宙弦等拓扑缺陷提供了量子计量学方法。

Abstract: The presence of a cosmic string modifies vacuum fluctuations, making the evolution of a two-level polarizable atom position dependent. Such modifications produce effects on the atomic dynamics analogous to those induced by a reflecting boundary. We show that these quantum boundary effects can be estimated by performing a sequence of $N$ measurements on a single probe atom. For a fixed total probe time, the precision limit is attained by preparing each probe in its optimal initial state, performing the corresponding optimal measurement, and shortening the probe time of each probe. The optimal measurement is uniquely determined by the probe's initial state, and the precision limit obtained with the atom initially in the excited state is four times higher than that for an equal-weight superposition state. The estimation precision displays damped oscillatory behavior as the atom-boundary or atom-string separation increases. While polarization parallel to the reflecting boundary is always optimal in the boundary case, the optimal polarization in cosmic-string space-time depends on both the atom-string separation and the deficit angle. For small deficit angles and sufficiently large separations, polarization along the cosmic-string direction becomes inferior to the other polarization directions.

</details>


### [132] [Incompatibility assisted Zeno-like confinement enables unbounded sharing of nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04298)
*Prerna Rao,Som Kanjilal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究贝尔非局域性的序列共享问题，发现无界副本共享需满足两个条件：测量不相容性以及后处理态的持续性非局域性。通过反交换泡利可观测量下的非精准测量和概率投影测量两种场景，证明了不相容性对初始态和测量噪声的约束，并发现渐近极限下所有副本的后处理态几乎相同，呈现量子芝诺效应式的非局域区域束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索贝尔非局域性是否可以被无限制数量的副本序列共享，并揭示实现这一目标所需满足的物理条件和约束。

Method: 针对两种测量场景（反交换泡利可观测量下的非精准测量和概率投影测量）进行理论分析，研究测量不相容性对初始非局域态选择和测量噪声的要求，并分析渐近极限下后处理态的行为。

Result: 发现测量不相容性对初始非局域态和测量噪声施加了非平凡约束；在渐近极限下，所有副本的非局域后处理态几乎相同，形成量子芝诺效应式的非局域区域束缚。

Conclusion: 实现了无界副本对贝尔非局域性的序列共享，揭示了不相容性约束与量子芝诺效应在该过程中的关键作用。

Abstract: To enable sequential sharing of Bell-nonlocality by an unbounded number of copies, each copy must satisfy two requirements. First, the measurements must be incompatible to extract nonlocality. Second, the post-processsed state, obtained as an equal mixture of the two post-measurement states, must remain nonlocal. We show that the incompatibility requirements impose nontrivial constraints on the choice of the initial nonlocal state and the amount of measurement noise required to ensure that the post-processed state remains nonlocal for an unbounded number of copies. We establish this result for two measurement scenarios, namely, when each copy performs unsharp measurements corresponding to a pair of anti-commuting Pauli observables, and when each copy performs probabilistic projective measurements (PPMs) of a pair of anti-commuting Pauli observables. Furthermore, we show that, in the asymptotic limit, the nonlocal post-processed states of all the copies are almost identical, leading to a quantum Zeno-like confinement within the nonlocal region.

</details>


### [133] [Fundamental limits on state preparation for an open qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04320)
*D. A. Abraamian,L. V. Lokutsievskiy,A. N. Pechen*

TL;DR: 本文解析地确定了由相干驱动控制的两能级开放量子系统中态制备的终极极限，给出了耗散量子比特在Bloch球中可达集的精确刻画，并证明了不可达区域具有非平凡几何结构，其边界为绕x轴的旋转曲面（除两个锥形奇点外是解析的）。作者推导了在边界上运动的闭环控制协议，并构造了将系统任意逼近指定边界态的显式协议。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定开放两能级量子系统在相干控制下态制备的终极极限，建立可达到状态集合的完整几何与解析描述，为量子控制保真度提供基本界限。

Method: 基于GKSL主方程描述耗散量子比特动力学，利用Bloch球表示分析可达集的几何结构，通过解析方法刻画不可达区域的边界（绕x轴旋转曲面，含两个锥形奇点），并构造闭环与显式控制协议。

Result: 给出了耗散量子比特可达集的精确特征描述；证明了不可达区域具有绕x轴对称的旋转曲面边界，除两个锥形奇点外为解析曲面；推导了边界运动的闭环控制协议；构造了逼近任意边界态的显式控制协议。

Conclusion: 该工作为开放两能级量子系统在标准耗散环境下的可达态提供了完整的几何构造性描述，确立了可控性和态制备保真度的基本界限，对量子控制理论具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We analytically determine the ultimate limits of state preparation in two-level open quantum systems driven by coherent control. For a dissipative qubit governed by a GKSL master equation, we give an exact characterization of the reachable set in the Bloch ball. Dissipation excludes a region of states in the Bloch ball which cannot be approached even under arbitrarily strong coherent driving, and we prove that this region has a nontrivial geometry whose boundary is a surface of revolution around the $x$-axis which is analytic except for two conical singularities. We derive a closed-form control protocol for moving on this boundary, and construct an explicit protocol that steers the system arbitrarily close to any prescribed boundary state. These results provide a complete geometric constructive description of reachable qubit states in the standard dissipative environment, establishing fundamental bounds on controllability and state-preparation fidelity for open two-level quantum systems.

</details>

Conclusion: 广义Dirac振子中的纠缠熵可通过超对称性代数求解，其相对论本质及能量依赖行为得到了清晰刻画。

Abstract: The supersymmetric properties of the generalized Dirac oscillator allow us to determine the entanglement entropy between the spin and the continuous variable in a purely algebraic manner. The entanglement has a relativistic origin and disappears in the nonrelativistic limit. The entanglement entropy attains its maximal value in the limit of infinite energy.

### [135] [Purcell effect and quantum Zeno effect suppressed self-discharging of quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04363)
*Da-Wei Liu,Guoqing Tian,Zi-Hao Li,Shi-Qi Gan,Ying Wu,Liu-Gang Si*
