TL;DR: 本文提出利用Purcell效应和量子Zeno效应将充电器引起的耗散转化为保护机制，从而抑制量子电池的自放电效应，使耗散降低四个数量级，实现近乎满充状态和高能量转换效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池在开放系统中存在自放电问题，尤其是充电器本身提供了不可避免的损耗通道，这严重阻碍了有用功的存储。

Method: 结合Purcell效应与量子Zeno效应，利用虚光子过程和Purcell效应诱导从强耦合到量子Zeno区域的转变，将充电器耗散转化为保护机制。

Result: 充电器对量子电池的耗散被抑制了四个数量级；量子Zeno效应还避免了电池与充电器之间的能量回流；储能可近乎满充，且存储能量几乎全部转化为可提取功。

Conclusion: 该方案有效解决了充电器耗散导致的量子电池自放电问题，显著提升了能量存储和转换效率。

Abstract: Quantum batteries (QB), as an energy storage and transfer device, not only show obvious advantages compared to classical electrochemical batteries, but also have important applications in quantum information. Self-discharging is a central obstacle to storing useful work in open QB, especially when the charger itself provides an unavoidable loss channel. Here we show that such charger-induced loss can be converted into a protection mechanism by combining Purcell effect with quantum Zeno effect. We reveal that the virtual photon process and the Purcell effect can induce the strong coupling regime to the quantum Zeno regime, in which the stronger the dissipation of the charger, the weaker the self-discharging effect of the QB. As a result, the dissipation caused by the charger to the QB can be suppressed four orders of magnitude in our scheme. Meanwhile, the quantum Zeno effect induced by the Purcell effect can also avoid the energy backflow between the QB and the charger. Owing to the significantly suppressed dissipation, the stored energy of QB can be charged to a nearly full state and the stored energy is almost converted into extractable work, which greatly improves the energy conversion efficiency.

</details>


### [136] [Topological properties and Majorana Multiplicity in Zigzag Kitaev Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04399)
*Rajiv Kumar,Panch Ram,Levan Chotorlishvili,Sunil Kumar Mishra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由两条对角耦合的一维Kitaev链构建的锯齿形Kitaev链，分析了其在零和有限超导配对相位差下的谱与拓扑性质。研究发现零相位差时存在支持两个、四个马约拉纳零模(MZMs)的拓扑相及平庸区；π相位差时MZMs简并部分解除，拓扑相发生改变。通过缠绕数ν=1、2和0分别对应两种拓扑相和平庸区，建立了完整的相图，为基于MZM的量子计算提供了最小平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究锯齿形Kitaev链在不同超导相位差下的拓扑性质，特别是马约拉纳零模的分布与拓扑相变，为马约拉纳量子计算寻找最小可行平台。

Method: 采用Bogoliubov-de Gennes(BdG)形式化方法，分析能谱、MZMs分布、准粒子色散及缠绕数等拓扑不变量，结合相图分析。

Result: 零相位差时系统呈现支持两个MZMs、四个MZMs的拓扑相以及平庸区；π相位差时MZMs简并部分解除导致拓扑相改变。缠绕数ν=1和2对应两种拓扑相，ν=0对应平庸区。能谱分析证实了相边界并揭示了杂化特征。

Conclusion: 锯齿形Kitaev链可作为工程化MZM量子计算的最小平台，对拓扑相和马约拉纳量子比特物理具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral and topological properties of a zigzag Kitaev chain constructed from two diagonally coupled one dimensional Kitaev chains with a zero and finite superconducting pairing phase difference. Using a Bogoliubov-de Gennes formulation, we analyze the energy spectrum, distribution of Majorana zero modes (MZMs), the quasi-particle dispersion, and the winding number, respectively. For a zero phase difference, the resulting energy spectrum shows topological phases with two, four MZMs, and trivial regions. The phases of gap closure determine the topological phase boundaries. In particular, for the phase difference between $φ=π$, the degeneracy of MZMs is partially lifted, leading to modified topological phases compared to the case $φ=0$. The topological and trivial phase boundaries are further confirmed by evaluating the quasi-particle dispersion and the topological invariant, namely the winding number. We show that the zigzag Kitaev chain contributes independently to the total winding number $ν= 1$ and $2$, giving rise to distinct topological phases that support two and four MZMs. The $ν= 0$ gives rise to a trivial region. The energy spectrum of systems corroborates the analytical phase boundaries and reveals characteristics associated with hybridization, enabling us to obtain the complete phase diagram of the zigzag model. Our results establish the zigzag Kitaev chain as a minimal platform for engineering MZM quantum computations, with potential applications in the study of topological phases and Majorana based qubit physics.

</details>


### [137] [Noise-Aware Synthesis of Quantum LDPC Encoder Circuits via Two-Sided Hamming Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04462)
*Aditya Sodhani,Keshab K. Parhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"两侧汉明下降"的电路重综合方法，用于优化量子LDPC编码器的CNOT块实现。该方法通过将编码器准备问题转化为线性可逆矩阵的重综合问题，在多个CSS LDPC编码器家族（包括Bivariate Bicycle、超图乘积和纠缠辅助码）上将门数量平均减少53.8%，双量子比特深度降低达71%，并提升了噪声环境下的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码是实现容错量子计算的重要途径，但其使用需要高效制备编码态。标准编码器构造通过固定代数程序生成电路，但这些电路包含大量冗余，导致在目标硬件上路由效率低且噪声影响大。因此需要一种优化方法，在保持功能等价的前提下降低编码器电路的尺寸和深度，并使其适应硬件约束。

Method: 将LDPC编码器准备问题形式化为电路重综合问题：针对编码器CNOT块实现的线性可逆矩阵，寻找更低成本的等价电路。提出两侧汉明下降(Two-sided Hamming descent)优化方法，并结合噪声感知的优化流水线，包括硬件校准的电路选择与存活区间调度(live-range scheduling)。

Result: 在Bivariate Bicycle、超图乘积和纠缠辅助码等多个CSS LDPC编码器家族上，该方法相比标准构造和综合基线，门数量平均减少53.8%（Bivariate Bicycle家族最高达68%），路由后双量子比特深度降低达71%。在电路级噪声下，实现了更高保真度的态制备。在Bivariate Bicycle家族上，存活区间调度在不增加双量子比特门的情况下，将路由后的制备失败率进一步降低最多13.7%。

Conclusion: 编码器矩阵重综合结合硬件校准的选择与调度，是制备量子LDPC码态的有效编译器级工具。该方法能够显著减小电路尺寸和深度，提升噪声环境下的态制备保真度，为量子LDPC码的实际部署提供了实用的编译优化手段。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes are a promising route to fault-tolerant quantum computation, but their use requires efficient preparation of encoded states. Standard encoder constructions generate circuits through fixed algebraic procedures, yet the resulting circuit can contain substantial redundancy. We formulate LDPC encoder preparation as a circuit-resynthesis problem: given the linear-reversible matrix implemented by the encoder's CNOT block, we seek a lower-cost equivalent circuit that can be routed efficiently on the target hardware and which mitigates noise. We propose a novel optimization approach referred as two-sided Hamming descent and a noise-aware optimization pipeline for this task.
  Across several families of Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) LDPC encoders, including Bivariate Bicycle, hypergraph-product, and entanglement-assisted codes, the proposed pipeline produces substantially smaller and shallower encoder circuits than the standard constructions and the synthesis baselines considered, cutting gate counts by 53.8% in aggregate across the benchmark and by up to 68% on the Bivariate Bicycle family. The gains remain visible after routing, where the two-qubit depth is reduced by up to 71% and translate into higher-fidelity state preparation under circuit-level noise. On the Bivariate Bicycle family, live-range scheduling further reduces routed preparation failure by up to 13.7% without adding two-qubit gates to the selected circuit. These results indicate that encoder-matrix resynthesis, combined with hardware-calibrated selection and scheduling, is an effective compiler-level tool for preparing quantum LDPC code states.

</details>


### [138] [Trace-to-Hilbert-Schmidt Speed Ratio in Quantum Dynamics: Universal Bounds and Effective Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04488)
*Hossein Rangani Jahromi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了可微量子态迹速度与Hilbert-Schmidt速度之比R(φ)=||∂_φρ(φ)||_1/||∂_φρ(φ)||_2，给出了该比值的严格界限：对秩为r的非零切算子有√2 ≤ R ≤ √r，且下界在秩为2时达到，上界在非零奇异值全相等时达到。在纯态族非平稳点处R=√2。对于奇数维Hilbert空间，证明了全局最大值R≤√(d-1/d)。将R²解释为切算子奇异值分布的逆参与比，建立了有效秩与量子Fisher信息之间的直接不等式，并推导了有效秩控制的量子速度极限层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态演化速率的不同度量（迹范数与Hilbert-Schmidt范数）之比受到切算子的厄米性和无迹性约束，其界限尚未被完整刻画，且该比值与量子信息的几何结构（如有效秩、量子Fisher信息）之间的关系有待阐明。

Method: 利用切算子X=∂_φρ的厄米无迹性质，通过奇异值分解和谱分析推导范数比的严格上下界；引入R²作为逆参与比，建立有效秩的经典（本征值）与量子（本征向量）分解；通过不等式关联有效秩与量子Fisher信息及迹速度。

Result: (1) 对秩r的切算子，√2 ≤ ||X||_1/||X||_2 ≤ √r，下界在秩2达到，上界在非零奇异值全相等时达到（要求偶数秩）；(2) 纯态族非平稳点处R=√2；(3) 奇数维全局最大值R≤√(d-1/d)；(4) 有效秩r_eff ≤ r_C + r_Q，其中一项为零时取等；(5) 建立有效秩与QFI的不等式，当QFI相对于迹速度平方较小时强制大量活跃奇异模存在；(6) 导出有效秩控制Hilbert-Schmidt速度量子速度极限紧致性的层级。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了量子态可微轨迹中两种常用范数速度之比的严格界限，揭示了该比值作为切算子有效秩诊断工具的含义，并通过经典-量子分解和与QFI的关联深化了对量子动力学局部结构几何的理解。

Abstract: We study the ratio between the trace speed and the Hilbert-Schmidt speed for differentiable finite-dimensional quantum states, $\mathcal R(φ)=\|\partial_φρ(φ)\|_1/\|\partial_φρ(φ)\|_2$. Because $\partial_φρ(φ)$ is always Hermitian and traceless, this ratio is constrained more strongly than for a generic operator. For any nonzero tangent operator $X=\partial_φρ$ of rank $r$, we prove the sharp bounds $\sqrt{2}\le \|X\|_1/\|X\|_2\le \sqrt r$. The lower bound is attained exactly for rank-two tangents, while the upper bound is attained exactly when all nonzero singular values are equal, which in the traceless Hermitian setting requires even rank. At every nonstationary point of a pure-state family, the tangent has rank two, implying $\mathcal R=\sqrt2$. For odd Hilbert-space dimension $d$, we further prove the sharp global maximum $\mathcal R\le \sqrt{d-1/d}$, with equality characterized by full-rank spectra whose positive and negative eigenvalues are separately degenerate and have multiplicities differing by one. We identify $\mathcal R^2$ as the inverse participation ratio of the singular-value distribution of the tangent operator, giving $\mathcal R$ a natural interpretation as an effective-rank diagnostic for local quantum dynamics. Furthermore, we decompose the effective rank into classical (eigenvalue) and quantum (eigenvector) contributions and prove the bound $r_{\mathrm{eff}}\le r_C + r_Q$, with equality guaranteed when either component vanishes. We establish a direct inequality linking the effective rank to the quantum Fisher information (QFI), which forces a large number of active singular modes when the QFI is small relative to the squared trace speed. Finally, we derive a hierarchy of quantum speed limits in which the effective rank controls the tightness of bounds expressed through the Hilbert-Schmidt speed.

</details>


### [139] [Schrodinger Oscillator and its Thermal Properties in a Dynamical Noncommutative Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04519)
*Ilyas Haouam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维薛定谔振子在动力学非对易空间中的行为，利用微扰理论推导能量本征值和本征向量，计算一阶修正，并通过配分函数分析热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索动力学非对易空间中二维薛定谔振子的量子行为，揭示动力学非对易参数τ对系统能谱和热力学性质的影响。

Method: 采用微扰理论推导能量本征值和本征向量的解析表达式；利用二维Bopp-shift将非对易问题映射到对易空间；通过配分函数研究系统热性质；进行数值计算分析系统行为。

Result: 获得了能谱的一阶修正，发现能量偏移显式依赖于动力学非对易参数τ；数值结果展示了系统在动力学和非动力学非对易设置下的行为差异。

Conclusion: 动力学非对易空间中的对易关系和形变参数具有位置依赖性，这为量子系统在非对易几何中的研究提供了新的视角和分析方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the two-dimensional Schrodinger oscillator within a dynamical noncommutative (DNC) space. By leveraging perturbation theory, we derive the energy eigenvalues and eigenvectors and systematically analyze the effects of both dynamical and non-dynamical noncommutative settings. First-order corrections to the eigensystem are obtained, revealing that the energy shift explicitly depends on the DNC parameter tau. Furthermore, we explore the thermal properties of the system by employing the partition function. Numerical results are presented to provide a comprehensive analysis of the system behavior under the considered effects. Notably, in the DNC framework, the commutation relations and the deformation parameter are position-dependent. Using the two-dimensional Bopp-shift, we effectively map the noncommutative problem to its commutative counterpart.

</details>


### [140] [Security of Quantum Conference Key Agreement with Two-Way Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04538)
*Shun Kawakami,Mori Watanabe,Takuya Ikuta,Koichi Takasugi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于三粒子GHZ态和两个测量基的量子会议密钥协商（QCKA）在双向经典通信下的渐近安全性，包括多轮B-step和P-step预处理。通过解析推导安全密钥率，证明迭代B-step可将容错阈值提升至20%以上，显著优于无双向通信的~11%和单次B-step的~15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际QCKA实现通常比传统双向量子密钥分发（QKD）面临更高的噪声水平，提高可容忍错误阈值是一个重要挑战。

Method: 对基于三粒子GHZ态和两个测量基的QCKA协议进行渐近安全性分析，采用双向经典通信中的多轮B-step和P-step预处理，解析推导安全密钥率。

Result: 迭代B-step可将可容忍错误阈值提升至20%以上，相较于无双向经典通信的~11%和单次B-step的~15%有显著提升。

Conclusion: 双向经典通信能够显著增强实际QCKA协议的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quantum conference key agreement (QCKA) enables multiple users to establish a common secret key with information-theoretic security and is regarded as a key primitive for secure communication in future quantum networks. However, practical implementations of QCKA typically suffer from higher noise levels than conventional bipartite quantum key distribution (QKD), making the improvement of the tolerable error threshold an important challenge. Gottesman and Lo proposed two preprocessing procedures for QKD with two-way classical communication, known as the B-step and the P-step, which enhance the tolerable error threshold. In this paper, we analyze the asymptotic security of QCKA with tripartite GHZ states and two measurement bases using two-way classical communication, including multiple B-steps and P-steps. We derive the corresponding secure key rate analytically and demonstrate that iterative B-steps can increase the tolerable error threshold beyond 20%, significantly improving upon the approximately 11% threshold achievable without two-way classical communication and the approximately 15% threshold obtained with only a single B-step. Our results show that two-way classical communication can substantially enhance the robustness of practical QCKA protocols.

</details>


### [141] [Equiangular weighted frames and conical 2-designs with unequal traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04555)
*Katarzyna Siudzińska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非等迹广义等角加权框架的锥形2-设计构造方法，突破了传统元素等迹的限制，详细分析了其性质、与量子测量的关系及应用，并给出了超越已知类别的示例，为完全刻画信息过完备量子测量迈出了重要一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥形2-设计具有重要的对称性性质，能有效表征关键量子度量，但其一般结构和性质尚不明确。现有构造方法局限于等迹元素，缺乏对更一般情形的理解。

Method: 基于广义等角加权框架向非投影算子的推广，提出了一种元素不必等迹的锥形2-设计构造方法。

Result: 给出了非等迹锥形2-设计的具体构造，分析了其性质、与量子测量的关系，并提供了超越已知类别的示例。

Conclusion: 该工作为完全刻画信息过完备量子测量奠定了重要基础，拓展了锥形2-设计的研究范畴。

Abstract: Conical 2-designs are families of positive operators possessing crucial symmetry properties that allow them to efficiently characterize important quantum measures. However, little is known about their general structures and properties. We propose a construction method of conical 2-designs whose elements are not of equal traces, based on a generalization of equiangular weighted frames to non-projective operators. We provide a detailed analysis of their properties, relations to quantum measurements, and applications, together with examples that go beyond the known classes. This constitutes a major step toward a full characterization of informationally overcomplete quantum measurements.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum random walks on d-regular graphs with Haar-random coin operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04584)
*Alice C. Quillen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用Haar随机酉硬币算子的离散量子随机游走变体，平均信道对硬币子空间产生退极化效应，使其类似于经典随机游走。尽管硬币子空间退极化，但顶点子空间的测量仍可揭示初始量子态信息，并在阿贝尔群Cayley图（如循环图、超立方体图）上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强随机性（Haar随机酉矩阵）作用于硬币算子时，量子随机游走的动力学行为，以及退极化信道下量子信息是否仍可被提取。

Method: 在d-正则无向连通简单图上，每步独立采样Haar均匀分布的随机酉矩阵作为硬币算子，通过统计平均分析量子信道的演化特性。

Result: 平均信道使硬币子空间完全退极化（类似公平硬币的经典随机游走），但顶点子空间的测量仍能长期保留初始量子态信息；在循环图和超立方体图上验证了这一现象。

Conclusion: Haar随机硬币的量子随机游走因退极化不适用于搜索算法，但可作为研究强扰动子系统中量子信息可恢复性的理论工具。

Abstract: With unitary coin operator that is a random matrix drawn from a uniform distribution with respect to the Haar measure, we construct a variant of a discrete quantum random walk using a d-regular undirected connected simple graph. With each step of the walk, the coin operator random matrix is drawn independently from the distribution. The expectation value over the distribution of random unitaries for the associated quantum channel gives a depolarization channel for the coin subspace and resembles the associated classical random walk where the direction a walker steps depends upon the outcome of a fair coin or balanced d-sided dice. Remarkably, despite the fact that the averaged channel depolarizes the coin subspace, measurements in the vertex subspace can be designed that would reveal information about the initial quantum state, even after many iterations of the channel. We illustrate with examples of quantum walks on Cayley graphs of Abelian groups, such as the cycle and hypercube graphs. These quantum walks are examples of bipartite strongly interacting systems, where one subsystem is strongly perturbed, yet information about the initial state in the other subsystem is potentially measurable forever. Due to its decoherence, a quantum random walk with a Haar-random coin would not be useful in search algorithms but could aid in understanding quantum systems with strongly perturbed subsystems.

</details>


### [143] [QuTuner: Feature- and Learning-Guided Optimization Pass Tuning for Quantum Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04586)
*Ming Zhong,Xiangyu Ren,Jinglei Cheng,Shaohua Li,Zhiding Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuTuner是一个面向量子编译器的特征引导的编译优化pass调优框架。它通过构建大规模优化数据集，结合静态电路特征和优化感知的pass嵌入来刻画电路特性，并训练两个离线模型来为未见过的电路检索和排序候选pass序列，再辅以轻量级精调。实验表明，在Qiskit上相比最强基线，QuTuner将评估指标提升最高84.85%，同时调优时间减少73.59%；在PyTKET上指标提升最高18.68%，调优时间减少64.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译器pass调优方法存在两个局限：仅搜索优化pass空间中的一小部分，且主要依赖静态特征，无法显式反映电路对编译器优化的响应。因此需要一种能更全面探索pass空间并融合动态响应特征的自适应调优框架。

Method: QuTuner首先构建大规模优化数据集；然后从两个互补视角刻画电路：静态电路特征描述电路结构，优化感知的pass嵌入总结电路对各个优化pass的响应；基于这些表示训练两个离线模型（检索和排序），为未见电路推荐候选pass序列，最后进行轻量级精调。

Result: 在Qiskit上，QuTuner相比最强基线将评估指标提升最多84.85%，同时调优时间降低73.59%；在PyTKET上，指标提升最多18.68%，调优时间降低64.49%。

Conclusion: QuTuner为量子编译器提供了一种有效的自适应pass调优方法，能够跨编译器和优化目标泛化，在提升优化效果的同时大幅减少调优时间。

Abstract: Quantum compilers play a key role in transforming quantum circuits into lower-cost implementations with improved execution fidelity. This process is commonly guided by circuit-level metrics, such as gate counts and circuit depth. Although compiler pass tuning has been widely studied in classical compilation, directly transferring these techniques to quantum compilers is challenging, because quantum programs are expressed as circuits and exhibit optimization behaviors that are shaped by quantum-specific structures. Prior quantum compiler tuning approaches have begun to use circuit features to guide pass selection, but they remain limited in two aspects: they search only a small portion of the optimization-pass space, and they mainly rely on static features that do not explicitly reflect how a circuit reacts to compiler optimizations.
  We present QuTuner, a feature-guided quantum compiler pass tuning framework that generalizes across compilers and tuning objectives. QuTuner first builds a large optimization dataset. It then characterizes each circuit from two complementary views: static circuit features that describe circuit structure, and optimization-aware pass embeddings that summarize the circuit's responses to individual optimization passes. Using these representations, QuTuner trains two offline models to retrieve and rank candidate pass sequences for unseen circuits, followed by lightweight refinement. We evaluate QuTuner on Qiskit and PyTKET using two benchmark suites. On Qiskit, QuTuner improves the evaluation-metric reduction by up to 84.85% over the strongest baseline while reducing tuning time by 73.59%. On PyTKET, it improves metric reduction by up to 18.68% with a 64.49% reduction in tuning time. These results show that QuTuner provides an effective approach to adaptive pass tuning for quantum compilers.

</details>


### [144] [Breaking the One-Dimensional Expressibility-Trainability Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04598)
*Kyoungho Cho,Yu-Seong Jeon,Jinhyoung Lee,Jeongho Bang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了参数化量子电路（PQC）中表达性与可训练性并非一维权衡，通过将纠缠能力（EP）和纠缠能力偏差（EPD）作为两个独立的设计旋钮，证明高希尔伯特空间覆盖与梯度消失（贫瘠高原）是不同阶段的相变事件，从而为设计高表达性且可训练的量子电路提供了实用准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知认为PQC的表达性（覆盖希尔伯特空间能力）与可训练性（避免贫瘠高原BP）之间存在内在矛盾——表达性越高，电路越随机，越容易触发梯度消失。作者试图证明这一矛盾并非一维折衷，而是由三个不等价对象（参数系综覆盖、固定电路纠缠响应、局域梯度矩）被错误地归约为单一标量叙事所致。

Method: 作者引入矩层级分析框架：EP是全局两拷贝平均（输出纠缠分布的均值），EPD是全局四拷贝涨落描述符（衡量输入依赖的可变性），而梯度方差是局域两拷贝收缩（由参数光锥和代价观测量选择）。通过这一层级分离，证明相同EP的电路可因EPD不同而具有不同的梯度方差（即不同的可训练性）。

Result: 理论证明EP和EPD构成两个独立的设计旋钮：EP衡量电路在覆盖度旋钮上的移动距离，EPD监测输入依赖的可变性是否存续。实验发现某些电路路由在达到高Haar-like覆盖度时，EPD和梯度方差尚未崩塌，表明覆盖度与BP激活是不同标度下的交叉事件。

Conclusion: 打破了一维表达性-可训练性权衡的简化认知，提出双旋钮设计规则：在高覆盖度但BP类均匀化尚未抹除可训练结构的窗口区间内，搜索高表达性PQC是可行的。EP/EPD框架为量子电路架构搜索提供了实用的度量标准。

Abstract: Expressive parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs) are often designed under a dilemma: the growth of expressibility and entangling power (EP) that improves Hilbert-space coverage is also expected to randomize an ansatz and activate barren-plateau (BP) conditions. We show that this dilemma is not a one-dimensional tradeoff. The usual picture collapses three inequivalent objects -- parameter-ensemble coverage, fixed-circuit entangling response, and local gradient moments -- into one scalar narrative. For a fixed circuit probed by Haar-product inputs, EP is a global two-copy mean of the output-entanglement distribution, whereas entangling-power deviation (EPD) is a global four-copy fluctuation descriptor. Gradient variance, however, is a local two-copy contraction selected by a parameter light cone and a cost observable. This moment hierarchy yields an analytic separation: equal EP need not imply equal trainability, as witnessed by equal-EP circuits with different EPDs and different gradient variances. These separations turn EP and EPD into a two-dial design rule for PQC ansatzes: EP measures how far the circuit has moved along the coverage dial, while EPD monitors whether input-dependent variability remains. We find that ansatz routes can reach high, Haar-like coverage before EPD and gradient variance collapse, showing that coverage and BP activation are distinct crossover events. The EP/EPD framework thus breaks the apparent one-dimensional expressibility-trainability tradeoff into a practical design rule: search for highly expressive PQCs in the window where coverage is high but BP-like homogenization has not yet erased trainable structure.

</details>


### [145] [Measurement Geometry as a Resource for Certifying Network Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04656)
*Leon Adachi,Le Bin Ho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双局域Alice-Bob-Charlie量子网络中，利用辅助量子比特的测量装置来评估网络非局域性，并在156超导量子处理器上实验验证了双局域非局域性和全网络非局域性，揭示了测量几何作为网络非局域性的独立资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络可以展现经典独立源模型无法解释的非经典关联。虽然纠缠的作用已被充分理解，但测量设计对网络非局域性的影响尚未被深入探索。

Method: 开发了一个操作框架，使用辅助量子比特的测量装置（ancilla-assisted meters）来评估双局域和全网络非局域性目击者所需的非局域可观测量，并在ibm_kingston 156超导量子处理器上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验观测到双局域非局域性指标S_BLHV=1.067(6)>1；全网络非局域性目击者达到认证阈值的99%和96%；Bob的联合测量决定了可达到的网络非局域性水平，不同测量设置分别优化双局域和全网络认证，且即使对于最大纠缠态，这两种违背也可能消失。

Conclusion: 测量几何是网络非局域性的独立资源，该框架为在可编程量子处理器上认证网络非局域性提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Quantum networks can exhibit nonclassical correlations that cannot be explained by classical models with independent sources. While the role of entanglement is well understood, the impact of measurement design remains largely unexplored. Here we develop an operational framework for certifying network nonlocality in the bilocal Alice--Bob--Charlie network using ancilla-assisted meters to evaluate the nonlocal observables required for bilocal and fully network nonlocal (FNN) witnesses. The approach successfully reproduces both bilocal and FNN correlations in simulation. On the 156-qubit superconducting processor \textit{ibm\_kingston}, we observe bilocal nonlocality with $\mathcal{S}_{\rm BLHV}=1.067(6)>1$ after readout-error mitigation, while the FNN witnesses reach $99\%$ and $96\%$ of their certification thresholds, implying the substantially stronger requirements for FNN certification. We further show that Bob's joint measurement determines the accessible level of network nonlocality: bilocal and FNN certification are optimized by different measurement settings, while both violations can disappear even for maximally entangled states. These results identify measurement geometry as an independent resource for network nonlocality and provide a practical route toward its certification on programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [146] [Estimation of a sparse multi-qubit Hamiltonian via compressed sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04669)
*Juntao Tu,Yuanlong Wang,Shuming Cheng,Shuixin Xiao,Zhibo Hou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于压缩感知的多量子比特哈密顿量估计方法，通过"尺度变换"技术解决高维系统中RIP条件失效问题，实现仅用O(k log(4^N/k))个方程估计k-稀疏哈密顿量，并在6和30量子比特系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N量子比特哈密顿量包含4^N-1个未知参数，需要指数级方程进行信息提取，而传统压缩感知方法在N≥3时因RIP条件失效而受限，亟需一种能在大规模量子系统中高效估计哈密顿量的方法。

Method: 提出"尺度变换"技术来恢复RIP条件，使得压缩感知方法适用于多量子比特系统；利用k-稀疏假设，仅需O(k log(4^N/k))个方程即可实现哈密顿量的压缩估计。

Result: 在6量子比特和30量子比特系统的数值实验中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的尺度变换技术有效解决了压缩感知在多量子比特哈密顿量估计中RIP条件失效的问题，提供了一种高效、可扩展的哈密顿量估计方案。

Abstract: Hamiltonian estimation is an effective approach in studying the structure and dynamical evolution of quantum systems. The difficulty in estimating the Hamiltonian is that an $N$-qubit Hamiltonian has $4^N-1$ unknown parameters, requiring exponentially many equations for information extraction. In this paper we develop a method based on compressed sensing to estimate the Hamiltonian of a multi-qubit system. We identify a problem where as $N$ increases, the common sufficient condition (Restricted Isometry Property) for compressed sensing often fails, obstructing the application of compressed sensing in ($N\geq 3$)-qubit Hamiltonian estimation. To solve this problem, we propose a ``scale transformation" technique to restore RIP and ensure a compressive estimation of a $k$-sparse Hamiltonian using only $O(k\log(4^N/k))$ equations. In the numerical examples, we estimate the Hamiltonians of two 6- and 30-qubit systems, demonstrating the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [147] [A Path-Superposition Framework for Quantum Gate Teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04672)
*Santiago Ávila,Marco Enríquez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径叠加的确定性量子门隐形传态通用框架，通过预共享最大纠缠资源中的相位和路径相关的局域酉算子族来指定目标非局域操作，建立了保证确定性隐形传态的设计条件，并展示了CNOT和CZ门的实现方案以及光子学验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子门隐形传态使远距离节点能够实现非局域量子操作而无需物理传输量子比特，这对分布式量子计算至关重要。现有方案缺乏通用设计框架，作者希望建立统一的确定性隐形传态理论框架。

Method: 基于路径叠加原理，通过预共享最大纠缠资源的相位和路径依赖的局域酉算子族来编码目标非局域操作，建立保证测量后确定性隐形传态和局域校正操作的一般设计条件。

Result: 构建了CNOT门和CZ门的隐形传态协议，证明不同非局域操作可在同一协议架构中通过调整设计参数实现，并提出了基于空间路径和偏振自由度的光子学原理验证方案。

Conclusion: 路径叠加是一种实现量子门隐形传态和分布式量子信息处理的通用资源，该框架为分布式量子计算提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Quantum gate teleportation enables distant parties to implement nonlocal quantum operations without physically transferring the participating qubits, making it a promising primitive for distributed quantum computing. We introduce a general framework for deterministic quantum gate teleportation based on path superposition, in which the target nonlocal operation is specified through the phase of a preshared maximally entangled resource and a suitable family of path-dependent local unitary operators. The framework establishes general design conditions that guarantee deterministic teleportation after measurement of the control qubits and the application of local correction operations. As representative realizations, we construct teleportation protocols for controlled-NOT (CNOT) and controlled-Z (CZ) gates, demonstrating that different nonlocal operations can be implemented within the same protocol architecture through appropriate choices of the design parameters. We further outline a proof-of-concept photonic realization based on spatial-path and polarization degrees of freedom. The proposed framework identifies path superposition as a versatile resource for quantum gate teleportation and distributed quantum information processing.

</details>


### [148] [Mapping open quantum dynamics onto graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04721)
*Kyuho Kim,Dayeong Lee,Seungkyun Park,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park,Sunkyu Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的马尔可夫量子动力学图论框架，将开放量子动力学映射到两个唯一定义的图上，将量子主方程解释为算符值信号在图上的平均波动特征。将该框架应用于开放量子Rabi模型，展示了Fock态晶格的开放系统推广、耗散的图拓扑特征刻画以及弱到超强耦合转变的分类。基于这些表示，图剪枝揭示了开放量子动力学的骨架结构，并实现了优越的图神经网络学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放量子动力学研究虽然引入了系统-浴耦合的大量相互作用算符，但主要采用代数形式（仅含部分跳变算符）或统计意义上的普适储库假设，未能充分探索其底层的连接结构。作者希望填补这一空白，将图论与开放量子动力学系统地联系起来。

Method: 提出一个通用图论框架，将马尔可夫开放量子动力学映射到两个唯一定义的图上，使量子主方程被严格解释为算符值信号在图上的平均波动特征。以开放量子Rabi模型为例进行验证，并利用图剪枝提取动力学骨干结构，结合图神经网络进行高效学习。

Result: 成功实现了Fock态晶格的开放系统推广，刻画了耗散的图拓扑特征，并分类了弱到超强耦合转变。图剪枝方法提取了开放量子动力学的关键骨架，基于此的图神经网络学习表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将图论与开放量子动力学桥接起来，为高维复杂量子系统的高效数据驱动分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Graph-theoretic frameworks have been widely employed in quantum physics to address the high-dimensional complexity of quantum systems. Although open quantum dynamics incorporates system-bath coupling via numerous interacting operators, it has been formulated algebraically with a partial set of jump operators or statistically universal reservoirs, leaving the underlying connectivity structure largely unexplored. Here, we propose a universal graph-theoretic framework for Markovian quantum dynamics. The framework maps open quantum dynamics onto two uniquely defined graphs, where the quantum master equation is rigorously interpreted as the average wave characteristic of operator-valued signals across the graphs. Applying this framework to the open quantum Rabi model, we demonstrate an open-system generalization of Fock-state lattices, characterize graph-topological signatures of dissipation, and classify the weak-to-ultrastrong coupling transition. Building on these representations, graph pruning reveals the backbone of open quantum dynamics, which enables superior graph neural-network learning. Our results bridge graph theory and open quantum dynamics, achieving efficient data-driven analysis of high-dimensional complexity.

</details>


### [149] [Magnetic graphs for cavity quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04736)
*Sunkyu Yu,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于单原子腔量子电动力学（QED）的磁图模型，通过图连通性解释从弱耦合到深强耦合的量子动力学转变，并揭示了子图拓扑引起的相位阻挫是驱动这一转变的主要机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强光-物质耦合是腔QED的核心挑战，但随耦合强度增加，即使是最简单的两能级原子与量子化场相互作用也需要谨慎处理（如规范不变的量子拉比模型）。现有方法对超强耦合机制下的量子动力学缺乏直观的几何解释。

Method: 将广义QRM映射到Floquet边界条件下的复二部图，通过图连通性（断开非磁子图的代价）作为单一度量来捕捉从弱到深强耦合的转变，并利用子图拓扑导致的相位阻挫解释连通性转变机制。

Result: 该图模型成功捕获了跨超强耦合机制的量子动力学，揭示了子图拓扑诱导的相位阻挫是连通性转变的主要驱动力，并且该框架可扩展到多体系统。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了图论与腔QED，证明即使在最简单的单原子腔QED设置中也会出现高度复杂的图动力学，为理解超强耦合机制提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Strengthening light-matter coupling has become a central challenge in cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED), enabling ultrafast gate operations, qubit protection, and deterministic nonlinear optics. As the coupling increases, even the simplest configuration, a two-level atom interacting with a quantized field, requires careful treatment, as exemplified by the gauge-invariant quantum Rabi model (QRM). Here we propose a magnetic graph model for single-atom cavity QED, which enables the interpretation of quantum dynamics across the ultrastrong coupling regime through graph connectivity. We demonstrate that the generalized QRM maps onto a complex bipartite graph of identical sites under Floquet boundary conditions. This framework captures the crossover from weak to deep-strong coupling via a single metric: the cost of disconnecting a nonmagnetic subgraph. We examine the mechanism underlying this connectivity transition, establishing phase frustration induced by subgraph topology as the primary driver. Scalable to many-body systems, this approach bridges graph theory and cavity QED, revealing highly complex-graph dynamics even in the simplest setting.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum-Optical Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04742)
*Ruo Kun Cai,Zhi Jiao Deng,Chun Wang Wu,Ping Xing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量子光学中的Fock态晶格模型，通过将多能级Jaynes-Cummings系统映射为两个耦合到共同连续体的非均匀SSH链，实现了束缚态在连续谱中（BIC）的量子模拟，并提出了利用单个囚禁离子实验检测BIC光谱特征的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC在经典波系统中已被广泛实现和利用，但在量子光学背景下，波场本身被量子化为玻色子激发，此时BIC的类似物是什么以及如何提取其实验信号尚不清楚。

Method: 构建了一个驱动多能级Jaynes-Cummings系统作为量子光学模型，利用Fock态晶格（FSL）概念将其映射为两个半无限非均匀Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链耦合到共同连续体的扩展结构。通过两个拓扑零模的量子叠加形成BIC，并利用手性对称性算符的时间动力学傅里叶变换提取BIC的光谱特征。

Result: 两个SSH链的拓扑零模通过破坏性量子干涉与共同连续体完全解耦，在Fock态维度中形成完美局域于连续谱内的BIC。通过对手性对称性算符的时间演化进行傅里叶变换，可获得嵌入连续背景中的离散峰（BIC光谱特征）。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了BIC物理与量子光学，为在量子极限下利用这种奇异态开辟了路径，并提供了基于单个囚禁离子的高可行性实验方案。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) are counterintuitive localized states that lie within the continuum of extended states. While extensively realized and utilized in classical wave systems, it is still unclear what a close analog of BICs would be, and how to extract their experimental signature in quantum-optical settings -- where the wave field itself is quantized into bosonic excitations. Here, we present a paradigmatic quantum-optical model consisting of a driven multi-level Jaynes-Cummings (JC) system, featuring few quantum degrees of freedom yet capable of hosting a BIC. Using the concept of a Fock-state lattice (FSL), this model can be mapped to an extended structure comprising two semi-infinite inhomogeneous Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains coupled to a common continuum. An appropriate quantum superposition of two topological zero modes from the separate chains forms a BIC that remains perfectly localized in the Fock-state dimension within the continuum spectrum, due to complete decoupling from the common continuum via destructive quantum interference. We further develop a method to extract the spectroscopic signature of the BIC -- a discrete peak embedded in a continuous background -- by Fourier-transforming the time-dependent dynamics of the system's chiral-symmetry operator. A highly feasible experimental proposal using a single trapped ion is provided. Our work bridges BIC physics with quantum optics, opening a pathway to harnessing such exotic states at the quantum limit.

</details>


### [151] [HamQASBench: A Hamiltonian-Informed Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04845)
*Jiayang Niu,Akib Karim,Yan Wang,Jie Li,Ke Deng,Azadeh Alavi,Muhammad Usman,Yongli Ren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 HamQASBench，一个基于哈密顿量信息的量子架构搜索诊断基准，通过结构层级分类和后验关键结构提取，揭示了传统能量指标无法检测的五种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QAS 基准按分子身份或量子比特数组织实例，与哈密顿量结构无关，且仅依赖能量精度评估，无法检测过参数化等结构失效。

Method: 将 11 个分子按照泡利算子基、计算基表示和基态纠缠指纹划分为五个结构层级；设计后验关键结构提取过程，找出满足各层级需求的最小电路；采用每量子比特纠缠分析和成对态保真度补充能量评估。

Result: 对五种 QAS 方法在四个范式下进行基准测试，发现了五种传统指标不可见的失败模式：最小主义过参数化、简并下的本征态承诺、强关联系统中的表示瓶颈、拓扑诱导的路由失败、电路搜索空间增长的可扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: HamQASBench 通过哈密顿结构感知的分类和结构性诊断，揭示了 QAS 方法中隐藏的失败模式，为更可靠的量子架构搜索评估提供了新的基准框架。

Abstract: Quantum Architecture Search (QAS) automates the design of parameterized quantum circuits for variational quantum algorithms, yet existing benchmarks organize instances by molecular identity or qubit count -- criteria agnostic to Hamiltonian structure -- and rely solely on energy accuracy, which cannot detect structural failures such as over-parameterization on near-product ground states. We introduce HamQASBench, a Hamiltonian-informed diagnostic benchmark organizing 11 molecules into five structural tiers via fingerprints derived from the Pauli operator basis, computational basis representation, and ground-state entanglement. A post-hoc critical-structure extraction procedure identifies minimal circuits consistent with each tier's requirements, complementing energy-based evaluation with per-qubit entanglement analysis and pairwise state fidelity. Benchmarking five QAS methods across four paradigms reveals failure modes invisible to conventional metrics: over-parameterization in the minimalism regime, eigenstate commitment under degeneracy, a representation bottleneck in strongly correlated systems, topology-induced routing failure, and circuit search space growth as a scalability bottleneck.

</details>


### [152] [Emergence of the Scrooge Ensemble in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04864)
*Zeyu Liu,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用可解的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK)模型，通过路径积分方法解析地证明了投影系综在高维混沌多体系统中精确收敛到Scrooge系综，揭示了深层热化现象的统计机制，并建立了测量路径积分中鞍点结构与副本置换对称性之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量概率性为理解多体波函数的结构提供了窗口。当系统仅部分被测量时，剩余自由度形成后测量态系综，其统计结构可揭示比传统热化更强的"深层热化"现象。近期数值证据表明该现象表现为投影系综收敛到Scrooge系综（给定密度矩阵下最大随机性的系综），但其微观机制尚不明确。

Method: 利用可解的SYK模型，将测量概率和后测量态表述为路径积分形式，解析地表征投影系综的所有矩，并与Scrooge系综的矩进行精确对比。

Result: 解析证明了在SYK模型中，投影系综的所有矩与Scrooge系综的矩精确匹配，且即使在短演化时间下也成立。进一步揭示了测量路径积分的鞍点结构自然产生Scrooge统计所需的副本置换对称性。

Conclusion: 确立了可解SYK模型作为探索混沌多体动力学中量子测量普适统计特性的可处理框架，为深层热化现象提供了解析理解的途径。

Abstract: The probabilistic nature of quantum measurement provides a direct window into the structure and complexity of many-body wave functions. When only part of a system is measured, the remaining degrees of freedom form an ensemble of post-measurement states whose statistical structure can reveal a stronger form of thermalization, known as deep thermalization. Recent numerical evidence suggests that this phenomenon is characterized by convergence of the projected ensemble to the Scrooge ensemble, a maximally random ensemble compatible with a given density matrix. In this Letter, we use the solvable Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model to unveil the mechanism by which the Scrooge ensemble emerges in many-body systems. By formulating measurement probabilities and post-measurement states in terms of path integrals, we analytically characterize all moments of the projected ensemble and show that they exactly match those of the Scrooge ensemble, even at short evolution times. We further connect this result to the saddle-point structure of the measurement path integral, which naturally generates the replica permutations underlying Scrooge statistics. Our results establish the solvable SYK model as a tractable setting for exploring universal statistics of quantum measurements in chaotic many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [153] [Photonic Cluster State Generation from a Quantum Dot Emitting in the Telecom C-band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04896)
*Giora Peniakov,Reza Hekmati,Johannes Michl,Mohamed Helal,Moritz Meinecke,Jochen Kaupp,Yorick Reum,Martin Kamp,Monika Emmerling,Andreas Theo Pfenning,Sven Hoefling,Tobias Huber-Loyola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在电信C波段实现了光子团簇态的直接确定性生成，利用砷化铟量子点中空穴自旋的重复激发，过程保真度达0.71，光子不可区分性≥83%，为与硅光子学兼容的量子通信提供了关键进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前光子团簇态的确定性生成局限于近红外波段，为实现量子通信优势并兼容硅光子学，需要在电信波段实现该功能，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用外磁场下砷化铟量子点中空穴自旋的重复激发，在电信C波段直接生成确定性团簇态；通过过程映射测量进行表征，并分析自旋-光子偏振纠缠及光子不可区分性。

Result: 团簇态生成过程保真度F=0.71±0.01，自旋-光子偏振纠缠负性N=0.27±0.02，光子不可区分性≥83%。

Conclusion: 证明了在电信波段实现确定性团簇态生成的可行性，高光子不可区分性表明其具备实现超越线性连接的光子团簇态融合门的潜力。

Abstract: Photonic cluster states are a key resource for photonic quantum information processing. So far, deterministic generation of these states has been limited to the near-infrared wavelength range. To achieve quantum advantage in communication while maintaining compatibility with silicon photonics, operation in the telecom wavelength range is required. In this work, we demonstrate deterministic cluster state generation directly in the telecom C-band. This is achieved through repetitive excitation of a hole spin confined in an indium-arsenide quantum dot subjected to an external magnetic field. We characterize the quantum process that generates the cluster state by measuring its process map, obtaining a fidelity of $\mathrm{F} = 0.71 \pm 0.01$ to the ideal case. As part of this characterization, we observe spin--photon polarization entanglement with a negativity of $\mathrm{N} = 0.27 \pm 0.02$. The emitted photons exhibit indistinguishability of at least 83%, demonstrating the potential for future fusion gates necessary for photonic cluster state generation beyond linear connectivity.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum orientation, Noether structure, composition of systems and operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04899)
*Heinz-Jürgen Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了除量子理论的统计结构外，还需要一种称为“诺特定理结构”的额外结构来描述系统组合和定义完全正操作，该结构体现了厄米算符作为可观测量与对称变换生成元的双重角色，并在有限维情况下建立了与完全正操作的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论中仅靠统计结构不足以完整描述系统组合和完全正操作的定义，需要引入诺特定理结构来体现厄米算符的双重角色。

Method: 在有限维情况下，借鉴Alfsen和Shultz关于Jordan乘积可扩展为算子代数结合乘积的条件的研究，通过诺特定理结构建立与完全正操作的联系，并将纯操作刻画为保定向映射。

Result: 建立了诺特定理结构与完全正操作之间的关联，指出纯操作可被表征为保定向映射。

Conclusion: 诺特定理结构是描述量子系统组合和定义完全正操作所必需的额外结构，在有限维情形下与完全正操作存在明确联系。

Abstract: In this paper we argue that, in addition to the statistical structure of quantum theory, another structure, referred to here as the ``Noether structure," is necessary to describe the composition of systems and to define completely positive operations. A Noether structure reflects the dual role of Hermitian operators as observables on the one hand and as generators of symmetry transformations on the other. This idea has been expressed in a similar form in the works of Alfsen and Shultz, who investigated the conditions under which the Jordan product can be extended to an associative product of operator algebras. Our investigations into the Noether structure and the composition of systems are limited to the finite-dimensional case and establish a connection to completely positive operations. In the case of pure operations, the latter can be characterized as orientation-preserving maps.

</details>


### [155] [Error Mitigation in Bosonic Systems via Virtual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04914)
*Leonardo Finocchiaro,Marco Robbio,Diogo Gomes,David Gunn,Adithi Udupa,Axel M. Eriksson,Leonardo Novo,Giulia Ferrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了玻色子量子信息处理中虚拟蒸馏的框架，利用无源线性光学干涉仪实现循环移位算符的对角化，推导了针对数算符、相移算符和任意正交算符的噪声缓解期望值估计协议，并应用于光子损耗和退相位噪声的抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟蒸馏最初针对量子比特系统开发，尚未在玻色子连续变量量子计算领域得到系统性建立，而玻色子系统面临光子损耗和退相位等独特噪声机制，需要相应的误差缓解方法。

Method: 基于无源线性光学干涉仪实现循环移位算符的对角化，通过多副本测量构建虚拟蒸馏期望值的实验可行协议；利用光子数分布的特征函数估计任意阶的虚拟蒸馏关联函数。

Result: 推导了数算符、相移算符和任意正交算符的噪声缓解期望值估计方法；展示了光子损耗和退相位噪声贡献的抑制效果。

Conclusion: 将虚拟蒸馏从量子比特系统扩展至玻色子量子信息处理领域，为玻色子量子处理器中利用现有线性光学资源实现误差缓解测量提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Virtual distillation is a promising error-mitigation technique that exploits multiple copies of a noisy quantum state to estimate observables as if measured on a purified state. Although originally introduced in the context of bosonic many-body systems under the name of virtual cooling, its development and applications have largely focused on qubit-based quantum computation. Here, we establish a framework for virtual distillation in bosonic quantum information processing and continuous-variable quantum computing. Building on a diagonalization of cyclic shift operators implemented with passive linear-optical interferometers, we derive experimentally accessible protocols for estimating virtually distilled expectation values of observables relevant to bosonic architectures. In particular, we show how to recover noise-mitigated expectation values of number operators, phase-shift operators, and arbitrary quadratures from multi-copy measurements. For number operators, we further demonstrate the estimation of virtually distilled correlators of arbitrary order through the characteristic function of the photon-number distribution. We apply the framework to states affected by photon loss and dephasing, two of the dominant noise mechanisms in bosonic quantum computation, and quantify the resulting suppression of noise contributions. Our results extend virtual distillation beyond its original setting and provide a practical route toward error-mitigated measurements in bosonic quantum processors using experimentally available linear-optical resources.

</details>


### [156] [How Hard Is Quantum Advantage? A Cloud Microphysics Stress Test for Variational Quantum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04915)
*Felix Herbort,Ellen Sarauer,Daniel Ohl de Mello,Paul Christiansen,Steffen Hien,Cedric Brügmann,Dieter Jaksch,Veronika Eyring,Martin Kiffner,Mierk Schwabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了混合量子神经网络（QNN）在云微物理数据集上的应用，通过优化频率谱和经典后处理提升性能，但QNN仍被简单全连接神经网络超越，表明需进一步改进变分量子模型以填补经典模型的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习虽具潜在优势，但在实际改进和可扩展性方面缺乏有力证据，且存在贫瘠高原等问题。本研究旨在探索QNN在复杂物理系统（云微物理）中的可行性，并识别其性能瓶颈。

Method: 采用混合量子神经网络（QNN），结合可训练的丰富频率谱和增强表达性的经典后处理，并对QNN进行广泛的超参数优化，应用于包含水相变及温度变化过程的云微物理数据集。

Result: QNN通过超参数优化展现出对复杂物理系统应用的可行性，但在性能上被简单的全连接神经网络所超越。

Conclusion: 当前变分量子模型在机器学习中存在瓶颈，需进一步理解和改进，以期在未来填补经典模型失效或低效的领域。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) could have the potential to leverage advantages of quantum over classical computing but still lacks strong evidence of actual improvements and scalability, partly due to phenomena such as barren plateaus. In this paper, we employ a hybrid quantum neural network (QNN) on a dataset on cloud microphysics, containing processes for phase transitions of water in the atmosphere and its related temperature changes, which are highly relevant for accurate climate predictions and projections. To reach optimal performance of our QNNs, we employ a rich and trainable frequency spectrum together with expressivity enhancing classical postprocessing. We find that our QNNs strongly benefit from extensive hyperparameter optimization and thereby demonstrate the feasibility of applying QNNs to complex physical systems. At the same time, the QNNs are outperformed by classical baselines in the form of simple fully-connected neural networks. We discuss identified bottlenecks of this class of quantum models to learn the full complexity of the cloud microphysics dataset to show that there is a need to further understand and improve variational quantum models for machine learning such that they might fill the gap where classical models fail or are inefficient.

</details>


### [157] [Noise-limited secret key agreement with twin optical physically unclonable functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04936)
*Georgios M. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于相关光学物理不可克隆函数（PUFs）生成的双光学生理指纹，用于硬件支持的密钥生成与分发。通过信息论密钥协商协议（含安全草图、错误协商和隐私放大），分析了在制造变异性与环境噪声下两个诚实方建立共享密钥的可行性、信息泄露及密钥长度下界，并探讨了将其集成到QKD网络中作为初始预共享密钥生成机制的前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用相关光学PUF产生的双光学生理指纹作为硬件安全平台，在无需计算假设或可信第三方的前提下，实现两个诚实方之间的密码密钥生成与分发。

Method: 采用信息论密钥协商协议框架，结合安全草图（secure sketches）、错误协商（error reconciliation）和隐私放大（privacy amplification）；量化公开辅助数据的信息泄露；推导最终密钥长度的下界；分析噪声区间对密钥协商可行性的影响；评估实用型与近容量协商方案的性能。

Result: 确定了密钥协商可行的噪声区间；给出了密钥长度的信息论下界；比较了不同错误协商方案在光学PUF场景下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 双光学PUF能够在不依赖计算安全假设的情况下实现安全的密钥协商，并可作为量子密钥分发（QKD）网络中初始预共享密钥的实用生成机制，增强网络的安全性基础。

Abstract: We investigate the use of twin optical fingerprints derived from correlated physical unclonable functions (PUFs), as a hardware-based platform for cryptographic key generation and distribution. Each fingerprint is associated with a random, yet reproducible speckle pattern, generated when coherent light is scattered by a disordered optical structure. We consider a pair of correlated optical PUFs, and study the conditions under which two honest parties can establish a common secret key, despite fabrication-induced variability and environmental noise. An explicit information-theoretic key-agreement protocol is developed, incorporating secure sketches, error reconciliation, and privacy amplification. We quantify information leakage due to public helper data, and derive lower bounds on the length of the final secret key. The analysis identifies the noise regimes in which secure key agreement is feasible, and examines the performance of both practical and near-capacity reconciliation schemes. Finally, we discuss how twin optical PUFs could be integrated into quantum key distribution (QKD) networks, as a mechanism for establishing an initial pre-shared secret key between two honest users, without relying on computational assumptions or trusted third parties.

</details>


### [158] [Contraction and Expansion Values of Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04950)
*Ruben Ibarrondo,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了收缩值和扩张值两个单调序列，它们以奇异值细化算子范数的方式细化了量子信道的收缩系数，通过无迹厄米算子子空间上的极小极大变分原理定义，具有操作解释，并与Gel'fand或Bernstein数等价，能更好地描述信道复合下的收缩累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子信息的迹距离收缩系数作为极值比，只刻画信道最乐观的行为，即使对非常嘈杂的信道也常常是平凡的，且单个标量不适合描述信道复合下收缩的累积效应。

Method: 通过无迹厄米算子子空间上的极小极大变分原理定义收缩值和扩张值两个单调序列；将其与Pietsch的s-数理论中的Gel'fand数或Bernstein数建立联系；通过两个态区分游戏给出操作解释。

Result: 建立了这些序列的主要结构性质；对单量子比特信道、d维振幅阻尼信道和直和信道进行了计算或估计；证明了它们在信道复合下能提供收缩系数单独无法提供的界。

Conclusion: 收缩值和扩张值序列是对传统收缩系数的有力细化，能更精细地刻画量子信道在复合下的行为，具有坚实的数学基础（s-数理论）和物理操作意义。

Abstract: The contraction coefficient of the trace distance is a central tool in quantum information, quantifying how strongly a quantum channel degrades the distinguishability of states. However, being an extremal ratio, it captures only the most optimistic behaviour of the channel and is often trivial, even for very noisy channels. Moreover, a single scalar is poorly suited to describe how contraction accumulates under channel composition. In this work we introduce the \emph{contraction and expansion values}, two monotone sequences that refine the contraction and expansion coefficients in the same way singular values refine the operator norm. They arise from a min--max variational principle over subspaces of traceless Hermitian operators, admit an operational interpretation in terms of two state-discrimination games, and are shown to coincide with the Gel'fand or Bernstein numbers of the channel restricted to traceless operators. This identification places the sequences within Pietsch's theory of $s$-numbers and yields, in particular, bounds under channel composition that the contraction coefficient alone cannot provide. We establish their main structural properties and compute or estimate them for single-qubit channels, $d$-dimensional amplitude damping channels, and direct-sum channels.

</details>


### [159] [Extending the Bloch sphere model to an N-qubit system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04979)
*Francisco Piñero,Cristian Franco,Hernán I. de la Cruz,Fernando L. Pelayo,Vicente Pascual,Mauro Mezzini,Jose Javier Paulet,Fernando Cuartero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种将Bloch球表示推广到任意N量子比特系统的几何模型，使用2^N-1个球体，并证明了与标准态矢表示的双射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch球是表示单量子比特状态的优雅工具，但缺乏被广泛接受的多量子比特系统（含纠缠）的推广模型。

Method: 利用2^N-1个球体表示N量子比特系统；对纯2量子比特态使用三个球体（两个单量子比特球+一个纠缠球）；通过沿Z轴和Y轴的控制旋转门层次化应用实现N量子比特参数化。

Result: 严格证明了该模型的角参数与标准态矢表示之间存在双射关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为多体纠缠提供了直观的可视化方法，可能为量子模拟器带来计算优势，并为量子门分析提供新视角。

Abstract: The Bloch sphere is an elegant tool for representing single-qubit states. However, a widely accepted generalization for multi-qubit systems with entanglement remains absent. We propose a novel geometric model extending the Bloch sphere representation to arbitrary $N$-qubit systems using $2^N-1$ spheres. We demonstrate that any pure 2-qubit state is uniquely described by three spheres: two for individual qubits and a third encapsulating bipartite entanglement. Generalizing this, we establish an $N$-qubit parameterization through the hierarchical application of controlled rotation gates along the $Z$ and $Y$ axes. We formally prove a strict bijection between the standard state vector representation and our model's angular parameters. This framework provides an intuitive visualization of multiple entanglement, offering potential computational advantages for quantum simulators and new analytical perspectives on quantum gates.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Hashing via Constrained Rydberg Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04991)
*Han-Chao Chen,Xin Liu,Zheng-Yuan Zhang,Dong-Sheng Ding,Bao-Sen Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了里德伯原子阵列中的约束多体动力学自然产生量子哈希机制，通过将三元串编码为态空间中的确定性轨迹，实现了从经典信息空间到希尔伯特空间量子态系综的映射，并展现了近正交性、随机分布和广泛几何覆盖等统计特性，从而自然具备低碰撞概率、单向性、篡改敏感性和隐私保护等密码学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子哈希的密码学功能是否可以自然地从约束多体动力学中涌现，而非依赖精心设计的算法，从而将量子动力学本身识别为密码学信息处理的物理资源。

Method: 利用里德伯原子阵列中的约束多体动力学，将三元字符串编码为态空间中的确定性轨迹，实现经典信息空间到希尔伯特空间量子态系综的映射；通过统计分析研究系综的几何特性（近正交性、随机分布、几何覆盖）。

Result: 该量子态系综以高概率展现出近正交性、类随机分布和广泛的几何覆盖，这些几何特征自然衍生出量子哈希的核心密码学属性：低碰撞概率、单向性、篡改敏感性和隐私保护。

Conclusion: 量子哈希的密码学功能可以从约束多体动力学中自然涌现，无需依赖刻意设计的算法，量子动力学本身可作为密码学信息处理的物理资源。

Abstract: In this Letter, we show that constrained many-body dynamics in Rydberg atom arrays naturally gives rise to a quantum hashing mechanism. By encoding ternary strings into deterministic trajectories in the state space, the classical information space is mapped onto a quantum state ensemble in the Hilbert space with an induced geometric structure. Statistical analysis reveals that this ensemble exhibits high probability near-orthogonality, random-like distribution, and broad geometric coverage. These geometric features naturally give rise to the essential cryptographic properties of quantum hashing, including low collision probability, one-wayness, tamper sensitivity, and privacy preservation. Our results demonstrate that the cryptographic functionality of quantum hashing need not rely on deliberately engineered algorithms, but can instead emerge naturally from constrained many-body dynamics, identifying quantum dynamics itself as a physical resource for cryptographic information processing.

</details>


### [161] [Transmon Phase Gates Controlled by Superconducting Soliton DAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05072)
*Derek Reitz,Tony X. Zhou,Aditya Sharma,Ryan Bilotta,John McFarland,Aref Fouladi,Jacob Glasby,Aruna Ramanayaka,Zachary Stegen,Aaron Pesetski,Mark Covington,Gregory Boyd,Jeremy Clark*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超导数字-模拟转换器（DAC），通过将梯形驱动脉冲转换为超导孤子并存储在负载环路中，实现对超导量子比特或门耦合器的磁通控制，从而在纳秒级完成高保真度量子门操作。实验展示了5.6 ns的S门操作，门诱导激发概率0.05%，并讨论了多芯片扩展中非局域电磁干扰导致的相移误差（1.6%/门）及缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超导量子计算中，控制信号噪声、多路复用能力不足以及CMOS兼容性等问题限制了量子门保真度和系统可扩展性，亟需一种既能滤波噪声、提供原生多路复用、又能与CMOS兼容的高速高保真控制方案。

Method: 将CMOS控制的梯形驱动脉冲通过超导电路转换为孤子，孤子在DAC负载环路中保持并产生可调磁通，耦合到超导量子比特或门耦合器。通过改变孤子保持时间或利用DC偏置可调耦合器控制模拟磁通输出，实现固定时间高保真度量子门。实验中采用transmon量子比特演示S门，并使用IRB序列评估多比特扩展性能。

Result: 成功实现5.6 ns的S门操作；DAC诱导的量子态激发概率为0.05%；DAC诱导的弛豫率低于transmon的固有T1极限；量子模拟表明过阻尼JJ可进一步降低激发率10倍；多比特扩展中观察到非局域DAC诱导相移误差1.6%/门。

Conclusion: 该超导DAC方案在单比特层面展示了高速低错误率的量子门控制潜力，但多芯片集成时需解决非局域电磁干扰引起的相移问题。未来可通过优化芯片布局、引入DC可调耦合器进行相位校正等策略来缓解EMI影响。

Abstract: We introduce a superconducting digital-to-analog converter (DAC) that filters control noise, provides native multiplexing, performs quantum gates in nanoseconds, and can be controlled by CMOS. This is achieved by transducing a trapezoidal drive pulse into a superconducting soliton, which is then held in the DAC load loop, applying flux to a mutually-coupled superconducting qubit or gate coupler. The analog flux output by the DAC can be easily controlled by varying the soliton hold time, or with a DC-biased tunable DAC-qubit coupler, allowing the DAC to perform a fixed-time, high-fidelity gate that's robust to fabrication variance or flux offsets in the quantum circuit. Our initial demonstration shows that the DAC can successfully perform 5.6 ns S-gates on transmons. We measure the DAC-induced quantum state excitation probability per gate to be 0.05%, and find that the DAC-induced relaxation rate from the qubit 1 state is below the intrinsic T1 rate limit of the transmon. Quantum simulations show qualitative agreement with the measured data, and predict that the DAC excitation rate can be lowered 10 times further by overdamping the Josephson junction (JJ) in the DAC load loop. may be limited by a Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking (IRB) sequences on an observer qubit reveal that, when scaling to many qubits, the DAC's performance may be limited by a non-local, DAC-induced phase error of 1.6% per gate, appearing in ancilla qubits that are not directly coupled to any of the 30 DACs on the chip. We discuss strategies for future layouts of multi-DAC chips that focus on mitigating the source of these non-local, high-frequency electromagnetic interactions (EMI), and how to incorporate a DC-tunable coupler for phase correction.

</details>


### [162] [Complementary 3D color codes for transversal quantum logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05107)
*Friederike Butt,Luis Colmenarez,Erik Weilandt,Tom Peham,Robert Wille,Markus Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于四面体三维色码及其Hadamard变换码（H-四面体码）的混合架构，利用互补的横向非Clifford操作和位向Hadamard变换实现几乎通用的横向逻辑门集，并通过容错轮转构造完成通用门集，实现了魔法态和纠缠的横向创建，将资源开销集中在少量非横向Clifford纠缠操作上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eastin-Knill定理禁止单一量子纠错码拥有通用的横向逻辑门集，因此需要探索混合编码架构来实现容错量子计算中的通用门集，同时尽可能保持操作的横向性以降低开销。

Method: 1. 提出四面体三维色码及其Hadamard变换码（H-四面体码），两者支持互补的横向非Clifford操作；2. 利用位向Hadamard变换在两种编码间切换；3. 实现从四面体码到H-四面体码的单向横向逻辑CNOT；4. 通过容错轮转（pieceably fault-tolerant round-robin）构造H-四面体码间的逻辑controlled-Z门；5. 使用逻辑二维色码辅助量子比特进行Steane型症候提取以降低开销。

Result: 实现了几乎通用的横向逻辑门集，能够横向创建纠缠和魔法态；通过少量非横向Clifford纠缠操作完成通用门集，将资源开销集中于此。

Conclusion: 该架构为实现经典难模拟的量子算法提供了新路径，其中魔法操作和大部分纠缠操作保持横向性，资源开销显著降低。

Abstract: Transversal logical gates provide a direct route to fault-tolerant quantum computation, but the Eastin-Knill theorem forbids a universal transversal gate set within a single quantum error-correcting code. We propose a hybrid architecture based on the tetrahedral three-dimensional color code and its Hadamard-transformed counterpart, which we call the H-tetrahedral code. The two encodings support complementary transversal non-Clifford operations. Combined with bitwise Hadamard transformations that switch between the two encodings and a one-way transversal logical CNOT from the tetrahedral code to the H-tetrahedral code, these operations realize an almost-universal transversal logical gate set that enables both the creation of entanglement and logical states with magic. We complete a universal gate set through a pieceably fault-tolerant round-robin construction of a logical controlled-$Z$ gate between two H-tetrahedral codes. This logical entangling gate is interleaved with reduced-overhead Steane-type syndrome extraction using logical two-dimensional color-code auxiliary qubits. Our construction provides a new route toward implementing classically hard-to-simulate quantum algorithms where magic and most entangling operations are transversal while the resource overhead is concentrated in a small number of non-transversal Clifford entangling operations.

</details>


### [163] [Characterisation of a satellite-to-ground channel for continuous variable quantum key distribution protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05109)
*Emma Tien Hwai Medlock,Vinod N. Roa,Timothy Spiller,Rupesh Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了星基连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD）中动态量子信道的损耗特性，分析了随天顶角变化的信道损耗对参数估计和散粒噪声的影响，并在实际假设下对不同场景、湍流强度和波长进行了信道损耗表征，结果表明在受限Eve安全假设下可以生成正安全密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星基QKD协议中量子信道具有动态特性，信道损耗随天顶角变化，这对CV-QKD的参数估计和传输本地振荡器的散粒噪声造成问题，因此需要表征信道损耗及其来源。

Method: 在实际假设下对动态信道损耗进行表征，考虑不同场景、湍流强度和波长条件进行分析。

Result: 对于所考虑的信道参数，在受限Eve安全假设下可以生成正安全密钥。

Conclusion: 动态信道损耗的准确表征对于星基CV-QKD的实际部署至关重要，且在特定安全假设下可实现正安全密钥生成。

Abstract: In space based quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols, the quantum channel will be dynamic in nature and the channel loss will change with respect to the zenith angle. In the context of continuous variable (CV)-QKD, this will cause issues with parameter estimation and for a transmitted local oscillator in particular it will also fluctuate the shot noise. Therefore, it is vital to characterise this channel loss and the sources of this loss. In this paper the varying channel loss is characterised under practical assumptions. This is shown for various different scenarios, turbulence strengths, as well as wavelengths. This work shows, for the channel parameters considered, it is possible to generate a positive secret key if restricted Eve security assumptions are made.

</details>


### [164] [Quasi-holonomy in non-adiabatic quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05218)
*Erik Sjöqvist,Adam Fredriksson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了非绝热量子演化中子空间准和乐（quasi-holonomy）的理论框架，通过将薛定谔演化分解为动力学部分和联络诱导部分，得到了一个依赖于动力学传播子的有效几何生成元。该准联络不构成原始Grassmann丛上的真联络，因为其规范变换律包含历史依赖的非局域项。文章指出，所有分解方案都面临类似的历史依赖问题，因此子空间的几何与动力学效应通常是纠缠的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从规范理论视角澄清量子子空间演化中几何部分（和乐）与动力学部分的分离问题，解决非绝热情形下传统几何相框架的局限性。

Method: 在运动基中将薛定谔演化分解为动力学传播子贡献和联络诱导贡献，定义有效几何生成元（准联络）；分析其规范变换性质并与其他分解方案进行对比。

Result: 准联络的规范变换律包含历史依赖的非局域项，表明其不构成Grassmann丛上的真联络；所有分解方案均无法彻底分离几何与动力学效应。

Conclusion: 非绝热子空间演化中几何与动力学效应本质上是纠缠的，无法像绝热情形那样实现干净的分离，这对于量子计算中的几何门实现具有重要启示。

Abstract: We develop a framework for quasi-holonomy in non-adiabatic quantum time evolution of subspaces along loops in a complex Grassmannian. By factoring the Schrödinger evolution into dynamical and connection-induced contributions in a moving basis, we obtain an effective geometric generator that depends explicitly on the dynamical propagator. This quasi-connection does not define a genuine connection on the original Grassmann bundle, since its gauge transformation law acquires a history-dependent, nonlocal term. Other ways of factoring the Schrödinger evolution are briefly discussed. All these approaches suffer from the same type of history-dependence, thereby defining transport of subspaces in which geometric and dynamical effects are generally intertwined, just as in the case of the quasi-holonomy. Our work sheds light on the issue of separating quantum evolution of subspaces into holonomic and dynamical parts from an essentially gauge-theoretic perspective.

</details>


### [165] [Routing Anonymity and Identifiability of Noisy Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05281)
*Ben Priestley,Mina Doosti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了量子云计算中后端可识别性的形式化框架，定义了路由匿名性安全概念，证明后端识别是一个假设检验问题，建立了效用-匿名性权衡，并通过理论分析和Amazon Braket实验验证了量子后端指纹的存在性及其隐私含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子云计算中，用户提交电路到服务商专有后端硬件，但含噪有限样本输出会携带后端特定指纹信息，可能泄露后端身份。此前相关工作主要从基准测试角度研究，缺乏对用户和提供者隐私考虑的系统性研究。

Method: 形式化定义后端可识别性博弈和路由匿名性安全概念；将后端识别建模为假设检验问题，利用Chernoff速率证明被动i.i.d.访问下路由匿名性指数衰减；利用Pauli转移矩阵工具建立深度原理；在Amazon Braket上使用离子阱和超导量子处理器进行实验验证。

Result: 超导量子后端间分类准确率87-90%，跨物理平台分类准确率96-100%，且可识别性能在自然后处理下仍能保持。

Conclusion: 路由匿名性是量子云计算中独立的安全需求，本文提供了量化和控制效用-匿名性权衡的框架。

Abstract: Present-day quantum computing is cloud-based, where a user submits a circuit to a service provider's proprietary backend hardware. While providers may wish to hide implementation details, scheduling choices, or even which physical device was used, noisy finite-shot outputs can carry backend-specific fingerprints: information imprinted in the classical output distribution that can reveal the backend identity. So far, such fingerprints have mostly been studied from a benchmarking perspective, with limited attention to privacy considerations for users and providers. This work develops the first formal framework for backend identifiability and its privacy implications. We introduce a backend-identifiability game and use it to formalise routing anonymity as a security notion for quantum cloud services. We show that backend identifiability is a hypothesis-testing problem and prove that, under passive i.i.d. access to a single backend, routing anonymity decays exponentially at the Chernoff rate. We also establish a utility-anonymity trade-off, imposing fundamental limits on how much backend-specific information can be removed from classical outputs without degrading their usefulness. In addition, we observe that, for noisy quantum hardware, identifying fingerprints are inherently an intermediate-depth phenomenon, and establish a depth principle using Pauli-transfer-matrix tools. We complement the theory with experiments on Amazon Braket on AWS, using ion-trap and superconducting quantum processors. We observe 87-90% classification between superconducting backends and 96-100% classification across physical platforms, and find that identifiability can survive natural forms of post-processing. Overall, these results establish routing anonymity as a distinct security requirement for quantum cloud computing, and provide a framework for quantifying and controlling the utility-anonymity trade-off.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05307)
*Yewei Yuan,Michele Minervini,Mark M. Wilde,Nana Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子谱异常检测（QSPADE），通过平均态的谱直接计算类似PCA的异常分数，用平滑温度控制谱阈值替代硬PCA秩选择，在零温极限恢复硬投影PCA分数。该方法样本复杂度与数据维度无关，数值模拟表明其行为类似核PCA，可检测横场伊辛相变而不需预定义序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子异常检测中，经典PCA因量子数据缺乏标准中心化、显式恢复主特征向量或构建全Gram矩阵成本过高而效率低下，需要更高效的量子异常分数计算方法。

Method: 提出QSPADE框架，从正常数据集的平均态谱直接计算PCA-like异常分数，用温度控制的平滑谱阈值代替硬PCA秩选择，使近阈值谱分量部分贡献于异常分数。

Result: 零温极限恢复硬投影PCA分数；测量为基础的量子检测器样本复杂度与数据维度无关；数值模拟显示行为类似核PCA，可检测横场伊辛相变而不需预定义序参量。

Conclusion: QSPADE为编码经典数据的量子核异常检测和未知诊断可观测量下的量子原生系统监测提供了高效框架。

Abstract: A core task in quantum anomaly detection is to compute an anomaly score that quantifies how strongly a test quantum state deviates from a given quantum dataset assumed to be normal. Classically, principal component analysis (PCA) for centered data computes the anomaly score by evaluating the test sample relative to the subspace spanned by the selected leading eigenvectors. However, for quantum data that lack a standard centering, explicitly recovering principal eigenvectors, constructing full Gram matrices, or loading quantum-random-access-memory-style data can be more costly than estimating the anomaly score itself. To avoid these costs, we propose Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection (QSPADE), which computes PCA-like anomaly scores directly from the spectrum of the average state of the normal dataset. By replacing hard PCA rank selection with a smooth, temperature-controlled spectral threshold, QSPADE makes near-threshold spectral components contribute partially to the anomaly score. This makes the score vary continuously rather than jump when a borderline component is included or excluded, and makes it less sensitive to noise or arbitrary hard cutoffs near the threshold. In the zero-temperature limit, QSPADE recovers the hard-projector PCA score. The proposed measurement-based quantum detector can be calibrated with a sample complexity independent of the data dimension. Numerical simulations show that QSPADE behaves like kernel-PCA on encoded classical data and detects changes across a transverse-field Ising transition without predefined order parameters. Consequently, QSPADE gives an efficient framework for both quantum-kernel anomaly detection on encoded classical data and the monitoring of quantum-native systems where diagnostic observables are unknown.

</details>


### [167] [Coherent Control of Energy Transport at Room Temperature in a Noisy Bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05361)
*Davinder Singh,Paul Brumer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在非平衡稳态下，通过与反应中心相连的供体-受体对中的相干控制实现能量传输调控。通过相位控制的相干场激发，诱导激发路径间的干涉，从而实现对能量通量的相位依赖性调制，可在室温耗散环境下实现类似光学能量开关的相干控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子相干控制从传统的低耗散瞬态领域扩展到存在噪声的非平衡稳态（NESS）室温环境中，探索在耗散体系中维持干涉效应的可能性。

Method: 构建反应中心连接的供体-受体对模型，体系连续与非相干辐射和声子浴相互作用，同时用相位控制的相干场驱动，利用激发路径间的量子干涉实现能量通量的相位调制。

Result: 相干激发供体-受体对可在激发路径间诱导干涉，产生相位依赖的能量通量调制，从而通过干涉增强或抑制能量转移。

Conclusion: 在耗散体系的NESS中仍能保持干涉效应，实现了室温噪声环境下的相干控制，拓展了量子控制的操作范围，可用于构建光学能量开关等应用。

Abstract: Coherent control of energy transport in a non-equilibrium steady-state (NESS) in a reaction-center-connected donor-acceptor pair is proposed. The pigments are considered to be continuously interacting with incoherent radiation and a phonon bath while being driven by phase-controlled coherent fields. Coherent excitation of the donor-acceptor pair is shown to induce interference between excitation pathways, resulting in phase dependent modulation of the flux. As a consequence one can enhance or suppress energy transfer via interference, e.g. an optical energy switch. The persistence of such interference enables coherent control at a NESS in dissipative regime suggests an extension of the operational scope of quantum control from traditional transient domain with low dissiaption to noisy environment NESS at room temperature.

</details>


### [168] [Logical Spectroscopy: Lifted-Product Codes with Addressable Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05386)
*Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"逻辑光谱学"方法，利用中国剩余定理将阿贝尔提升积码的群代数分解为Frobenius特征包，在每个包中进行有限域计算来构造完整的可寻址共轭逻辑基，从而赋予这类量子LDPC码类似超图积码的结构透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图积码具有透明的逻辑算符结构，因其输入矩阵可在二元域上行化简；而阿贝尔提升积码的种子项是群代数环中的移位（而非域元素），导致主元块未必可逆，全局行约化失效，因此需要新的方法来显式构造共轭逻辑算符。

Method: 利用中国剩余定理（CRT）将群代数分解为Frobenius特征包；在每个特征包中进行有限域计算（核、商、乘积复形同调）；通过CRT幂等元将结果提升回原始空间；通过互逆迹对偶包配对X和Z逻辑算符，得到完整的可寻址共轭逻辑基。

Result: 得到了有限阿贝尔提升积码的完整可寻址共轭逻辑基；包秩揭示了逻辑扇区的分裂方式；提升代表元给出了所构造共轭基宽度的认证上界；整轨道擦除可分解为按包归因的擦除逻辑维度。

Conclusion: 该方法使阿贝尔提升积qLDPC码族具备类似HGP码的特性（可寻址逻辑基），同时保留了群代数提升的布局自由度，并在有界种子形状和群基支撑假设下具有实用性。

Abstract: Quantum LDPC memories can encode many logical qubits, but dimension alone does not make them usable: applications need explicit conjugate logical operators with structured labels and physical representatives. For hypergraph-product (HGP) codes this structure is transparent, since the input matrices are binary and can be row-reduced over $\mathbb{F}_2$. Abelian lifted-product codes are subtler. Their seed entries are shifts, or sparse sums of shifts, in a group-algebra ring rather than a field, so pivot blocks need not be invertible and global row reduction can fail.
  We address this with \emph{logical spectroscopy}, a spectral construction that replaces global row reduction by finite-field computations in the Frobenius character packets of the Abelian lift group. The Chinese remainder theorem (CRT) decomposes the group algebra into these packets. In each packet, we compute kernels, quotients, and product-complex homology; we then lift the resulting representatives back with CRT idempotents and pair $X$ and $Z$ logicals through reciprocal trace-dual packets. This gives complete addressable conjugate logical bases for finite Abelian lifted products $\mathsf{LP}(A,B)$.
  The same packet data also gives design diagnostics. Packet ranks show how logical sectors split, the lifted representatives give certified upper bounds on the width of the constructed conjugate basis, and whole-orbit erasures decompose into packet-attributed erased-logical dimensions. Thus, CRT packets also serve as working coordinates: they label logical sectors, certify the constructed basis width, and attribute structured erasure failures. Under bounded seed-shape and group-basis-support assumptions, this construction gives Abelian lifted-product qLDPC families an HGP-like feature while preserving the layout freedom of group-algebra lifts.

</details>

### [169] [Effective potentials for polar molecules under non-orthogonal dual microwave fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03179)
*Fulin Deng,Xinyuan Hu,Su Yi,Tao Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文针对非正交双微波场中极性分子屏蔽问题，发展了Floquet理论框架，推导出解析有效势并实现了精确散射计算。结果表明，尽管非正交会削弱屏蔽效果，但在实验相关条件下非弹性损失仍被强烈抑制，且非正交性提供了额外的相互作用各向异性和强度可调性。该框架可推广至任意多频驱动场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双微波屏蔽实验中，两个微波场通常无法完美正交，这种非对准会导致偶极-偶极相互作用无法完全抵消，并使单分子缀饰态本质上含时，传统定态散射理论不再适用。作者旨在发展能处理这一含时问题的理论框架。

Method: 发展了Floquet理论，推导出解析有效势，实现了非正交双微波场中极性分子的精确散射计算。

Result: 非正交性虽会中等程度削弱屏蔽效果，但在实验相关条件下非弹性损失仍被强烈抑制；同时，非正交性提供了额外的相互作用各向异性和强度可调性，该理论已成功应用于解释气滴转变和费米面形变等实验观测。

Conclusion: 该理论框架不仅适用于双微波屏蔽，还可直接推广至任意多频驱动条件，为在复杂微波构型下操控超冷极性分子提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Dual-microwave shielding has emerged as a powerful tool for stabilizing ultracold polar molecules while tuning their intermolecular interactions. However, the two microwave fields are generally not perfectly orthogonal in experiments. Such misalignment introduces an in-plane component of the linearly polarized microwave, whose frequency differs from that of the elliptically polarized field. This component prevents complete cancellation of the dipole-dipole interaction and, more critically, renders the single-molecule dressed state intrinsically time-dependent, so that the conventional time-independent scattering framework is no longer available. Here we develop a Floquet theory that yields an analytic effective potential and enables accurate scattering calculations for polar molecules in non-orthogonal dual microwave fields. We find that, though misalignment weakens the shielding moderately, inelastic losses remain strongly suppressed under experimentally relevant conditions. Meanwhile, misalignment provides additional tunability of the interaction anisotropy and strength, which has been directly applied to recent experimental observations on the gas-to-droplet transition~[Z. Shi \textit{et al}, arXiv:2508.20518 (2025)] and Fermi-surface deformation in microwave-shielded molecular gases~[S. Biswas \textit{et al}, arXiv:2602.22447]. The framework is not restricted to dual-microwave shielding and can be generalized straightforwardly to arbitrary multi-frequency driving, providing a versatile tool for manipulating ultracold polar molecules under complex microwave configurations.

</details>


### [170] [Experimental signatures of bosonic pairing in a two-component Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03902)
*Omar Almonajed,M. Iskin,M. Ö. Oktel*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文比较了描述二元玻色滴的Bogoliubov理论与玻色配对理论，发现后者在全实激发谱下给出更低的巨势，并预测了可实验检验的独特关联特征，如长波长相间动量空间关联增强和有限动量静态结构因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bogoliubov理论在描述玻色滴时存在虚激发谱分支，通常被忽略；玻色配对理论则提供全实激发谱。本文旨在建立玻色配对理论的算符形式，并系统比较两种理论在从弱相互作用气体到液滴区间的预测差异。

Method: 在固定粒子密度下自洽确定玻色配对理论的变分参数，使用算符形式重新表述玻色配对理论使其近似透明化，并在两种理论均具有实激发谱的区间进行直接比较。

Result: 玻色配对理论比Bogoliubov理论给出更低的巨势；预测了增强的长波长相间动量空间关联和有限动量的静态结构因子；这些差异在广泛相互作用强度范围内持续存在。

Conclusion: 关联函数和结构因子测量可作为区分两种理论的直接实验检验手段，为跨越气体-液滴区间的玻色配对提供实验验证。

Abstract: Two-component bosonic droplets are commonly described within Bogoliubov theory, where beyond-mean-field quantum fluctuations stabilize the system against mean-field collapse. In the interaction regime where droplets form, however, the Bogoliubov excitation spectrum contains an imaginary branch associated with the underlying instability, which is typically omitted when evaluating the beyond-mean-field energy. Bosonic pairing theory provides an alternative description with a fully real excitation spectrum. In this work, we reformulate bosonic pairing theory within an operator formalism, making its underlying approximations transparent, and compare its predictions with those of Bogoliubov theory for a homonuclear binary Bose mixture across the crossover from a weakly interacting gas to the droplet regime. Working at fixed particle density, we determine the variational parameters of bosonic pairing theory self-consistently and focus on the regime in which both theories possess real excitation spectra, allowing a direct comparison. We find that bosonic pairing theory yields a lower grand potential than Bogoliubov theory and predicts qualitatively distinct correlation signatures, including enhanced long-wavelength interspecies momentum-space correlations and a finite density static structure factor at experimentally accessible low momenta. These differences persist over a broad range of interaction strengths and suggest that correlation and structure-factor measurements can provide direct experimental tests of bosonic pairing across the gas-to-droplet crossover.

</details>


### [171] [Bloch-sphere rotations in driven double-well for ultracold atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04873)
*Michele Modugno,Marco Fattori,Giulio Pettini*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究在驱动双阱势中非相互作用玻色凝聚体的可控旋转，通过线性时间周期势实现布洛赫球赤道面任意轴的旋转与z轴快速旋转的组合，基于Floquet方法提供精确的两能级模型，并讨论其在Beat-Note超晶格实验平台上的直接应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在驱动双阱势中实现非相互作用玻色凝聚体在布洛赫球赤道面上任意轴的可控旋转，为量子信息处理提供新的控制方案。

Method: 采用空间线性时间周期势驱动双阱中的非相互作用冷凝物；通过数值模拟研究动力学；基于Floquet方法构建完整的时间依赖演化算子，提供精确的两能级模型。

Result: 实现了布洛赫球赤道面任意轴的受控旋转，并结合z轴快速旋转；所提方案可直接应用于基于Beat-Note超晶格的双阱势阵列实验平台。

Conclusion: 所提出的驱动方案能够实现对非相互作用玻色凝聚体的精确量子操控，且与现有实验平台兼容，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We show that, by using suitable protocols for a non-interacting condensate in a driven double-well, one can achieve controlled rotations about arbitrary axes in the equatorial plane of the Bloch sphere, composed with fast rotations about the $z$-axis. Specifically, we investigate the dynamics induced by a spatially linear time-periodic potential, by means of numerical simulations. We also provide an explicit two-level model that accurately captures the microscopic evolution of the driven system, with the full time-dependent evolution operator obtained using a Floquet-based approach. The analysis is carried out using as a reference a recently realized experimental platform consisting of arrays of double-well potentials based on Beat-Note Superlattices, to which the proposed control scheme is directly applicable.

</details>


### [172] [Excitation spectra and rank tomography of linear matrix product tangent spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05269)
*Otto T. P. Schmidt,Iacopo Carusotto*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）代数簇的切空间方法，用于研究开放边界条件下非均匀量子多体系统的激发谱。同时引入了MPS切空间的秩层析成像技术，通过粒子扇区秩轮廓来表征其表达能力。以Bose-Hubbard模型为基准，验证了该方法能够再现低能激发态，并捕捉到从Mott绝缘体到超流相变的有限尺寸前兆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的张量网络方法在处理非均匀量子多体系统（特别是开放边界条件）的激发谱时存在局限性，需要发展一种适用于更一般情形的切空间方法，并量化MPS切空间的表达能力。

Method: 1) 在MPS代数簇上构建切空间方法；2) 引入秩层析成像技术，通过粒子扇区秩轮廓分析切空间的表达能力；3) 以Bose-Hubbard模型为测试基准进行数值验证。

Result: 该方法能够准确再现Bose-Hubbard模型的低能激发态，并有效捕捉Mott绝缘体到超流相变的有限尺寸前兆信号。

Conclusion: 所提出的切空间方法适用于开放边界条件下的非均匀量子多体系统，秩层析成像技术为评估MPS切空间表达能力提供了有效工具，该方法在强关联量子多体系统的激发谱研究中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We formulate a tangent-space method for algebraic varieties of matrix product states (MPS) to study excitation spectra of non-uniform quantum many-body systems with open boundary conditions. We further introduce a rank tomography of the MPS tangent space, which characterizes its expressivity in terms of particle-sector rank profiles of the underlying MPS variety. Using the Bose--Hubbard model as a benchmark, we illustrate that the method reproduces low-lying excitations and captures finite-size precursors of the Mott-insulator to superfluid transition.

</details>


### [173] [Mass-imbalanced SU(N) Fermi gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05328)
*Jordi Pera,Joaquim Casulleras,Jordi Boronat*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过微扰理论推导了任意质量不平衡的SU(N) 费米气体在零温下超越平均场修正的解析表达式，揭示了质量不平衡打破顺磁对称性、偏好重组分占据、降低完全极化所需相互作用强度等关键效应，并识别出质量不平衡作为超冷费米混合物磁有序调控的强大工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将稀释费米气体理论从质量平衡情形推广到质量不平衡情形，为多组分混合物提供紧凑的状态方程，并探究质量不平衡对巡游铁磁性的影响。

Method: 采用气体参数 x = kF a0 的微扰理论，推导到二阶的相互作用能，获得任意自旋极化和任意质量比下的解析表达式。

Result: 得到了质量不平衡混合物超越平均场修正的闭合解析表达式；发现质量不平衡在无相互作用极限下就打破顺磁对称性，偏好重组分占据，并降低达到完全极化态所需的相互作用强度。对于 S=1/2，系统从质量诱导的部分极化态连续演化到完全铁磁态；对于大自旋，不同质量分布产生不同的极化路径（光滑或非连续）。

Conclusion: 质量不平衡是控制超冷费米混合物磁有序的有效手段。

Abstract: We report a fully analytical description of zero-temperature itinerant ferromagnetism in repulsive SU(N) Fermi gases with arbitrary mass imbalance among components. Using perturbation theory in the gas parameter x = kFa0, with kF the Fermi momentum and a0 the s-wave scattering length, we derive the second-order energy for arbitrary spin polarization and arbitrary mass ratio. Our main result is a closed analytic expression for the beyond-mean-field correction in mixtures with unequal masses. This analytical result extends the theory of dilute Fermi gases beyond the mass-balanced case and provides a compact equation of state for multicomponent mixtures. We show that mass imbalance breaks the paramagnetic symmetry already in the non-interacting limit, favors the occu pation of heavier components, and lowers the interaction strength required to reach a fully polarized state. For S = 1/2, the system evolves continuously from a mass-induced partially polarized state to full ferromagnetism. For larger spins, distinct mass distributions generate qualitatively differ ent polarization paths, including smooth and discontinuous sequences. Our results identify mass imbalance as a powerful control tool for magnetic ordering in ultracold Fermi mixtures.

</details>
