### [174] [Correlation-Induced Topological Reconstruction in a Periodically Driven Kagome Mott Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02717)
*Rahul Ghosh,Subhajyoti Pal,Ganesh C Paul*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kagome晶格Hubbard模型在周期性圆偏振光驱动下的电子关联效应。利用Brillouin-Wigner微扰理论推导出有效Floquet哈密顿量，发现驱动会重整化裸跳跃并产生手性近邻和次近邻项，导致重复拓扑相变和可连续调谐的平带。通过slave-rotor平均场理论，发现Mott转变在驱动振幅上呈现强非单调性，产生重复的金属-绝缘体转变，且Mott相内的自旋子激发获得与非相互作用Floquet能带不同的拓扑结构。这种关联驱动的拓扑重构产生了纯驱动或纯相互作用无法实现的平带自旋子绝缘体。结果表明，周期性驱动的Kagome系统可作为非平衡条件下工程化关联拓扑平带物理的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Kagome晶格中电子关联与周期性圆偏振光驱动之间的相互作用，特别是驱动如何影响Mott转变以及关联效应如何改变Floquet能带的拓扑性质，寻找纯驱动或纯相互作用无法实现的新的拓扑关联物态。

Method: 采用Brillouin-Wigner微扰理论推导有效Floquet哈密顿量，结合slave-rotor平均场理论处理电子关联效应，分析驱动振幅对Mott转变和拓扑性质的影响。

Result: 驱动重整化裸跳跃并产生手性近邻和次近邻项，诱导重复拓扑相变和连续可调平带；Mott转变在驱动振幅上呈强非单调性，产生重复金属-绝缘体转变；Mott相内自旋子激发获得不同于非相互作用Floquet能带的带拓扑，形成平带自旋子绝缘体。

Conclusion: 周期性驱动的Kagome系统是非平衡条件下工程化关联拓扑平带物理的有效平台，可在超冷原子晶格中实验实现。

Abstract: We study the interplay between electronic correlations and circularly polarized periodic driving in the Hubbard model on a Kagome lattice. Using Brillouin-Wigner perturbation theory, we derive an effective Floquet Hamiltonian in which the drive renormalizes the bare hopping and generates chiral nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor terms, producing repeated topological transitions and a flat band whose position is continuously tunable across the spectrum. Within slave-rotor mean-field theory, we show that the resulting Mott transition is strongly non-monotonic in the drive amplitude, yielding repeated metal-insulator transitions, and that the spinon excitations inside the Mott phase acquire a band topology distinct from that of the non-interacting Floquet bands. This correlation-driven topological reconstruction produces emergent flat-band spinon insulators inaccessible to either driving or interactions alone. Our results establish periodically driven Kagome systems as a platform for engineering correlated topological flat-band physics out of equilibrium, with proposed realizations in ultracold atomic lattices.

</details>


### [175] [Quantum magnetism of the spin-1 kagome-lattice antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03086)
*Katsuhiro Morita*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模Lanczos对角化和有限温度Lanczos方法，研究了自旋-1 Kagome格子海森堡反铁磁体，揭示了零温磁化过程中的四个平台（m=0, 1/3, 7/9, 8/9），其中m=0为三聚体价键晶体态，m=7/9和8/9为磁振子晶体态（m=8/9对应精确局域磁振子晶体态），并分析了有限温度下的比热双峰结构和磁化曲线热效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自旋-1 Kagome格子海森堡反铁磁体的基态和热力学性质，特别是零温磁化平台和有限温度行为，为候选材料的热力学和高场磁化测量提供基准数据。

Method: 采用大规模Lanczos对角化（零温）和有限温度Lanczos方法，结合高斯核平滑方法构建零温磁化曲线。

Result: 发现了四个磁化平台（m=0, 1/3, 7/9, 8/9）；m=0平台为三聚体价键晶体态，m=7/9和8/9为磁振子晶体态（m=8/9为精确局域磁振子晶体态）；比热在T/J≈0.1和T/J≈1.1处呈现双峰结构；m=1/3平台在低温下可见，高场平台迅速被热效应抹除。

Conclusion: 为自旋-1 Kagome格子材料的热力学和高场磁化测量提供了基准理论数据，揭示了不同磁化平台的微观起源及其对温度的敏感性。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-1 kagome-lattice Heisenberg antiferromagnet using large-scale Lanczos diagonalization and the finite-temperature Lanczos method. The zero-temperature magnetization process exhibits plateaus at $m=0$, $1/3$, $7/9$, and $8/9$, where $m$ is the normalized magnetization. The $m=0$ plateau is identified as a trimer valence-bond-crystal state, while the high-field plateaus at $m=7/9$ and $8/9$ are identified as magnon crystals. In particular, the $m=8/9$ plateau corresponds to the exact localized-magnon crystal state. A smoothed zero-temperature magnetization curve constructed using the Gaussian-kernel smoothing method indicates magnetization jumps at the lower-field edge of the $m=1/3$ plateau and at the upper-field edges of the $m=7/9$ and $8/9$ plateaus. At finite temperatures, the specific heat exhibits a double-peak structure with peaks around $T/J\simeq0.1$ and $T/J\simeq1.1$, and the low-temperature peak may be related to trimer valence-bond-crystal ordering. The finite-temperature magnetization curves show that the $m=1/3$ plateau remains visible at low temperatures, whereas the high-field plateaus are rapidly smeared out by thermal effects. These results provide benchmark data for thermodynamic and high-field magnetization measurements in candidate spin-1 kagome-lattice materials.

</details>


### [176] [Winding charge density wave: intertwining of structural chirality and phase topology of electronic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03102)
*Shun Asano,Youichi Yanase*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一类称为"缠绕电荷密度波"(winding CDWs)的新型手性电荷密度波，其通过单一有序波矢即可展现宏观手性。在手性晶体中，手性声子驱动的Peierls不稳定性选择特定的晶体角动量通道，赋予CDW整数方位角相位缠绕。在非手性晶体中，自发对称性破缺可稳定任意手性的缠绕CDW，实现非手性到手性的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索手性电荷密度波的新机制，揭示手性结构与电子序相位拓扑之间的基本联系。

Method: 基于手性声子驱动的Peierls不稳定性理论分析，结合晶体角动量通道选择规则和电子-声子耦合的选择定则，构建缠绕CDW的理论框架，并推广到具有离散旋转对称性的非手性晶体。

Result: 发现缠绕CDW在手性晶体中通过手性声子驱动Peierls不稳定性产生确定的角动量通道；在非手性晶体中可通过自发对称性破缺稳定任意手性的缠绕CDW，实现非手性到手性的相变。

Conclusion: 揭示了手性结构的几何与电子序相位拓扑之间的基本联系，为理解手性电荷密度波提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose a class of chiral charge density waves (CDWs), dubbed winding CDWs, that exhibit macroscopic chirality despite a single ordering wavevector. In screw-symmetric chiral crystals, chiral phonons drive a Peierls instability that selects a definite crystal angular momentum channel, thereby endowing the CDW with an integer azimuthal phase winding dictated by the selection rule governing electron-phonon coupling. We further extend this framework to achiral crystals with discrete rotational symmetry and demonstrate that spontaneous symmetry breaking stabilizes a winding CDW with either handedness, realizing an achiral-to-chiral phase transition. Our results reveal a fundamental link between the geometry of chiral structures and the phase topology of electronic orders.

</details>


### [177] [What Makes Three-Dimensional Quantum Spin Liquids Possible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03352)
*Yasir Iqbal,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文论证三维量子自旋液体并非脆弱例外，而是可以通过局部约束、相干隧穿和规范缺陷拓扑实现稳定存在的物质相，并讨论了约束驱动的库仑相、弱谐波序-by-无序选择以及自旋-轨道或多极相互作用等关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统低维直觉（弱配位和强零点运动导致量子自旋液体），指出另一条通往量子无序的路径——局部约束流形，并解释为何这种相在三维空间中可以稳定存在。

Method: 围绕约束驱动的库仑相、弱谐波序-by-无序选择以及自旋-轨道/多极相互作用产生微观动力学这三个组织框架进行论述，并分析紧致规范理论在三维空间中与低维不同的行为。

Result: 三维量子自旋液体可以在有限参数区域内稳定存在，紧致规范理论在三维中甚至可能反转通常的维度直觉——在低维中不稳定或需精细调节的相在三维中可稳定存在。

Conclusion: 三维量子自旋液体不需要被视为脆弱例外，而是可以由局部约束、相干隧穿和规范缺陷拓扑自然产生。最后讨论了实验诊断、候选材料和开放的理论问题。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids are often introduced through a low-dimensional intuition: weak coordination and strong zero-point motion frustrate conventional magnetic order. This view has shaped much of the field, but it misses another route to quantum disorder. In many frustrated magnets, the more natural starting point is not a fluctuating version of an ordered state, but a locally constrained manifold of low-energy configurations. Within such a manifold, quantum dynamics can generate an emergent gauge theory, with fractionalized excitations and collective modes absent in ordinary magnets. Here we ask why such phases can be stable in three spatial dimensions. We argue that three-dimensional quantum spin liquids need not be regarded as fragile exceptions to the tendency of 3D magnets to order. They can arise when local constraints suppress premature order selection, coherent tunneling processes connect the constrained manifold, and the topology of gauge defects permits a deconfined regime. For compact gauge theories, three dimensions can even reverse the usual dimensional intuition: regimes that are unstable or fine-tuned in lower dimensions may become stable over finite regions of parameter space. We organize the discussion around constraint-driven Coulomb phases, weak harmonic order-by-disorder selection, and the role of spin-orbit or multipolar interactions in generating the required microscopic dynamics. We close with experimental diagnostics, candidate materials, and open theoretical questions for three-dimensional quantum spin liquids

</details>


### [178] [Pressure-Driven Structural Transitions without a Displacive Charge-Density Wave in La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03363)
*J. Huang,Sitaram Ramakrishnan,P. Rodière,P. Toulemonde,Z. Rahmany,V. Balédent,B. Vignolle,Sourav Marik,P. Fertey,P. Foury-Leylekian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了双层镍氧化物La₂SmNi₂O₇在高压和低温下的结构性质，发现其在常压下单斜超结构不同于先前报道，低温下无电荷密度波迹象，并在高压下经历从单斜到正交(15 GPa)再到四方(21 GPa)的结构相变。在超导穹顶内获得了高质量X射线衍射数据，为理解超导机制提供了结构依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双层镍氧化物La₂SmNi₂O₇在压力和温度变化下的晶体结构演化，特别是其与超导电性出现之间的关联，为理解镍基超导体的超导机制提供结构基础。

Method: 采用X射线衍射技术，在变温变压条件下系统研究了La₂SmNi₂O₇的晶体结构，包括常压低温结构测定和高压结构相变追踪。

Result: 1) 常压下该材料呈现与La₃Ni₂O₇近似的单斜超结构，区别于此前报道的结构；2) 低温下未探测到电荷密度波序；3) 随压力增加，先后在15 GPa和21 GPa发生单斜→正交→四方的结构相变；4) 在超导穹顶区域内获得了可进行结构精修的高质量衍射数据。

Conclusion: 明确了La₂SmNi₂O₇的真实常压晶体结构，排除了低温CDW序的存在，揭示了压力诱导的结构相变序列，并为超导穹顶内的理论建模提供了可靠的实验结构数据。

Abstract: We investigated the structural properties of bilayer nickelate La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$ as a function of pressure and temperature. At ambient conditions, we show that the material crystallizes as a monoclinic superstructure distinct from the one previously reported and close to the pseudo-orthorhombic structure of pristine La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. No signatures of satellite reflections associated with charge density wave (CDW) ordering are detected at low temperature. Upon compression, a sequence of pressure-induced structural transitions from monoclinic to orthorhombic 15 GPa and then tetragonal 21 GPa symmetry is observed. Within the superconducting dome, the quality of the X-ray diffraction data enables structural refinements enabling theoretical models to understand the emergence of superconductivity.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum Destabilization of Skyrmions in Centrosymmetric Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03378)
*Amit Kumar,Kalpataru Pradhan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用神经网络量子态方法，研究了自旋量子数s对J1-J2-J3中心对称量子海森堡模型中斯格明子稳定性的影响。结果表明，从半经典极限过渡到极端量子极限（s=1/2）时，斯格明子稳定性Q严重退化直至消失。该失稳由量子纵向涨落驱动，Q随自旋矩倒数1/s呈幂律衰减，但s=1/2时显著偏离该标度行为。微观上，斯格明子稳定性与纠缠（第二Rényi熵）及局域自旋幅度存在线性对应关系：纠缠增加、局域自旋幅度被抑制时，Q线性消失。此时斯格明子数C作为自旋取向的几何遗迹保留，但Q因纵向自旋抑制而消失。研究指出，受挫晶格中经典鲁棒的斯格明子相仅限于高自旋材料，至少需要s=3/2才能实现稳定的原子尺度拓扑纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋量子数s对受挫量子磁体中斯格明子稳定性的影响，特别是从半经典到极端量子极限的转变过程中斯格明子的行为，明确量子涨落对拓扑自旋纹理的破坏机制。

Method: 采用神经网络量子态方法，对J1-J2-J3中心对称量子海森堡模型在正方晶格上的斯格明子稳定性进行数值研究，通过调控自旋量子数s分析稳定性Q的标度行为，并计算第二Rényi熵和局域自旋幅度建立定量对应关系。

Result: 1) 斯格明子稳定性Q在半经典到量子极限的过渡中严重退化，s=1/2时Q消失；2) Q随1/s呈幂律衰减，但s=1/2显著偏离此标度；3) 量子纵向涨落是失稳的主要驱动因素；4) 斯格明子稳定性与纠缠正相关、与局域自旋幅度负相关，呈线性对应关系；5) 稳定斯格明子要求自旋矩至少s=3/2。

Conclusion: 受挫晶格中经典上鲁棒的斯格明子相在量子极限下不可存活，其稳定性需要足够大的自旋矩（至少s=3/2）来抑制量子纵向涨落。该工作为原子尺度拓扑自旋纹理的实现提供了量子限制的物理画像和设计准则。

Abstract: We investigate the role of the spin quantum number $s$ on the stability of skyrmions in a $J_1-J_2-J_3$ centrosymmetric quantum Heisenberg model on a square lattice using the neural network quantum states method. Our results reveal that the skyrmion stability $Q$ is severely degraded when transitioning from the semiclassical regime to the extreme quantum limit ($s=1/2$), where it ultimately vanishes. We demonstrate that this destabilization is driven by quantum longitudinal fluctuations, with $Q$ exhibiting a power-law decay as a function of the reciprocal spin moment $1/s$. Notably, the extreme quantum limit ($s=1/2$) deviates drastically from this scaling behavior, exhibiting distinct physics compared to larger spin moments. Furthermore, we reveal the microscopic origin of this decay by establishing a quantitative correspondence between skyrmion stability, entanglement, and local spin magnitude: as the local second Rényi entropy (an indicator of entanglement) increases and the local spin magnitude is suppressed, the skyrmion stability vanishes linearly. This regime marks a quantum state where the skyrmion number $C$ remains as remanent geometric feature of the spin orientations, yet the skyrmion stability $Q$ vanishes due to the longitudinal suppression of the local spin magnitude. Our findings suggest that classically robust skyrmion phases in frustrated lattices are fundamentally restricted to high-spin materials, indicating that a spin moment must of at least $s = 3/2$ is required for the realization of stable, atomic-scale topological textures.

</details>


### [180] [Fixed-point tensor network for compactified boson conformal field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03534)
*Gong Cheng,Dong-Yu Bao,Zheng-Cheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用边界（开弦）数据和共形边界条件，为2D紧致玻色子理论（一个典型的非有理CFT）构建了固定点张量网络，精确再现了闭弦谱并实现了稳定的RG流，同时识别了单张量层次上的可控边缘形变，从而沿着c=1模空间连续流动，为描述2D非有理CFT提供了具体的格点级路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定点张量网络为共形场论提供了离散时空表示，有望理解全息对偶、广义对称性和量子引力。此前该方法多应用于有理CFT，需要将其扩展到非有理CFT（如紧致玻色子理论）以验证其普适性。

Method: 利用具有共形边界条件的边界（开弦）数据构建2D紧致玻色子理论在一般紧致半径下的固定点张量；采用张量复重整化算法测试RG流稳定性；在单张量层次上引入可控的边缘形变（Exactly Marginal Deformation）。

Result: 所构造的张量高精度地再现了闭弦谱；张量复重整化算法下生成了稳定的RG流；成功识别了单张量层次上的边缘形变，能够沿c=1模空间连续流动。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个具体的格点级框架，为描述一大类2D非有理CFT提供了新途径。

Abstract: Fixed-point (FP) tensor networks provide a discrete spacetime representation of conformal field theories (CFTs), offering a new route toward understanding holographic duality, generalized symmetries, and even quantum gravity. In this work, we construct FP tensors for the 2D compactified boson theory at a generic compactification radius, an archetypal irrational CFT, using boundary (open-string) data with conformal boundary conditions. We show that the resulting tensors reproduce the closed-string spectrum with high accuracy and generate stable renormalization-group (RG) flows under the tensor complex renormalization algorithm. Moreover, we identify a controllable exactly marginal deformation at the level of a single tensor, enabling flows that move continuously along the $c=1$ moduli space. This framework establishes a concrete lattice-level route toward describing a broad class of 2D irrational CFTs.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond-adiabatic flat Chern bands from a double-helix skyrmion crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03707)
*Bin Xi,Ken Chen,Qiang Luo,Jie Lu,Jia-Wei Mei,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双螺旋斯格明子晶体（DHSKX）结构，通过相位聚类机制在抑制动能的同时保留贝里曲率，实现了平坦的Chern能带和分数Chern绝缘体物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦能带工程的核心挑战是抑制动能而不牺牲贝里曲率，现有方法难以同时满足这两个条件，亟需新的设计方案。

Method: 在受挫蜂窝自旋模型中，通过双交换机制得到双螺旋斯格明子晶体（DHSKX），其π锁定螺旋性将波函数相位缠绕驱逐出斯格明子核心，磁C3对称性将相位固定为三个锁相簇，通过分布式相消干涉抑制净输运同时保留贝里曲率。

Result: 实现了|C|=1的孤立平坦Chern能带，在中等耦合区间超越绝热参考的量子几何；在超越绝热区间通过能带投影精确对角化得到ν=1/3 Laughlin型分数Chern绝缘体的有限尺寸证据；还得到C=-2的高Chern平坦能带。

Conclusion: DHSKX架构仅基于位点分辨的复数跃迁即可构建，可直接在拓扑电路、声学和光子学平台中编程实现。

Abstract: A central challenge in flat-band engineering is suppressing kinetic energy without sacrificing Berry curvature. We show that a double-helix skyrmion crystal (DHSKX)--two sublattice-resolved skyrmion textures locked at opposite helicities, obtained here as the classical ground state of a frustrated honeycomb spin model--provides such a route under double exchange. The key mechanism is a single real-space organization, phase clustering: the $π$-locked helicities expel the wave function's phase winding from the skyrmion cores, and the magnetic $C_3$ symmetry pins it into three phase-locked clusters whose distributed destructive interference cancels net transport while preserving the Berry curvature. Ordinary skyrmion crystals, even with the same symmetry, do not develop this organization. Phase clustering yields isolated flat $|C| = 1$ Chern bands over broad coupling windows, one of which surpasses the adiabatic reference in quantum geometry at intermediate coupling. In this beyond-adiabatic window, band-projected exact diagonalization gives finite-size evidence consistent with $ν= 1/3$ Laughlin-type fractional-Chern-insulator physics; the same texture also hosts a higher-Chern ($C = -2$) flat band. Built from site-resolved complex hoppings alone, the DHSKX architecture is directly programmable in topolectric, acoustic, and photonic platforms.

</details>


### [182] [Pauli Spectrum and Stabilizer Rényi Entropy in Gapless Symmetry-Protected Topological Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03762)
*Ying-Lin Li,Po-Yao Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称保护拓扑（SPT）相中的稳定子Rényi熵和Pauli谱。稳定子Rényi熵在相变点附近呈现极值但无法区分不同SPT相，而Pauli谱在相变点处显示出特征性交叉结构，反映了不同拓扑相之间非局域弦序参量的主导Pauli-弦关联的交换。对于有能隙和非本征无能隙SPT相，这种交叉可通过局域幺正对偶性理解；对于本征无能隙SPT相，则需非可逆对偶变换实现Pauli谱映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠已被广泛用于诊断拓扑物相，但非稳定子纠缠（量子魔法）作为不同于纠缠的量子多体态度量，其在对称保护拓扑相中（包括有能隙、非本征无能隙和本征无能隙SPT相）的行为尚未被充分研究，本文旨在探索其能否提供新的表征视角。

Method: 数值计算稳定子Rényi熵和Pauli谱，分析在对称性保持微扰下SPT相变过程中的行为；利用局域幺正对偶性和非可逆对偶变换解释Pauli谱的映射关系。

Result: 稳定子Rényi熵在相变点附近呈现极值但不能区分不同SPT相；Pauli谱在相变点处显示特征性交叉结构，反映不同拓扑相中非局域弦序参量的主导关联交换；有能隙和非本征无能隙SPT相可通过局域幺正对偶性理解，本征无能隙SPT相则需要非可逆对偶变换。

Conclusion: 稳定子Rényi熵仅提供相变的粗粒化诊断，而Pauli谱包含弦序扇区交换的更精细信息；量子魔法（非稳定子纠缠）为表征有能隙和无能隙SPT相提供了互补视角。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is widely used as a diagnostic of topological phases of matter. Beyond entanglement, non-stabilizerness captures a distinct aspect of quantum many-body states by quantifying their distance from the manifold of stabilizer states. In this work, we study the stabilizer Rényi entropy in symmetry protected topological (SPT) phases, including both gapped SPT, non-intrinsically gapless SPT, and intrinsically gapless SPT phases. Under symmetry preserving perturbations, we find numerically that the stabilizer Rényi entropy exhibits an extremum near the phase transition. However, the stabilizer Rényi entropy alone cannot distinguish different SPT phases. In contrast, the Pauli spectrum reveals a characteristic crossing structure at the transition point. This crossing reflects the exchange of dominant Pauli-string correlations associated with the non-local string order parameters of the two topological distinct phases. For gapped SPT and non-intrinsically gapless SPT phases, the crossing structure can be understood from a local-unitary duality that maps the Pauli spectrum between the two phases. For intrinsically gapless SPT phases, such a local-unitary mapping is absent. Instead, we find that the Pauli spectrum mapping is generated by a non-invertible duality transformation. These results show that although the stabilizer Rényi entropy provides only a coarse diagnostic of phase transitions, the Pauli spectrum contains finer information about the exchange of string order sectors. Our findings demonstrate that quantum magic offers a complementary perspective for characterizing both gapped and gapless SPT phases.

</details>


### [183] [Cuprate d-wave superconductivity based on Non perturbative many body theory for spin fluctuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04168)
*Jianwei Gong,Junnian Xiong,Ruitao Xiao,Hui Li,Ziyu Li,Huaqing Huang,Dingping Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用非微扰多体自旋GW方法和协方差方法分别计算Hubbard模型中的单粒子格林函数和两体关联函数，得到的奈尔温度和超导临界温度Tc与实验数据定量吻合，并展示了不同掺杂区费米面的演化，该方法无费米子符号问题，可模拟64×64晶格，为研究强关联系统特别是铜氧化物超导体提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为强关联系统（特别是铜氧化物超导体）提供一种非微扰、无符号问题、可模拟大尺寸晶格的新计算方法，以准确计算关键物理量并达到热力学极限。

Method: 采用非微扰多体自旋GW方法计算单粒子格林函数，结合协方差方法计算两体关联函数，在Hubbard模型框架下进行模拟，支持64×64晶格尺寸。

Result: 奈尔温度和超导临界温度Tc与实验数据定量一致；展示了不同掺杂区费米面的演变，清晰呈现从赝能隙到奇异金属再到费米液体的相变过程；方法在强耦合和低温下无费米子符号问题。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究强关联系统特别是铜氧化物超导体提供了一条新途径，能够准确再现关键物理量并实现热力学极限下的模拟。

Abstract: We employ the nonperturbative many-body spin GW method to compute the single-particle Green's function, and the covariance method to evaluate the correlation functions of two body, for the Hubbard model for superconductors. The key transition temperatures, namely the Neel temperature and the superconducting critical temperature Tc are found to be in quantitative agreement with experimental data. The evolution of the Fermi surface in different doping regimes is also presented, which clearly shows transitions from pseudogap to strange metal and to Fermi liquid phase. The method has been benchmarked at strong coupling and low temperature and is free from the fermion sign problem. Simulations on lattices up to 64 times 64 are also feasible, making access to the thermodynamic limit possible. This approach may offer a new route toward investigating strongly correlated systems, especially cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [184] [Orbital-Selective Mott and Antiferromagnetic Phases in Diagonally Compressed Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04621)
*Jiewei Ding,Ho-Kin Tang,Wing Chi Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过行列式量子蒙特卡罗模拟，研究了半填充Hubbard模型在斜角压缩kagome晶格上的几何压缩效应。通过改变晶格角度θ和相互作用强度U，发现了几何压缩诱导的子格分化：当θ≳52°时，A子格建立长程跳跃并抑制B/C子格的金属性，驱动选择性Mott转变；当θ≲52°时，B-C链发展长程反铁磁关联并抑制A子格的金属性。研究揭示了阻挫二维系统中晶格几何、磁阻挫与强关联之间的复杂相互作用，构建了包含顺磁金属、顺磁Mott、反铁磁金属和反铁磁Mott态的丰富U-θ相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究kagome晶格几何压缩对强关联电子系统的影响，特别是晶格形变如何调控子格间的电子关联、磁序和Mott转变，揭示几何阻挫、晶格各向异性与强关联电子之间的竞争与协同机制。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡罗(DQMC)方法模拟半填充Hubbard模型，引入指数衰减长程跳跃t(r)=t₀exp(-r/r₀)以反映键长演化，通过改变晶格角度θ和相互作用强度U，测量双占据、电荷压缩率和自旋-自旋关联函数。

Result: 发现θ≈52°为临界点：θ≳52°时A子格主导长程跳跃，B/C子格被抑制发生选择性Mott转变；θ≲52°时B-C链发展反铁磁长程序，抑制A子格金属性。A位点的临界相互作用U^c_A在B-C反铁磁关联出现时急剧下降，而U^c_{B/C}上升，形成轨道选择性Mott相和包含四种状态的丰富相图。

Conclusion: 晶格几何压缩通过子格依赖的电子跳跃和磁关联，在kagome晶格中诱导出选择性Mott转变和轨道选择性Mott相，揭示了几何阻挫二维系统中晶格几何、磁阻挫与强关联电子之间的非平庸耦合机制。

Abstract: We perform determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations of the half-filled Hubbard model on a diagonally compressed kagome lattice, introducing exponential decay long-range hopping $t(r) = t_0 \exp\bigl(-r / r_0\bigr)$ to account for the evolving bond length. By varying the lattice angle $θ$ and the on-site interaction $U$, double occupancy, charge compressibility, and spin-spin correlation functions of the whole system and each sub-lattice are measured. We find that geometric compression induces a clear sublattice differentiation: for $θ\gtrsim52^\circ$, the A sublattice establishes long-range hoppings, which in turn suppresses the metallic behavior of the $B/C$ sublattice and drives a selective Mott transition; for $θ\lesssim52^\circ$, the $B$-$C$ chains develop long-range antiferromagnetic correlations within the finite-size simulations, which in turn suppresses the metallic behavior of the $A$ sublattice and drives a selective Mott transition. The critical interaction $U^c_A$ for the $A$ sites decreases sharply near the onset of $B$-$C$ antiferromagnetic correlations, while $U^c_{B/C}$ increases. These competing orders give rise to an orbital-selective Mott phase and a rich $U$-$θ$ phase diagram featuring paramagnetic-metal, paramagnetic-Mott, antiferromagnetic-metal, and antiferromagnetic-Mott states. Our results highlight the complex interplay between lattice geometry, magnetic frustration, and strong correlations in frustrated two-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [185] [Supercritical fluid of quantum electrons in three-dimensional superconducting fullerides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04693)
*Xinying Li,Yuki Matsuda,Chongli Yang,Huaxue Zhou,Takuma Ogasawara,Qin Wang,Liguo Zhang,Hidekazu Shimotani,Hailiang Xia,Khuong K. Huynh,Panagiotis Kotetes,Satoshi Heguri,Katsumi Tanigaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过双探针（电导率和磁化率）实验首次在三维超导富勒烯CsxRb3-xC60中识别出热力学平衡的超临界流体相及其Mott端点，揭示了金属-绝缘体共存相，并给出了量子临界预言区域的临界指数，同时为超导Tc穹顶提供了新的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对Cr掺杂V2O3和有机Mott体系的研究报道了不一致的临界指数，且单观测量的标度分析无法解决相共存、非均匀性和渗流等问题。本文旨在通过双探针手段克服这些局限，识别热力学平衡的SCF相。

Method: 采用电导率和磁化率两种独立探针，对三维超导富勒烯CsxRb3-xC60进行实验测量；对磁化率进行无假设的双粒子分析，以最大两相混合熵标识金属-绝缘体共存SCF相；同时对电导率进行标度分析提取临界指数。

Result: 发现两条不同的金属-绝缘体转变线收敛于单一Mott端点；磁化率分析显示金属-绝缘体共存SCF相，符合热力学平衡的Widom线图像；电导率标度给出的临界指数落在量子临界预言区域内。

Conclusion: 双探针方法提供了Mott SCF的统一微观图像，其特征长度低于当前衍射分辨率，并为超导Tc穹顶起源提供了新解释。

Abstract: The supercritical fluid (SCF) of quantum electrons at the Mott metal-insulator transition without symmetry breaking is one of the most elusive phenomena in strongly correlated electron physics. Prior studies of Cr-doped V2O3 and organic Mott systems reported discrepant critical exponents. A key limitation is that the scaling analysis relies on a single experimental observable, leaving the roles of phase coexistence, inhomogeneity, and percolation unaddressed. Here we report the first experimental identification of a thermodynamically equilibrated SCF phase and its associated Mott endpoint in the three-dimensional superconducting fullerides CsxRb3-xC60, using two independent probes of electrical conductivity and magnetic susceptibility, which reveal two distinct metal-insulator transition lines converging at a single Mott endpoint. A hypothesis-free two-particle analysis of magnetic susceptibilities yields a metal-insulator coexisting SCF by exhibiting the maximum two-phase mixing entropy, in agreement with a picture of a thermodynamically equilibrated Widom line. Simultaneously, conductivity scaling yields a critical exponent in the regime of quantum critical predictions. Our new dual-probe approach provides a unified microscopic picture of the Mott SCF with a characteristic length scale below current diffraction resolution, in addition to a new interpretation on the origin of superconducting Tc-dome.

</details>


### [186] [Nonlocal Kondo-exchange-driven intrinsic anomalous Hall effect in localized-$4f$ antiferromagnetic metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04805)
*Akimitsu Kirikoshi,Junya Otsuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对f电子反铁磁金属中反常霍尔效应的起源问题，提出了一种理论框架，并应用于Ce₂CuGe₆，发现其反常霍尔电导和能带自旋劈裂源于非局域Kondo交换耦合诱导的自旋依赖的格点间跳跃，而非局域Zeeman型有效场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁金属中的反常霍尔效应虽已引起广泛关注，但已有研究主要集中于d电子体系（巡游电子同时负责电荷输运和磁有序），而f电子体系（局域磁矩与传导电子分属不同轨道）的相关机制尚不明确。

Method: 构建了一个描述局域反铁磁序对传导电子影响的理论框架，并将其应用于新近发现的4f反铁磁金属Ce₂CuGe₆中。

Result: 识别出Ce₂CuGe₆中本征反常霍尔电导和能带自旋劈裂的起源是非局域Kondo交换耦合诱导的自旋依赖的格点间跳跃，而非通常认为的作用于局域传导电子的Zeeman型有效场。

Conclusion: 在f电子反铁磁金属中，反常霍尔效应的微观机制与d电子体系不同，强调了非局域交换耦合的关键作用。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect in antiferromagnetic metals has attracted considerable attention. Most known realizations involve itinerant $d$ electrons that simultaneously mediate charge transport and magnetic order. Here, we focus on $f$-electron materials, where localized magnetic moments and conduction electrons are hosted in different orbitals. We develop a theoretical framework to describe the impact of localized antiferromagnetic order on itinerant electrons. Applying this approach to the recently discovered $4f$ antiferromagnetic metal $\mathrm{Ce}_{2}\mathrm{Cu}\mathrm{Ge}_{6}$, we identify the origin of both the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity and the spin splitting of the energy bands as spin-dependent intersite hopping induced by nonlocal Kondo exchange coupling, rather than a Zeeman-type effective field acting locally on the conduction electrons.

</details>


### [187] [Chirped Floquet linear drives activate forbidden charge-to-spin conversions in Rashba two-dimensional electron gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04946)
*Mohsen Yarmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在Rashba二维电子气中，通过啁啾（chirped）Floquet线性驱动，利用啁啾诱导的面内Floquet-Zeeman场和奇宇称动量漂移，同时打破旋转和时间反演对称性，从而激活传统上被禁戒的Edelstein电荷-自旋转换通道（包括纵向和面外方向），无需外加静磁场或界面工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Rashba二维电子气中，Edelstein效应仅能实现横向电荷-自旋转换，纵向或面外转换需要静磁场或界面工程，但这些方法会产生杂散场且缺乏可调谐性。动态圆/椭圆Floquet驱动虽可解锁禁戒通道，但线性Floquet驱动无法实现。本文旨在探索一种仅利用啁啾线性Floquet驱动即可激活禁戒Edelstein转换通道的新方案。

Method: 提出啁啾Floquet线性驱动方案，通过啁啾效应产生面内Floquet-Zeeman场以及奇宇称动量漂移，同时破坏系统的旋转对称性和时间反演对称性，从而在Rashba 2DEG中激活原本被禁戒的Edelstein电荷-自旋转换通道。

Result: 啁啾线性驱动能够有效解锁传统上被禁戒的纵向和面外Edelstein电荷-自旋转换通道，且该方案可通过可编程空间光调制器或光学延迟线在实验上实现，具有高度可调谐性。

Conclusion: 该啁啾线性驱动方案为自旋轨道转矩开关和高效率自旋电子学提供了一条普适且可调谐的新途径，无需静磁场或复杂界面工程，实验可行性高。

Abstract: In Rashba two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs), charge-to-spin conversion via the Edelstein effect is conventionally limited to the transverse plane. Accessing longitudinal or out-of-plane pathways typically requires static magnetic fields or interface engineering, which cause stray fields and lack tunability. While dynamic circular or elliptical Floquet drives can also unlock these forbidden pathways, a simple Floquet linear drive cannot. Here, we propose an alternative approach: a \textit{chirped} Floquet linear drive. The chirp induces in-plane Floquet-Zeeman fields and an odd-parity momentum drift, which simultaneously break rotational and time-reversal symmetries. This mechanism activates the forbidden Edelstein charge-to-spin conversions in Rashba 2DEGs. Experimentally accessible via programmable spatial light modulators or optical delay lines, this tunable chirped linear drive offers a broadly applicable route to spin-orbit torque switching and high-efficiency spintronics.

</details>


### [188] [Optical switching of antiferromagnetic domains by nonreciprocal heat current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04975)
*Takeshi Hayashida,Shunsuke Izumikawa,Kenta Kimura,Carl S. Davies,Andrei Kirilyuk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了反铁磁体LiFePO4中基于非互易光学效应的确定性磁畴切换，利用中红外光从不同方向照射可选择性稳定相反畴态，该效应在宽波长范围内有效，源于光生热电流的非互易性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用光的非互易性来确定性控制反铁磁畴，克服传统共振光学切换方案的谱宽和材料限制。

Method: 使用中红外光从相反方向照射磁电反铁磁体LiFePO4，利用材料中空间反演和时间反演对称性同时破缺产生的非互易光学响应，通过光生热电流实现确定性畴切换。

Result: 从相反方向照射可选择性稳定相反的反铁磁畴态；切换效应覆盖宽波长范围，而非局限于特定共振跃迁的窄谱区。

Conclusion: 非互易性可作为用光确定性控制对称性破缺相的一般原理，为光学控制反铁磁畴提供新范式。

Abstract: What distinguishes front from back? In physics, such directionality emerges only when an underlying symmetry is broken. Antiferromagnets that inherently break both space-inversion and time-reversal symmetries provide a striking example, exhibiting nonreciprocal optical responses that depend on the direction of light propagation. Beyond distinguishing antiferromagnetic domains, we show that this nonreciprocity can deterministically create them. Using mid-infrared light, we demonstrate deterministic switching of antiferromagnetic domains in the magnetoelectric antiferromagnet LiFePO4, where illumination from opposite sides selectively stabilizes opposite domain states. Remarkably, the switching persists over a broad wavelength range rather than being confined to a narrow transition-specific spectral region, overcoming the spectral and material constraints of resonance-based optical switching schemes. The broadband switching originates from the material's intrinsic nonreciprocity through optically generated heat currents. Our results establish nonreciprocity as a general principle for deterministically controlling symmetry-broken phases with light.

</details>


### [189] [Near itinerancy and slow singlet formation in the triangular lattice NaRuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05287)
*Charles C. Tam,Alon Hendler Avidor,Pritam Bhattacharyya,Yongseong Choi,Daniel Haskel,Sven Luther,Hlynur Gretarrson,Liviu Hozoi,Stephen D. Wilson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: NaRuO₂ 形成类水钠锰矿结构，包含边缘共享 RuO₆ 八面体的三角子晶格。在低至 100 mK 的温度下未显示磁有序迹象，并存在持续自旋涨落，暗示量子无序磁基态。通过 RIXS、XAS 和脉冲高场磁化测量，结合随机单态模型分析，提出一个近巡游体系，强磁和电荷涨落破坏了长程磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征 NaRuO₂ 中量子无序磁基态所源自的物理区间的特征，理解其局域电子结构和磁相互作用。

Method: 使用 Ru-L₂,₃ 边的共振非弹性 X 射线散射 (RIXS) 和 X 射线吸收光谱 (XAS)，以及脉冲高场磁化测量，结合随机单态模型分析。

Result: XAS 显示显著的自旋-轨道耦合，但 RIXS 未观察到自旋-轨道激子（自旋-轨道辅助 Mott 绝缘体的特征），而是观察到阻尼的轨道内激发（金属特征）。高场磁化数据符合随机单态模型。

Conclusion: NaRuO₂ 是一个近巡游体系，强磁和电荷涨落抑制了长程磁有序，导致量子无序磁基态。

Abstract: NaRuO$_2$ forms a delafossite-like structure that contains triangular sublattices of edge-sharing RuO$_6$ octahedra. It shows no evidence of magnetic order down to 100 mK and persistent spin fluctuations, suggestive of a quantum disordered magnetic ground state. In order to characterize the physical regime from which this disordered state arises, we use resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) and X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) at the Ru-$L_{2,3}$-edge, along with pulsed high-field magnetization to characterize both the local electronic structure and the magnetic interactions. Despite significant spin-orbit coupling inferred from XAS measurements, a spin-orbit exciton, characteristic of a spin-orbit assisted Mott insulator, was not observed with RIXS due to the presence of damped intraorbital excitations, which are characteristic of a metal. Corroborated by models of the high-field magnetization to a random singlet model, we propose a picture of a nearly itinerant system with strong magnetic and charge fluctuations that destabilize long-range magnetic order.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Non-equilibrium coupling to a diffusing density breaks Ising universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02661)
*Mattia Scandolo,Johannes Pausch,Michael E. Cates,Luca Di Carlo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现当序参量与非互易守恒扩散密度耦合时，Ising普适类在非平衡扰动下的鲁棒性被破坏。在d<4维下，系统流向快扩散不动点，产生新的非平衡普适类，其特征是关联与响应函数的标度指数分裂。双圈计算证实不动点稳定但修正标度指数很小（d=3时ω≈0.020），修正Harris判据表明该不动点在d=3主导临界行为，仅在d=2恢复Ising普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ising普适类对非平衡扰动通常具有鲁棒性，但序参量与非互易守恒密度耦合时这一鲁棒性是否失效尚不清楚，需要探索是否存在新的非平衡普适类。

Method: 采用重整化群分析，在d<4维下研究耦合非互易守恒扩散密度的临界动力学；进行双圈计算确定不动点稳定性及修正标度指数；提出修正Harris判据。

Result: 发现新的快扩散不动点，密度作为长程乘性噪声产生新普适类；关联与响应函数标度指数分裂，违反涨落-耗散定理；d=3时修正标度指数ω≈0.020，预示强有限大小修正；修正Harris判据确认d=3由该不动点主导，d=2恢复Ising普适类。

Conclusion: 非互易耦合可破坏Ising普适类的鲁棒性，产生新的非平衡普适类，其可观测标志是关联与响应函数标度指数的分裂。

Abstract: The Ising universality class is remarkably robust to non-equilibrium perturbations, which generically flow to zero under renormalization. We show that this robustness fails when an order parameter is coupled nonreciprocally to a conserved diffusive density. Below $d_c=4$, the renormalization group flows to a fast-diffusion fixed point at which the density acts as a long-range multiplicative noise, producing a novel universality class. The non-equilibrium nature of the fixed point is manifest in the large-scale violation of the fluctuation-dissipation relations, reflected in a splitting of the scaling exponents of the two-point correlation and response functions--a measurable hallmark of non-equilibrium critical fluctuations. A two-loop calculation establishes the stability of this fixed point but yields a small correction-to-scaling exponent $ω\approx0.020$ in $d=3$, implying strong finite-size corrections. An all-orders modified Harris criterion $ν>2/(d+z-2)$ confirms that the BIM fixed point governs criticality in $d=3$, with Ising universality recovered only at $d=2$.

</details>


### [191] [Non-equilibrium phase transition in the Brownian Ising Model: field theory, renormalization group, and exact results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02667)
*Mattia Scandolo,Luca Di Carlo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过完整的场论重正化群分析，研究了布朗伊辛模型（BIM），其中$\mathbb{Z}_2$序参量与被动守恒密度耦合，破坏了细致平衡。研究发现该耦合在四维以下是RG相关的，驱动系统到达一个不同于伊辛普适类的非平衡固定点，并计算了临界指数。该新普适类的特征是关联函数和响应函数具有不同的反常维度，这是平衡系统中不可能出现的涨落-耗散定理破坏的直接可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对布朗伊辛模型进行完整的场论重正化群分析，探究密度-序参量耦合如何驱动系统偏离平衡态，以及该非平衡固定点与伊辛普适类的关系。

Method: 使用Martin-Siggia-Rose形式论和$\varepsilon=4-d$展开，对布朗伊辛模型进行重正化群分析，计算最低阶临界指数（部分需两圈分析），并推导重正化因子间的精确关系。

Result: 1) 密度-序参量耦合在$d<4$时是RG相关的；2) 系统到达非平衡固定点，具有负的反常维度$\eta$；3) 关联与响应函数具有不同的反常维度($\eta\neq 2-\gamma/\nu$)，直接表明大尺度上FDT破坏；4) 存在小的修正-标度指数导致大的预渐近修正；5) 推导出精确标度关系$\nu=2/(d+z-2)$。

Conclusion: BIM固定点是$d=3$中任意非零扩散常数下唯一的红外吸引子，伊辛普适类和淬火稀释伊辛普适类在该维度下均不稳定。

Abstract: We present a complete field-theoretical renormalization-group (RG) analysis of the Brownian Ising Model (BIM), in which a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ order parameter is coupled to a passive conserved density, breaking detailed balance. Using the Martin-Siggia-Rose formalism and an $ε=4-d$ expansion, we show that this density-order parameter coupling is RG-relevant below four dimensions and drives the system to a new non-equilibrium fixed point, distinct from the Ising universality class. Critical exponents are computed at lowest nontrivial order, some of which require a dedicated two-loop analysis. At large scales, the density acts as an effective noise that is white in time but long-range in space, enhancing order-parameter fluctuations and producing a negative anomalous dimension $η$. A defining feature of the new class is that the correlation and response functions acquire different anomalous dimensions, $η\neq 2 - γ/ ν$ - a direct, observable signature of fluctuation-dissipation-theorem violation at large scales that cannot occur in equilibrium. We also find a small correction-to-scaling exponent, implying large preasymptotic corrections that must be accounted for in numerical and experimental tests. We further derive a set of relations among renormalization factors that hold to all orders in perturbation theory, following from the linearity of the density dynamics and an emergent shift symmetry. These yield an exact scaling relation $ν= 2/(d+z-2)$ at the BIM fixed point and establish that the Ising universality class, as well as that of quenched diluted-Ising, is unstable in $d=3$. This establishes the BIM fixed point as the unique infrared attractor for any nonzero diffusion constant.

</details>


### [192] [Measurement-induced spatially nonuniform fluctuations of the local particle number and their crossover in a quasiperiodic free-fermion chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03799)
*Toranosuke Matsubara,Kazuki Yamamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究准周期斐波那契格子上自由费米子链的连续监测动力学，发现连续测量会导致局域粒子数涨落从均匀分布转变为非均匀空间模式，并揭示了由测量强度驱动的从长程空间结构到局域环境主导的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究准周期长程序与连续测量之间的相互作用如何影响量子系统的非平衡动力学，特别是局域粒子数涨落的空间分布特性。

Method: 采用物理空间和垂直空间分析方法，研究斐波那契晶格上自由费米子链在连续监测下的动力学行为，分析局域粒子数涨落及相关关联函数。

Result: 发现连续测量导致涨落出现非均匀空间模式；弱测量下分布反映准周期系统的长程空间结构，增强测量后分布由每个位点的局域环境主导；该测量诱导的交叉现象也出现在关联函数等物理量中。

Conclusion: 揭示了连续监测动力学中涌现的非平衡准周期现象，为理解测量与准周期序的相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: We study continuously monitored dynamics of a quasiperiodic free-fermion chain defined on a Fibonacci lattice. We focus on fluctuations of the local particle number, which exhibit a spatially uniform distribution in the unitary limit. Remarkably, we demonstrate that they exhibit a nonuniform spatial pattern originating from the quasiperiodic long-range order under continuous measurement. Furthermore, employing both physicaland perpendicular-space analyses, we elucidate that measurement-induced crossover emerges in fluctuations due to the interplay between the incommensurate modulation and the continuous measurement. While weak measurement yields a distribution reflecting the long-range spatial structure of the quasiperiodic system, an increase in measurement strength alters the distribution into one dominated by the local environment of each site. We also elucidate that the measurement-induced crossover emerges in other physical quantities such as connected correlation functions. These findings offer insights into nonequilibrium quasiperiodic phenomena emerging in continuously monitored dynamics.

</details>


### [193] [Finite-Time Thermodynamics of Battery Discharging: Power-Efficiency Trade-Off and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03157)
*Rui-Han Liu,Yun-Qian Lin,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文揭示了电池放电过程中功率与效率之间的抛物线关系 $P\propto\eta(1-\eta)$，最大功率时效率精确为1/2。基于此，提出了多阶段恒流放电（MSCD）调度策略，通过KKT条件得到最优电流策略 $I_{i}^{\star}=\max(I_{i}^{-},I_{0})$，为电池管理系统提供了热力学基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池放电受限于输出功率与能量转换效率之间的固有权衡（由内耗引起），现有研究缺乏对这种权衡普遍形式的严格刻画，以及如何将其转化为实际调度规则的解析方案。

Method: 理论推导证明功率-效率抛物线关系；构建多阶段恒流放电（MSCD）调度问题，约束条件包括实时负载需求和全局放电截止时间；利用Karush--Kuhn--Tucker（KKT）条件解析求解最优策略。

Result: 得到最优电流策略 $I_{i}^{\star}=\max(I_{i}^{-},I_{0})$：受外部需求限制的阶段以最小所需电流运行，其余阶段统一提升至由截止时间决定的基线电流 $I_{0}$；通过耗散-时间帕累托前沿量化内阻对运行边界的影响。

Conclusion: 建立了电池管理系统调度层的严格热力学基线，可自然扩展至包含温度和荷电状态依赖的非线性模型。

Abstract: Battery discharging is governed by a fundamental trade-off between output power and energy conversion efficiency due to internal dissipation. In this paper, we demonstrate that such a trade-off universally yields a parabolic envelope $P\proptoη(1-η)$. The efficiency at maximum power is exactly one half, mirroring the well-known half-Carnot limit in finite-time thermodynamics. To extend this bound into practical operational rules, we formulate a multistage constant-current discharging (MSCD) schedule subject to simultaneous real-time load demands and a global discharging deadline. Analytical resolution via the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions reveals a remarkably compact optimal policy: $I_{i}^{\star}=\max(I_{i}^{-},I_{0})$. Under this rule, stages limited by external demand run exactly at their minimum required currents, while all remaining stages are elevated to a uniform baseline $I_{0}$ fixed by the deadline constraint. By tracing the dissipation--time Pareto front, we quantify how internal resistance shifts the operational boundaries and sharpens the trade-off corner. This analysis establishes a rigorous thermodynamic baseline for the scheduling layer of battery management systems, offering natural extensions to nonlinear models incorporating temperature and state-of-charge dependencies.

</details>


### [194] [Variance of the $SIS$ Epidemic on Networks: A Diffusion Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03300)
*Lucija Nora Farkaš,Sebastian Morel Balbi,Hrvoje Štefančić,István Zoltán Kiss,Vinko Zlatić*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 针对配置模型图上的SIS传染病过程，本文结合Gleeson近似主方程与van Kampen系统规模展开，推导出封闭的漂移-扩散矩阵，并通过Langevin/Lyapunov方程获得时变协方差，实现了对均场轨迹周围涨落的精确近似，在泊松、正则和幂律网络上与Gillespie模拟高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIS过程中康复节点以部分已知的邻域信息重新进入易感池，破坏了SI过程中干净的邻域分布，导致不存在严格的泛函中心极限定理，因此需要开发一种可处理的方差近似方法。

Method: 将Gleeson的近似主方程框架与van Kampen系统规模扩展相结合，针对约化的易感态/SI边/SS边计数向量推导出封闭的漂移矩阵和扩散矩阵，并通过关联的Langevin/Lyapunov方程求解时变协方差。

Result: 在泊松、正则和幂律网络上的Gillespie模拟验证显示，该方法与模拟结果高度一致，但在流行病阈值附近和强异质性网络中存在一定偏差。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为SIS过程在配置模型图上的涨落分析提供了有效的近似工具，弥补了严格FCLT缺失带来的理论空白。

Abstract: Functional laws of large numbers (FLLNs) describe the mean-field trajectory of epidemics on networks, but say nothing about the fluctuations around it. These fluctuations are governed by moments of the degree distribution not relevant at the level of the mean. A rigorous functional central limit theorem (FCLT) exists for the susceptible--infected ($SI$) process on configuration-model graphs, but no analogue exists for $SIS$, where recovery reintroduces vertices into the susceptible pool with partially known neighborhoods, breaking the clean neighborhood distribution the $SI$ derivation relies on. We develop a tractable variance approximation for Markovian $SIS$ on configuration-model graphs, combining Gleeson's approximate master equation (AME) framework with a van Kampen system-size expansion in the spirit of the $SI$ FCLT. We derive a closed drift and diffusion matrix for a reduced susceptible/$SI$-edge/$SS$-edge count vector and obtain the time-dependent covariance via the associated Langevin/Lyapunov equation. Validation against Gillespie simulation across Poisson, regular, and power-law networks shows close agreement, with deviations near the epidemic threshold and in strongly heterogeneous networks.

</details>


### [195] [Geometric Characteristics of Subproblems in Ising-Machine-Assisted Large Neighborhood Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05014)
*Masashi Yamashita,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过比较两种基于大邻域搜索（LNS）的子问题构造方法（LNS-K基于路径结构，LNS-Q基于QUBO变量和约束关系），发现LNS-K在控制子问题规模时能获得更短的总距离，且其位置方差随迭代递减。结果表明，面向Ising机器的序列优化子问题设计应综合考虑语义和几何结构，而非仅关注规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模QUBO公式常超出Ising机器输入限制或导致解质量下降，LNS虽能缓解此问题，但区分子问题的结构性因素（除二进制变量数外）尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 以车辆路径问题为研究对象，对比两种LNS子问题构造方式：LNS-K（基于当前解的车辆路径）和LNS-Q（基于QUBO变量与约束关系），在控制子问题二进制变量数相同的条件下进行匹配比较。

Result: LNS-K在匹配规模比较中总距离更短；LNS-K的所选客户位置方差随迭代递减。

Conclusion: Ising机器的序列优化子问题设计不应只考虑子问题规模，还应考虑从当前解继承的语义和几何结构。

Abstract: Large-scale quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) formulations of constrained combinatorial optimization problems often exceed the input-size limit of present Ising machines or suffer from degraded solution quality as the number of binary variables increases. Large neighborhood search (LNS) mitigates this difficulty by sequentially optimizing restricted subproblems, but the structural factors that distinguish subproblems beyond the number of binary variables remain insufficiently characterized. In this study, we examine vehicle routing problems and compare a construction based on the vehicle routes of the current solution, denoted by LNS-K, with a construction based on QUBO variables and constraint relations, denoted by LNS-Q, while controlling the number of binary variables in the subproblems. Under the tested conditions, LNS-K obtained shorter total distances than LNS-Q in the matched-size comparisons, and the position variance, a measure of the spatial spread of the selected customers, decreased during the iterations in LNS-K. These observations suggest that subproblem design for sequential optimization with Ising machines should consider not only subproblem size but also semantic and geometric structures inherited from the current solution.

</details>


### [196] [Free energies and optimal reaction coordinates via entropy production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03722)
*Jérémy Diharce,Line Mouaffac,Axel Dian,Fabio Pietrucci*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和数值实验证明，利用短分子动力学轨迹直接计算熵产生，可以估计活化过程的自由能垒并识别最优反应坐标。该方法基于随机能量学分析，在基准系统上验证后，成功应用于碳纳米颗粒-水复杂高维系统，并与committor概率结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法计算自由能垒和识别反应坐标通常需要大量采样或先验知识。本文旨在探索是否可以通过短轨迹上的熵产生计算，以较低的计算成本获得自由能垒估计并识别最优反应坐标，从而简化活化过程的分析。

Method: 基于随机能量学，对从相同初始构型出发、向平衡态弛豫的一组轨迹进行熵产生分析，并将轨迹投影到不同的候选坐标上。通过比较不同投影下的熵产生行为来估计自由能垒并排序反应坐标质量。

Result: 在简单基准系统上验证了方法的有效性，并成功估计了碳纳米颗粒在水中这一复杂高维系统的自由能垒；识别反应坐标质量排序与committor概率分析结果一致。

Conclusion: 短轨迹熵产生计算为估计自由能垒和识别最优反应坐标提供了一种高效方法，揭示了第二定律、自由能景观、反应坐标与动力学速率之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We show through theory and numerical experiments that a straightforward calculation of entropy production on short molecular dynamics trajectories allows estimating free-energy barriers and identifying optimal reaction coordinates of activated processes. To this aim, we perform an analysis based on stochastic energetics on a set of trajectories relaxing towards equilibrium from a same initial configuration, projected on different putative coordinates. After demonstrating the approach on simple benchmarks, we show that it is possible to estimate the free-energy barrier of a complex high-dimensional system (carbon nanoparticles in water) and to rank the quality of order parameters, in agreement with the committor probability. The results shed light on the entanglement between the second law, free-energy landscapes, reaction coordinates and kinetic rates.

</details>


### [197] [Quantum tunneling Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03845)
*Hisao Hayakawa,Satoshi Takada*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续一维对称双势阱中量子隧穿Mpemba效应，证明偶宇称谱系数a₂(Tᵢ)的非单调行为是量子统计力学的普适拓扑性质，受Sturm-Liouville振荡定理支配且与系统尺寸L无关，同时阐明了实际观测异常穿越现象需要严格的时间尺度分离条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放量子系统中Mpemba效应的普适机制，超越传统抽象态空间淬火方法，在连续实空间中桥接经典边界耗散与开放量子动力学。

Method: 采用非厄米有效哈密顿量的谱分解方法，分析边界开放双势阱系统的弛豫动力学；利用Sturm-Liouville振荡定理证明谱系数的拓扑性质；通过时间尺度分离分析总存活概率和迹距离的异常穿越行为。

Result: 证明了偶宇称谱系数a₂(Tᵢ)存在与系统尺寸L无关的普适热峰，该峰源于局域未扰动态与非厄米衰变通道的几何和节点对齐；揭示了观测Mpemba效应需要过势垒逃逸率远大于深阱隧穿双重态衰变率（Γ₂≫Γ₀, Γ₂≫Γ₁）的严格条件。

Conclusion: 该连续形式成功桥接了经典边界驱动耗散与开放量子动力学，为通过定制边界损失工程非平衡态提供了新见解。

Abstract: The quantum tunneling Mpemba effect is investigated within a continuous one-dimensional symmetric double-well potential open to external environmental sinks at the boundaries ($x=\pm L$). Using a non-Hermitian spectral decomposition of the effective Hamiltonian, we characterize the open-system relaxation dynamics without relying on abstract state-space quenches. We mathematically prove that the non-monotonic behavior of the first non-trivial even-parity spectral coefficient, $a_{2}(T_{i})$, with respect to the initial preparation temperature $T_{i}$ is a universal topological property born from quantum statistical mechanics. Crucially, we demonstrate that this intermediate thermal peak is governed by the Sturm-Liouville oscillation theorem and remains completely invariant with respect to the global system size $L$, contrasting sharply with the boundary-driven classical Mpemba effect. This universal peak arises from the geometric and nodal alignment between highly localized unperturbed states and extended non-Hermitian decay channels. Furthermore, we clarify that while this mechanism is robust, the actual observation of anomalous crossings in the total survival probability trace $S(t,T_{i})$ and the trace distance $\mathcal{D}(t,T_i)$ demand a strict separation of timescales, requiring the over-barrier escape rate to vastly exceed the decay rate of the deep-well tunneling doublet ($Γ_{2}\gg Γ_{0}$ and $Γ_2\gg Γ_1$). Our continuous formulation successfully bridges real-space classical boundary-driven dissipation with open quantum dynamics, providing novel insights for engineering non-equilibrium states via tailored boundary loss.

</details>


### [198] [The Reweighting Principle in Statistical Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03867)
*Salvatore Romano*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过最小相对熵原理统一了概率测度重加权框架下的指数倾斜（软约束）与条件化（硬约束），揭示了正则系综与微正则系综的热力学对偶结构，并在热力学极限下通过大偏差理论证明了系综等价性源于熵-偏倚竞争，最后将框架延拓至路径空间统一描述平衡热力学与条件随机动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为指数倾斜、条件化及统计力学中的系综变换提供一个统一的信息论视角，揭示不同概率测度更新规则之间内在的热力学结构关联。

Method: 以最小相对熵（KL散度）为统一原理，分别推导软约束与硬约束下的最优更新；通过引入扩增概率空间将可观测量显式变量化，建立微正则与正则系综的条件-边缘分布关系；利用大偏差理论分析热力学极限下的系综等价性。

Result: 指数倾斜对应正则系综的勒让德结构，条件化对应微正则系综的玻尔兹曼熵；在热力学极限下，条件化由指数倾斜通过测度集中涌现，系综等价性由熵与偏倚的竞争决定；该框架自然延拓至路径空间。

Conclusion: 概率重加权框架为平衡热力学与条件随机动力学提供了统一的信息论描述，揭示了不同系综之间的深层结构等价关系。

Abstract: Reweighting of probability measures provides a unifying perspective on conditioning, exponential tilting, and, more generally, ensemble transformations in statistical mechanics. We show that exponential tilting and conditioning arise as the minimum-relative-entropy updates associated with soft and hard constraints, respectively. Their relative entropies naturally inherit complementary thermodynamic structures: exponential tilting gives rise to the Legendre structure of the canonical ensemble and reduces to the Gibbs entropy for a uniform reference measure, while conditioning reduces to the Boltzmann entropy through the surprisal of the constrained macrostate. By introducing an enlarged probability space in which observables are treated as explicit variables, we further show that microcanonical and canonical ensembles arise as conditional and marginal distributions of a common structural prior after exponential reweighting. In the thermodynamic limit, described through large deviation theory, conditioning emerges from exponential tilting by concentration of measure, revealing ensemble equivalence as a consequence of entropy--bias competition. Finally, we outline how the same information-theoretic framework naturally extends to path space, suggesting a unified probabilistic description of equilibrium thermodynamics and conditioned stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [199] [Universal fluctuations of first discoveries in competitive exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04252)
*Arthur Plaud,P. L. Krapivsky,S. Redner,Olivier Bénichou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了竞争探索中首位发现者的份额波动，发现其相变由谱维数 d_s 控制：d_s<2 时竞争结果持续随机，2≤d_s<3 时出现异常慢的非高斯集中，d_s≥3 时呈高斯集中（d_s=3 时有对数修正）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞争探索场景中（如觅食者竞争食物、智能体捕获目标），首个到达者独占资源。传统探索度量只关注哪些位置被发现，但竞争探索需要确定每个发现位置由哪个搜索者最先到达。核心问题是：两个相同竞争者的平均发现份额均为1/2，但这一均分是出现在每条长探索历史中还是仅出现在平均意义上——即早期的竞争优势是否会被抹除。

Method: 引入发现份额 X_n（标记搜索者在头 n 个集体发现中所占比例），通过谱维数 d_s 表征单搜索者返回起始点的概率衰减 p_0(t)~t^{-d_s/2}，分析不同扩散类型（普通扩散、长程超扩散、拥挤或记忆导致的次扩散）下的竞争波动行为，推导 d_s≥2 时的精确渐近方差及发现尺度。

Result: 发现份额的波动行为由谱维数 d_s 控制，存在三个相区：d_s<2 时持续随机（递归探索），2≤d_s<3 时异常慢的非高斯集中，d_s≥3 时高斯集中（d_s=3 时有对数修正）。推导了 d_s≥2 的精确渐近方差和竞争不平衡抹除的发现尺度。首次发现标签的两点相关识别了这些区域的记忆机制。

Conclusion: 这一相结构在几何变化、竞争者异质性、竞争者数量和记忆变化下保持稳定，揭示了首次到达不等式的通用涨落理论。

Abstract: Random exploration is usually quantified by how fast new space is found, from
  the range of a single walker to the territory collectively covered by many
  walkers. In competitive exploration, first arrival secures an exclusive resource, as when foragers compete for food items or agents capture distributed targets. It is then no longer enough to know which sites have been discovered: one must determine, for each discovered site, which searcher reached it first. We introduce the discovery
  share $X_n$, the fraction of the first $n$ collective discoveries secured by
  a tagged searcher. For two identical competitors, exchange symmetry fixes
  $\langle X_n\rangle=1/2$, but the central question is whether this equal
  split emerges in each long exploration history or only on average, \emph{i.e.} whether early
  competitive advantages are erased or persist. Here we show that the answer is
  controlled by the spectral dimension $d_s$, defined by the large-time decay of the probability that a single searcher is at
  its starting point after $t$ steps, $p_0(t)\sim t^{-d_s/2}$.
  Across ordinary diffusion, long-range superdiffusion and subdiffusion induced
  by crowding or memory, $d_s$ separates persistent randomness in
  recurrent exploration $(d_s<2)$, anomalously slow non-Gaussian concentration
  for $2\le d_s<3$, and Gaussian concentration, logarithmically corrected at
  $d_s=3$, for $d_s\ge3$. For $d_s\ge2$, we derive exact asymptotic
  variances, including prefactors, and the discovery scale on which competitive
  imbalances are erased. Two-point correlations of first-discovery labels identify the memory mechanism behind these regimes.
  The same phase structure persists under changes in geometry, competitor
  heterogeneity, number of competitors and memory, revealing a general fluctuation
  theory of first-arrival inequalities.

</details>


### [200] [Critical behavior of the driven Curie-Weiss model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05130)
*Ruohan Xu,Faezeh Khodabandehlou,Christian Maes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文完整绘制了周期性外场驱动下宏观居里-外斯磁体的相图，发现了顺磁与铁磁相在低温强驱动下的共存区域，并详细分析了非平衡比热在临界点的发散行为，给出了新的临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间周期外场驱动的非平衡系统中，传统平衡态统计力学的相变理论不再适用。现有研究对驱动磁体的完整相图及热力学响应函数的临界行为缺乏系统理解，特别是比热在非平衡相变中的发散性质尚不清楚。

Method: 采用宏观居里-外斯模型，在时间周期外场驱动下，通过Floquet分析研究系统的动力学临界性；从顺磁相和铁磁相两侧分别计算比热随温度的变化，提取临界指数。

Result: 发现低温大驱动振幅和频率下存在顺磁与铁磁相的稳定共存区；非平衡比热在临界温度处发散，临界指数在$β\downarrow β_c$时为$α=1$，在$β\uparrow β_c$时为$α≈0.86$；居里温度随驱动增强而降低。

Conclusion: 驱动场的存在使得系统表现出与平衡态本质不同的动力学临界行为，比热呈现非对称临界指数，揭示了非平衡相变的新普适类特征。

Abstract: We complete the phase diagram of the macroscopic Curie-Weiss magnet in a time-periodic external field, as a function of temperature and driving parameters. There is a regime (large enough driving amplitude and frequency, at low temperatures) where stable paramagnetic and ferromagnetic phases coexist. In particular, we present a new detailed analysis of the (nonequilibrium) specific heat, diverging at the same critical inverse temperature $β_c$ as the magnetic susceptibility. The new Curie temperature decreases with the driving, and we find critical exponent $α=1$ for $β\downarrow β_c$, and $α\simeq 0.86$ for $β\uparrow β_c$, even for small driving. A Floquet analysis shows the nature of the criticality, which is dynamical, with implications that remain unseen and are mostly impossible when the system is in thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [201] [On data-driven parameterizations of multidimensional generalized Langevin dynamics in the presence of a quadratic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05151)
*Maximilian Braun,Martin Hanke,Niklas Wolf*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种数值算法，通过扩展变量列表构建马尔可夫模型，以参数化存在记忆效应时多维粗粒化物理系统在外势中的运动方程。该方法利用平稳速度的自相关数据，但避免先求解记忆核这一逆问题，而是直接构建自相关函数的Prony级数近似，其参数直接对应马尔可夫模型。数值结果表明，一维和二维系统分别使用5个和12个辅助变量即可获得良好匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当记忆效应显著时，需要一种有效方法来构建粗粒化物理系统的马尔可夫模型，同时避免直接求解记忆核这一逆问题带来的计算困难。

Method: 利用平稳速度的自相关数据构建Prony级数近似，从Prony级数参数直接导出对应的马尔可夫模型，从而绕过传统方法中先求解记忆核的逆问题步骤。

Result: 对一维系统使用5个辅助变量、对二维系统使用12个辅助变量的参数化模型，与分子动力学数据实现了良好匹配。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效构建存在记忆效应的多维粗粒化系统的马尔可夫模型，且通过适当数量的辅助变量可获得高精度结果。

Abstract: We propose a numerical algorithm to construct a Markov model with an extended list of variables to parameterize the equation of motion of a multidimensional coarse-grained physical system in an external potential, when memory effects are relevant. Our method uses autocorrelation data of the stationary velocities, but it avoids the inverse problem of finding the corresponding memory kernel from these data in a first step. Rather, the data are used to construct a Prony series approximation of the autocorrelation function, and the parameters of this Prony series provide the corresponding Markov model. Numerical results for molecular dynamics data show a good match for parameterized models with five auxiliary variables for a one-dimensional, and twelve auxiliary variables for a two-dimensional system.

</details>


### [202] [Ising-Machine-Assisted Large Neighborhood Search with Flexibly Tunable Subproblem Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05169)
*Koshiro Fujimoto,Masashi Yamashita,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出LNS-VT方法，通过控制每辆车连续优化步数来精细调整子问题规模，提升Ising机器辅助的大邻域搜索性能，在VRP和QMKP上验证了子问题规模控制的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Ising机器辅助的大邻域搜索方法在求解VRP时，子问题规模无法精细调整，导致解质量受限。作者希望提出一种能灵活控制子问题规模的方法来提升求解性能。

Method: 提出LNS-VT方法，引入"每辆车连续重新优化的步数"作为参数，在保持可行性的同时精细控制子问题规模。该方法适用于VRP，并推广到二次多背包问题(QMKP)进行验证。

Result: 在300节点、5车辆的VRP实例上，最佳设置相比现有方法在100次迭代后目标值降低约10%；且最佳子问题规模随当前解质量变化。在QMKP上也验证了存在合适的子问题规模。

Conclusion: 子问题规模控制是Ising机器辅助的大邻域搜索中提升解质量的关键因素，LNS-VT通过精细控制该参数实现了更优的求解性能。

Abstract: Ising machines are heuristic solvers for combinatorial optimization, but their solution quality can degrade when large-scale constrained problems are solved directly. Ising-machine-assisted large neighborhood search (LNS) instead repeatedly updates a feasible current solution by solving smaller subproblems. An existing feasibility-preserving method for the vehicle routing problem (VRP) re-optimizes the entire routes of selected vehicles and thus cannot adjust the subproblem size finely. We propose LNS-VT, which introduces the number of consecutive steps re-optimized per vehicle as a parameter to control the subproblem size finely while preserving feasibility. For a 300-site, 5-vehicle VRP, its best setting reduced the objective value by approximately 10\% relative to the existing method after 100 iterations, and the appropriate setting changed with the current solution quality. Applying the same principle to the quadratic multiple knapsack problem, we confirmed that an appropriate subproblem size also exists, indicating that subproblem-size control is important in Ising-machine-assisted LNS.

</details>


### [203] [Semi-Markovian switching in a fluctuating harmonic trap: An age-structured formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05173)
*Derek Frydel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究谐波陷阱中刚度以任意等待时间统计切换的布朗粒子（半马尔可夫过程），通过年龄结构状态空间恢复马尔可夫性，导出精确稳态解，并分析随机重置极限下的方差空间切换描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理半马尔可夫动力学中刚度切换引入的时间记忆效应，建立可解析求解的理论框架。

Method: 在扩大的年龄结构化状态空间中重新表述问题以恢复马尔可夫性，得到局域Fokker-Planck描述；通过将稳态空间分布表示为方差波动的Gaussian态叠加，将问题转化为方差空间中的切换过程。

Result: 导出了稳态空间分布和出生分布的精确积分方程，获得了稳态矩、注入功率和势能的精确表达式；在随机重置极限下得到了方差分布的精确积分方程。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了半马尔可夫切换系统的稳态分析，方差空间方法简化了随机重置极限的解析处理。

Abstract: We study a Brownian particle in a harmonic trap whose stiffness switches between two values with arbitrary waiting-time statistics, generating semi-Markovian dynamics. To treat the resulting temporal memory, we formulate the problem in an enlarged age-structured state space, restoring Markovianity and yielding a local Fokker--Planck description. Within this framework, we derive exact steady-state integral equations for the spatial and birth distributions and obtain exact expressions for stationary moments, injected power, and potential energy. In the second part of the paper, we analyze the stochastic-resetting limit, corresponding to a particle alternately released and trapped. By representing the stationary spatial distribution as a superposition of Gaussian states with fluctuating variance, the problem can be reformulated as a switching process in variance space. This yields exact integral equations for the variance distributions and leads to a simplified description amenable to direct analytical treatment.

</details>


### [204] [Nucleation and time-reversal symmetry breaking in nonconserved scalar field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05194)
*Noah Ziethen,Michalis Chatzittofi,Michael E. Cates,Cesare Nardini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了非平衡成核理论(NNT)，将经典成核理论(CNT)推广到具有非守恒序参量的非平衡场论中，通过两种等价推导（随机投影和Freidlin-Wentzell作用量最小化）描述液滴半径动力学，并证明了在大的临界半径极限下准势垒与时间反演弛豫路径的预测不同。通过毛细波动力学分析和两个显式示例（活性模型A和种群动力学模型）验证了理论的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典成核理论仅适用于平衡态系统，无法描述广泛存在的非平衡系统中的成核现象（如活性物质、反应扩散系统和种群动力学）。本文旨在建立一套系统的非平衡成核理论框架，克服CNT在非平衡条件下的局限性，特别是修正使用时间反演弛豫路径作为瞬子假设所导致的准势垒预测误差。

Method: 1) 随机投影方法：将随机场方程直接投影到径向反应坐标上；2) Freidlin-Wentzell作用量最小化方法。两种方法均利用大临界半径极限下的微扰结构进行解析推导。同时推导了毛细波动力学以保证近球形液滴假设的有效性。通过活性模型A和种群动力学模型（两种噪声选择）进行数值验证。

Result: NNT预测的准势垒不同于假设瞬子为弛豫路径时间反演的情况；界面密度分布与弛豫路径不同；通过定义合适的反应坐标仍可实现解析推导；毛细波稳定性分析保证了近球形液滴假设的成立；在活性模型A和种群动力学模型中，解析NNT预测与数值作用量最小化结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: NNT为活性物质、反应扩散系统和种群动力学等广泛非平衡系统中的成核理论提供了系统性的构建框架，成功将CNT推广到非平衡领域，并通过具体示例验证了理论的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Classical nucleation theory (CNT) describes the formation of a stable phase from a metastable one in terms of a single reaction coordinate that corresponds to the radius of a nucleating droplet. In this work, we provide a full account of nonequilibrium nucleation theory (NNT), which generalizes CNT to non-equilibrium field theories with non-conserved order parameter. We present two equivalent derivations of the dynamics of the droplet radius: a stochastic route, based on a direct projection of the stochastic field equation onto the radial reaction coordinate, and a route based on the minimization of the Freidlin-Wentzell action. Crucially, the quasipotential barrier predicted by NNT differs from the one found when assuming the instanton to be the time-reversal of the relaxation dynamics. Whereas the interfacial density profile differs from that on the relaxation path, an analytical derivation of NNT remains possible using a careful definition of the reaction coordinate. This leverages the perturbative structure that (in common with CNT) emerges in the limit of large critical radius. We further derive with similar techniques the dynamics of capillary waves, whose stability is required for the CNT/NNT precept of a near-spherical droplet to prevail. After deriving our theory for generic non-conserved field-theories, we address two explicit examples: a non-equilibrium generalization of Model A (Active Model A), and a population dynamics model (with two choices of noise that each break time-reversal symmetry). In both cases, we validate our analytical NNT against numerical results obtained by action minimization, with excellent agreement. NNT provide a systematic framework for constructing nucleation theories in a broad class of non-equilibrium systems from active matter, reaction-diffusion systems and population dynamics.

</details>


### [205] [A universal emulator for planar Ising lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05308)
*Riccardo Ben Alì Zinati,Alessandro Codello*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出“伊辛模拟器”概念，通过将二维伊辛模型映射到单一宿主晶格的稀释超晶胞，利用固定Feynman-Vdovichenko/Kac-Ward解，并结合二元掩码来统一计算铁磁/反铁磁耦合下的热力学量。该方法适用于所有11种阿基米德晶格、20种2-均匀晶格（首次获得热力学量）以及五边形、分形和无序伊辛模型，无需修改底层机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二维伊辛模型求解方法对不同晶格结构需要分别处理，缺乏统一框架。作者希望建立一个通用的“模拟器”方法，使得不同晶格（包括规则、均匀、五边形、分形和无序晶格）的伊辛模型热力学量可以通过同一套机制高效计算。

Method: 提出“伊辛模拟器”概念：将任意二维晶格表示为单一宿主晶格的位点稀释或键稀释超晶胞，利用预计算的Feynman-Vdovichenko/Kac-Ward解，通过晶格特定的二元掩码（masks）来区分不同晶格结构。具体构建了方形和三角形两种模拟器，使用同一转移矩阵搭配不同掩码即可计算所有热力学量。

Result: 成功应用于所有11种阿基米德晶格、20种2-均匀晶格（首次获得其热力学量）、多种五边形晶格，并直接扩展到分形和无序伊辛模型，无需修改底层算法。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一、高效且普适的二维伊辛模型求解方法，能够处理从规则到无序的广泛晶格类型，展现了极强的扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of an Ising emulator for two-dimensional Ising models: flat, unit-edge-length lattices can be represented as site- or bond-diluted supercells of a single host lattice, for which the Feynman--Vdovichenko/Kac--Ward solution is fixed once and for all. We construct explicit square and triangular emulators and show that a single transition matrix, supplemented by lattice-specific binary masks, gives all the thermodynamic quantities of interest for both ferro- and antiferromagnetic couplings. We apply the framework to all eleven Archimedean lattices, to all twenty $2$-uniform lattices -- whose thermodynamics is obtained here for the first time -- and to several pentagonal lattices, and show that the same construction extends directly to fractal and disordered Ising models, with no modification to the underlying machinery.

</details>
